Our approach utilizes a multi-stage pipeline: first, extracting visually observable clinical keywords from pathology reports; second, aligning visual features with these keywords via a Vision-Language meta-teacher (MedSigLIP) to provide dense semantic guidance; and finally, performing adaptive KD where teacher contributions are weighted based on their semantic alignment with the clinical narrative. Experiments on WSI captioning, visual question answering, and slide-level classification tasks demonstrate that an 87M parameter LaGuadia student model matches or exceeds foundation-scale models such as GigaPath and UNI, achieving strong factual consistency and robust generalization. These results highlight clinical language as an effective semantic anchor for building efficient and reliable digital pathology systems. Code is available at https://github.com/hvcl/LaGuadia.

</details>


### [144] [The Devil Is in the Leakage: A Disentangled Dual-Purification Framework for High-Fidelity Hairstyle Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11281)
*Jijie Li,Jiankuo Zhao,Xiangyu Zhu,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双净化框架（DPF），通过对抗性发型净化（AHP）和对比几何净化（CGP）两种正则化器，分别解决发型特征中的身份泄露问题和秃头图像中的瑕疵泄露问题，实现高质量的换发迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有发型迁移方法存在双重泄露问题：发型特征携带参考图像的身份或姿态信息（身份泄露），以及秃头图像中的残留伪影被传播到最终合成结果（瑕疵泄露），导致编辑精度和保真度不足。

Method: 提出DPF框架，包含两个训练时正则化器：1) 对抗性发型净化（AHP）基于互信息启发的对抗目标抑制发型特征中的身份可预测性；2) 对比几何净化（CGP）通过对比目标正则化ControlNet通路，减少模型对秃头条件中几何伪影的依赖。

Result: 在多种基准上实现了高保真、保持身份的换发迁移，达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过联合净化发型表示和几何通路，DPF有效解决了发型迁移中的双重泄露问题，验证了分别处理特征层面和条件层面污染的可行性。

Abstract: Hairstyle transfer aims to synthesize a photorealistic portrait by transplanting the hairstyle from a reference image onto a source subject while preserving the source identity. Recent foundation models show strong generative capability, but they struggle with the zero-shot disentanglement required for precise local editing, often entangling the reference hairstyle with its original identity and pose. Existing diffusion-based pipelines typically decompose the task by first generating a "bald" image from the source and then injecting hairstyle features from the reference. However, we show that this paradigm suffers from a fundamental leakage problem. Identity Leakage in Hairstyle occurs when hairstyle features retain reference identity or pose information, while Flaw Leakage in Bald arises when residual artifacts in the bald image are propagated into the final synthesis. To address both issues, we propose the Dual-Purification Framework (DPF), which introduces two complementary training-time regularizers. Adversarial Hairstyle Purification (AHP) purifies hairstyle features by suppressing identity predictability under a mutual-information-inspired adversarial objective. Contrastive Geometric Purification (CGP) regularizes the ControlNet pathway with a contrastive objective, reducing the model's reliance on geometric artifacts in the bald condition. By jointly purifying the hairstyle representation and geometric pathway, DPF achieves high-fidelity, identity-preserving hairstyle transfer and state-of-the-art performance on diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [145] [SalientGS: Unified SfM-to-3DGS with Importance-Guided MCMC Gaussian Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11285)
*Tianyu Xiong,Rui Li,Suning Ge,Jiaqi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SalientGS提出了一种统一的SfM-to-3DGS流水线，通过重要性引导的MCMC高斯分配，在15分钟内实现端到端重建，并达到最先进的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从无序图像重建3D场景的瓶颈在于昂贵的SfM预处理和冻结的位姿接口，现有方法效率低下。

Method: 提出重要性引导的MCMC高斯分配，聚合多视图残差生成每个高斯的欠拟合和冗余信号，定义平滑的重要性加权采样分布，引导高斯的新增和重定位到欠拟合区域，同时保持SGLD不变。

Result: 15分钟内实现端到端重建，达到最先进的感知质量（LPIPS指标）。

Conclusion: SalientGS通过统一SfM与3DGS流水线并引入重要性引导的高斯分配策略，显著提升了重建效率与感知质量。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D scenes from unordered images remains bottlenecked by expensive Structure-from-Motion (SfM) preprocessing and frozen pose interfaces. We present SalientGS, a unified SfM-to-3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) pipeline. Its central contribution is importance-guided Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) Gaussian allocation, which aggregates multi-view residuals into per-Gaussian underfit and redundancy signals. These signals define a smooth importance-weighted sampling distribution that biases both birth and relocation toward underfit regions. This reallocates capacity from well-fit areas without altering the underlying stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics (SGLD). SalientGS achieves end-to-end reconstruction in 15 minutes with state-of-the-art perceptual quality. The supplementary material provides dedicated sections for Per-Scene Qualitative Comparisons and Per-Image Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity (LPIPS) Analysis, including failure cases. Code and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/Six-Bit-TX/SalientGS.

</details>


### [146] [A Unified Framework for Comprehensive Cardiac CT Segmentation and Phenotyping: Human-in-the-Loop Data Annotation, Vision Foundation Model Development, Multicenter Evaluation and Clinical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11287)
*Pooya Mohammadi Kazaj,Leo Fridolin Weber,Wen Xie,Seyed Amir Ahmad Safavi-Naini,Anselm Stark,Giovanni Baj,Ali Mokhtari,Toshiya Yoshida,Christoph Ryffel,Taishi Okuno,Yoshihiro Akashi,Ronny R. Buechel,Thomas Pilgrim,Waldo Valenzuela,George C. M. Siontis,Xiaowei Xu,Moritz Hundertmark,Stephan Windecker,Christoph Grani,Isaac Shiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的心脏CT分割与表型分析框架，包含人机协同标注流程、CT数据增强技术和基于60,000例无标注CT扫描预训练的自监督基础模型。构建了迄今最大最全面的专家标注心脏CT分割数据集（1598例，14个心脏结构），在五个外部数据集上优于现有开源工具。自监督预训练显著提升了低数据场景下的标注效率，而架构选择对精度影响较小。该框架可扩展至人群级表型分析，并开放了数据集、代码、模型权重等资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏CT影像的全面定量分析受限于测量可扩展性而非数据可用性，常规临床实践中难以广泛应用。

Method: 采用人机协同标注管道、心脏CT专用数据增强技术，以及基于60,000例无标签心脏CT扫描预训练的自监督基础模型。构建了包含1598例、14个心脏结构的专家标注数据集（1000训练/598测试），在五种外部数据集上评估，并比较了卷积、Transformer和状态空间等不同架构。

Result: 框架在五个外部数据集上对所有心脏结构的分割准确性和全面性均优于现有开源工具；自监督预训练在低数据外部评估中提升最大；不同架构性能相当，表明数据质量和预训练比架构更关键；分割解剖结构携带的室功能和疾病严重程度信息超越了人口学变量。

Conclusion: 通过公开最大规模的心脏CT分割数据集、代码、模型权重、CT增强库和软件，为常规CT扫描的机会性心脏表型分析提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Comprehensive quantification of cardiac structures from computed tomography (CT) remains limited not by data availability but by the scalability of measurements, which makes routine use impractical. Here we present a unified framework for comprehensive cardiac CT segmentation and phenotyping that combines a human-in-the-loop annotation pipeline, a cardiac CT augmentation technique, and a self-supervised foundation model pre-trained on 60,000 unlabeled cardiac CT scans. Using this approach, we assembled the largest and most comprehensive expert-annotated cardiac CT segmentation dataset to date, comprising 1598 cases and 14 distinct cardiac structures (1000 for training, 598 for the external test set). Across five external datasets, the framework segmented all structures more accurately and comprehensively than existing open-source tools. Self-supervised pre-training improved labeling efficiency, with the most significant gains observed during external evaluation in the low-data regime. Benchmarking across convolutional, transformer, and state-space architectures showed comparable performance, indicating that data quality and pre-training, rather than architecture, drove accuracy. The framework was scaled to population-level phenotyping, with segmented anatomy that carries functionally relevant information about ventricular function and disease severity beyond demographic variables. By openly releasing the largest dataset with human labels, code, model weights, a CT augmentation library, and software, this work provides a reproducible foundation for opportunistic cardiac phenotyping from routinely acquired CT scans.

</details>


### [147] [Metadata Supervised MRI Representations for Modelling and Controlling Acquisition Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11295)
*Mehmet Yigit Avci,Pedro Borges,Virginia Fernandez,Natalia Glazman,Paul Wright,Mehmet Yigitsoy,Sebastien Ourselin,Jorge Cardoso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过联合建模MRI图像和DICOM元数据，学习解耦的解剖表征和对比度表征，实现跨扫描仪、跨协议的表征分离与统一协调，并验证了该方法在序列理解、数据一致性检测和跨模态/跨站点适应中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI采集存在显著变异性，相同解剖结构在不同扫描仪和成像协议下表现迥异，导致模型学到的表征混杂了生物结构与采集依赖特征，限制了可解释性、泛化能力和临床部署。

Method: 利用大规模临床脑MRI数据，联合建模图像与DICOM元数据，学习分离解剖结构与对比度依赖外观的解耦表征；并基于此构建统一解剖保留协调模型，支持基于图像或元数据条件的跨模态/跨站点适应。

Result: 对比度表征能有效组织异质采集、支持序列理解并检测图像-元数据不一致性；解剖表征抑制采集特异性变异同时保留生物学相关信息；协调模型实现跨模态与跨站点解剖保留适应。

Conclusion: 采集变异性是成像过程中可建模、可审计、可控制的结构化成分，为大规模医学影像中的采集感知表征学习提供了基础。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance imaging exhibits substantial acquisition variability, where identical anatomy can appear markedly different across scanners and imaging protocols. Consequently, learned representations entangle biological structure with acquisition-dependent appearance, limiting interpretability, generalisation, and clinical deployment. We show that these sources of variation can be separated by jointly modelling MRI images and DICOM metadata. Using large-scale clinical brain MRI data, we learn representations that separate anatomical structure from contrast-dependent appearance. Resulting contrast representations organise heterogeneous acquisitions, support sequence understanding, and detect image--metadata inconsistencies, whereas anatomical representations suppress acquisition-specific variation while preserving biologically relevant information. Building on these disentangled representations, we introduce a unified anatomy-preserving harmonisation model for cross-modality and cross-site adaptation, conditioned on image or acquisition metadata. Our findings suggest that acquisition variability is a structured component of the imaging process that can be modelled, audited, and controlled, providing a foundation for acquisition-aware representation learning in large-scale medical imaging.

</details>


### [148] [ASUMOT: Motion-Consistency-Based Asynchronous UAV Detection and Tracking with Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11303)
*Baofeng Jia,Xiaoyu Chen,Jingyuan Zhang,Zongze Wu,Haochen li,Jing Han,Lianfa Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ASUMOT，一种基于运动一致性的异步无人机检测与跟踪框架，直接在原始事件流上处理，通过运动一致性估计、轻量级验证器和聚类模块解决事件相机中无人机目标稀疏、碎片化和噪声干扰导致的跟踪不稳定问题，并发布了ES-UAV高分辨率事件级无人机基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机虽具有微秒级时间分辨率和高动态范围优势，但远距离无人机产生的事件响应稀疏、碎片化且含噪声，同一语义目标可能呈现为多个空间分离的斑点，直接进行斑点级异步跟踪会导致重复轨迹和身份不稳定。

Method: ASUMOT将每个无人机建模为一组运动一致性事件斑点，包含：局部运动一致性估计器触发可靠候选点、轻量级多任务验证器提供无人机置信度和运动方向线索、运动一致性聚类将碎片化斑点聚合成身份一致的无人机轨迹。

Result: 在公开无人机跟踪数据和ES-UAV基准上，ASUMOT在保持异步事件处理的同时，改善了精度与效率的权衡。

Conclusion: ASUMOT有效解决了事件相机下远距离无人机跟踪中的碎片化和身份不稳定问题，代码和数据集将开源。

Abstract: Event cameras offer microsecond-level temporal resolution and high dynamic range for low-altitude UAV perception. However, long-range UAVs often produce sparse, fragmented, and noise-contaminated event responses, where one semantic target may appear as multiple spatially separated blobs. Direct blob-level asynchronous tracking therefore suffers from duplicate trajectories and unstable identities. We propose ASUMOT, a motion-consistency-based asynchronous UAV detection and tracking framework operating directly on raw events. ASUMOT models each UAV as a set of motion-consistent event blobs. A local motion-consistency estimator triggers reliable candidates, a lightweight multi-task verifier provides UAV confidence and motion-direction cues, and motion-consistency clustering aggregates fragmented blobs into identity-consistent UAV tracks. We also introduce ES-UAV, a high-definition event-level UAV benchmark with dense semantic annotations. Experiments on public UAV tracking data and ES-UAV show that ASUMOT improves the accuracy--efficiency trade-off while preserving asynchronous event processing. Code and Dataset will be released.

</details>


### [149] [SLVMBench: Skill Learning from Video Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11312)
*Yudong Yang,Guangzhi Sun,Yixuan Li,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SLVMBench是首个同时评估视频大语言模型从长视频记忆中学习技能并实时应用的基准。模型需从2-3小时视频流（含嵌入的教程视频）中提取程序性知识，并回答实时问题。评测显示现有视频LLM在此任务上表现显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解基准仅关注被动理解，技能学习基准仅依赖短视频示范，缺乏一个能够评估模型从长视频中学习技能并实时迁移的综合性基准。

Method: 构建SLVMBench基准：包含2-3小时视频流（教程视频嵌入无关视频中），人工标注亚秒级时间校准，设计排除常识猜测的问题，整理教程覆盖所需技能。评估多个开源和商业视频LLM。

Result: 视频LLM在从视频中学习和应用技能知识方面表现显著不足；当技能知识位于长视频记忆中时，性能明显下降。

Conclusion: 现有视频LLM在实时技能获取与应用方面存在关键局限，SLVMBench可作为研究该方向的首个基准。

Abstract: We introduce Skill Learning from Video Memory (SLVMBench), the first benchmark that jointly evaluates whether video large language models (video-LLMs) can learn skills from long video memory and apply them to real-time tasks. SLVMBench presents models with 2-3 hour video streams that contain a tutorial video embedded in a stream of arbitrary irrelevant videos, resembling real-world human learning practices. Video-LLMs are asked to apply the acquired skill to answer real-time questions about an ongoing video. Unlike long-video understanding benchmarks that emphasize passive comprehension and skill-learning benchmarks that rely on short, immediate demonstrations, SLVMBench tests the full pipeline of memorizing and extracting procedural knowledge, as well as transferring it to real-time tasks. Moreover, rigorous human annotations feature sub-second-level temporal calibration, manually engineered questions eliminating common-sense guessing, and collated tutorials to ensure coverage of the required skills. Evaluations on state-of-the-art proprietary and open-source video LLMs show that video-LLMs struggle substantially with learning and applying skill knowledge from videos. Moreover, performance degrades markedly when the skill knowledge is placed within a long video memory. These results reveal a key limitation of existing video LLMs and position SLVMBench as the first benchmark for studying real-time skill acquisition and application from long-context video memory.

</details>


### [150] [HierCAD: Hierarchical Text-to-CAD Design via Structure Alignment and Parameter Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11339)
*Jimin Xu,Tianbao Wang,Tao Jin,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HierCAD提出了一种分层文本到CAD框架，通过将CAD生成分解为对象级和部件级推理轨迹，并结合结构对齐与参数接地学习策略，在CAD序列生成和模型重建上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到CAD方法利用大语言模型时，难以保持复杂设计的结构一致性，且几何参数接地不准确。

Method: 提出HierCAD框架，将CAD构造树分解为对象级程序推理和部件级拓扑推理轨迹；引入统一的结构对齐与参数接地（SAPG）学习策略，包括拓扑轨迹与参数跨度对齐、结构保持的参数扰动及基于排序的监督来缓解捷径学习。

Result: 在CAD序列生成和重建CAD模型评估上均优于先前最先进方法。

Conclusion: HierCAD通过分层推理和结构化学习策略有效提升了文本到CAD的结构一致性与参数精度。

Abstract: Recent text-to-CAD approaches have shown promising results by leveraging large language models, but they often struggle with maintaining structural consistency in complex designs and accurately grounding geometric parameters. To address these issues, we propose HierCAD, a hierarchical text-to-CAD framework that improves both structural reasoning and parameter prediction. HierCAD reformulates CAD generation as progressive reasoning by decomposing CAD construction trees into object-level procedural reasoning and part-level topology reasoning trajectories. To further improve generation fidelity, we introduce a unified Structure Alignment and Parameter Grounding (SAPG) learning strategy. Structure alignment aligns topology reasoning trajectories with their corresponding parametric CAD spans, while parameter grounding mitigates shortcut learning through structure-preserving parameter perturbations and ranking-based supervision. Experiments demonstrate that HierCAD outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods on both CAD sequence generation and reconstructed CAD model evaluation. Our code is available at https://github.com/Collab-Gen/HierCAD.

</details>


### [151] [Longitudinal Multi-View Breast Cancer Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11343)
*Solveig Thrun,Zijun Sun,Suaiba A. Salahuddin,Kristoffer Wickstrøm,Elisabeth Wetzer,Stine Hansen,Robert Jenssen,Michael Kampffmeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LMV-Net，一种纵向量多视角乳腺癌风险预测模型，通过联合分析CC和MLO两个解剖互补的视角，在显式时间对齐框架下建模，在EMBED和CSAW-CC数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么使用单一乳腺摄影视角进行显式时间对齐，要么建模多视角但缺乏纵向量化对齐，无法充分利用临床实践中关键的互补时空信息。

Method: 提出LMV-Net，在显式时间对齐的纵向量化框架中联合分析解剖上互补的CC（头尾位）和MLO（内外斜位）两个视角。

Result: 在EMBED和CSAW-CC公共数据集上，LMV-Net在整体风险预测性能以及不同乳腺密度和癌症亚组中均一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 纵向量多视角建模有潜力增强风险分层，为个性化筛查、高危患者早期识别和更高效的筛查资源分配奠定基础。

Abstract: Accurate breast cancer risk prediction from screening mammography is critical for enabling personalized screening intervals and early detection. Recent deep learning methods have shown the value of longitudinal data and explicit temporal alignment. However, existing approaches either perform explicit alignment using a single mammographic view or model multiple views without explicit longitudinal alignment, limiting their ability to exploit the complementary spatial-temporal information used in clinical practice. To address this gap, we propose LMV-Net, a longitudinal multi-view breast cancer risk prediction model that jointly analyzes anatomically complementary CC and MLO views within an explicitly aligned longitudinal framework. We evaluate our approach on the public EMBED and CSAW-CC datasets, comparing it to state-of-the-art breast cancer risk prediction methods. Our model consistently outperforms existing approaches in overall risk prediction performance and across different breast density and cancer subgroups. Importantly, these improvements highlight the potential of longitudinal multi-view modeling to enhance risk stratification, paving the way for future work on personalized screening, earlier identification of high-risk patients, and more efficient screening resource allocation. The code is available at https://github.com/sot176/LMV-Net.

</details>


### [152] [Benchmarking Edge Inference Strategies for Deep Learning Models in Industrial Machine Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11356)
*Miguel Gomez Fernandez,David Castro Boga,Roi Mendez-Rial,Eric Lopez-Lopez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种工业机器视觉推理框架（PyTorch、ONNX Runtime、OpenVINO、TensorRT）在多种CPU/GPU平台上的推理性能，发现OpenVINO在CPU上最优，TensorRT在GPU上最优，但TensorRT对Transformer模型不优于原生PyTorch。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器视觉系统对低延迟、数据安全或有限连接有严格要求，常采用边缘部署方案；尽管有多种推理优化框架可用，但缺乏在工业部署条件下系统性的推理性能比较研究。

Method: 选取四种主流推理框架（PyTorch、ONNX Runtime、OpenVINO、TensorRT），在多种CPU和GPU硬件平台上，对传统CNN和Transformer两类视觉模型进行推理时间对比评估。

Result: OpenVINO在CPU上推理时间最低；TensorRT在GPU上推理时间最低；但对于Transformer模型，TensorRT未优于原生PyTorch。

Conclusion: 不同推理框架在不同硬件和模型架构下表现差异显著，选择需结合实际部署场景；Transformer模型对现有GPU优化框架提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Edge deployment is often the preferred solution for industrial machine vision systems when low latency, data security, or limited connectivity are critical requirements. Several frameworks are available to optimise inference on edge devices; however, relatively few studies have systematically compared their inference-time performance under industrial deployment conditions.
  In this work, we present a comparative study of four widely used approaches for machine vision inference in industrial settings: plain PyTorch, ONNX Runtime, OpenVINO, and TensorRT. The evaluation focuses on inference time, covers several CPU- and GPU-based hardware platforms, and includes both conventional convolutional neural networks and a transformer-based vision model. For the hardware platforms and models evaluated, the results show that OpenVINO achieves the lowest inference time on CPUs, while TensorRT achieves the lowest inference time on GPUs. However, TensorRT does not outperform plain PyTorch for the transformer-based model considered in this study.

</details>


### [153] [Efficient Tuning Before Low-Bit Post-Training Quantization for Stochastic Gradient Descent-optimized Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11359)
*Peng Xia,Junbiao Pang,Muhammad Ayub Sabir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ETBQ方法，在量化前对全精度模型进行预条件调优，通过注入权重和激活量化误差的扰动来引导模型走向对后续PTQ更不敏感的损失景观区域，从而提升低比特（2/4-bit）PTQ的性能，无需像QAT那样训练伪量化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特PTQ的性能退化不仅源于量化后误差最小化不足，更源于全精度模型本身对量化误差的容忍度有限。现有PTQ方法主要在无约束的全精度模型上进行后验重建，忽略了模型自身的抗量化干扰能力。

Method: 在PTQ之前加入一个预条件调优阶段：对SGD优化的全精度模型，从权重和激活量化的误差分布中采样扰动并注入模型进行优化，使模型收敛到对后续PTQ更不敏感的损失景观区域。ETBQ输出一个全精度模型，可被任意PTQ后端使用，不训练伪量化部署模型。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet、ImageNet和Cityscapes四个数据集上，ETBQ在W2A4设置下对Tiny-ImageNet提升2.14% top-1准确率，对Cityscapes提升5.80% mIoU，且在不同任务上均有一致性提升。

Conclusion: ETBQ通过量化前的高效调优显著提升低比特PTQ性能，计算和内存开销低于QAT，且与任何PTQ后端兼容，是一种即插即用的通用预处理方法。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) compresses deep neural networks for deployment under limited memory and computational budgets. However, low-bit (i.e., 2-bit or 4-bit) PTQ often suffers from substantial performance degradation. Most existing PTQ methods operate on an unconstrained full-precision (FP) model and primarily address quantization errors through post-hoc reconstruction. We argue that low-bit PTQ accuracy is limited not only by post-quantization error minimization, but also by the quantization-error tolerance of a FP model itself. In this paper, we propose Efficient Tuning Before Quantization (ETBQ), a pre-conditioning tuning stage for Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD)-optimized models before PTQ. During tuning, the FP model is optimized under perturbations sampled from the error distributions of weight and activation quantization, guiding the model toward a loss-landscape region that is less sensitive to the subsequent PTQ. Unlike QAT, ETBQ does not train a fake-quantized deployment model, which is computationally and memory intensive. Instead, ETBQ outputs a FP model that can be used by any PTQ backend. Experiments on CIFAR-100, Tiny-ImageNet, ImageNet, and Cityscapes provide consistent evidence that ETBQ improves low-bit PTQ across diverse tasks. Under W2A4 settings, e.g., ETBQ improves over naive PTQ by 2.14\% top-1 accuracy on Tiny-ImageNet and by 5.80\% mIoU on Cityscapes. Code is available at https://github.com/xpxpxp2001xpxpxp/ETBQ.

</details>


### [154] [Self-supervised training for high-resolution close-range multispectral remote sensing imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11366)
*Leon-Friedrich Thomas,Mikael Änäkkälä,Antti Lajunen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了自监督学习（SSL）在厘米级多光谱无人机影像上的预训练效果，利用MoCo-v3和掩码自编码器对Transformer编码器进行预训练，在作物-杂草语义分割任务上取得了最优性能，并提供了跨传感器、跨区域的泛化能力证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习在近距遥感中可减少标注成本，但其在高分辨率多光谱无人机影像上的有效性因数据有限而未被充分探索。

Method: 使用MoCo-v3和掩码自编码器对Swin Transformer等编码器进行预训练，预训练数据为芬兰多年度多传感器无人机影像（4个光谱波段），下游任务为WeedMap数据集上的作物-杂草语义分割。

Result: MoCo-v3预训练的Swin Transformer在德国（Task A）和瑞士（Task B）两个任务上均取得最优性能，超越了Doornbos等人的预训练模型，并展现了跨传感器和跨区域的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练在厘米级多光谱无人机影像上有效，所提方法可提升作物-杂草分割精度并泛化到不同传感器和区域，同时公开了芬兰多年度多光谱无人机数据集以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Although self-supervised learning (SSL) offers a promising way to reduce annotation effort in close-range remote sensing, its effectiveness for high-resolution multispectral unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) imagery remains underexplored due to limited data. This study evaluated SSL pretraining for precision agriculture using cm-scale multispectral drone imagery collected across multiple sensors, years, and regions. Transformer-based encoders were pretrained with Momentum Contrast v3 (MoCo-v3) and Masked Autoencoders on a harmonized dataset combining msuav500K with newly collected multi-year UAV imagery from agricultural fields in Finland. Pretraining used four spectral bands (Green, Red, Red-Edge, Near-Infrared) for cross-sensor compatibility. The models were evaluated on crop-weed semantic segmentation using the WeedMap dataset with 5--100% training data. The following two subsets served as downstream tasks: Task A (Germany, RedEdge-M), where all pretrained models were compared under partial and full fine-tuning, and Task B (Switzerland, Sequoia), where the best encoder from Task A was assessed. Our Swin Transformer pretrained with MoCo-v3 achieved the strongest performance on both tasks, surpassing the Swin Transformer model of Doornbos et al. pretrained on a pre-release of msuav500K. Our pretrained Swin Transformer further demonstrated cross-sensor and cross-region generalization. We additionally provide a public multi-year multispectral UAV dataset from Finland to support future research.

</details>


### [155] [Uncertainty Quantification for EO Regression Tasks: Building Height, Tree Canopy Height and Above-ground Biomass Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11412)
*Ritu Yadav,Andrea Nascetti,Yifang Ban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于时间序列遥感数据的不确定性估计方法（高斯UC和分位数UC），在建筑高度、冠层高度和地上生物量三个回归任务中实现了与确定性模型相当甚至更优的精度，同时提供了良好校准且可解释的逐像素置信度估计，在冠层高度估计任务上超越了当前10米分辨率的最先进不确定性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在地球观测回归任务中仅输出确定性预测，缺乏逐像素的可靠性指示，而实际应用中（如建筑高度、冠层高度、生物量估计）由于地表异质性、目标分布偏斜、传感器噪声以及高目标值信号饱和等问题，不确定性估计对于可靠推理至关重要。

Method: 利用一整年的Sentinel-1 SAR和Sentinel-2 MSI时间序列数据，提出两种互补的不确定性建模方法：(i) 高斯不确定性——在高斯假设下联合预测均值和标准差；(ii) 分位数不确定性——估计第10、50和90分位数以捕捉非对称和异方差误差分布。两种模型均在10米空间分辨率下进行三个代表性EO回归任务的评估。

Result: 两种方法在精度上与确定性基准模型及现有全球产品相当或更优，同时提供了良好校准、可解释且具有操作实用性的置信度估计。两种模型在冠层高度估计任务上均超越了当前10米分辨率的state-of-the-art不确定性感知模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的不确定性估计方法在不牺牲预测精度的前提下，有效提升了深度学习模型在地球观测回归任务中的可靠性和可解释性，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Earth Observation regression tasks such as building height, canopy height, and above-ground biomass estimation underpin critical applications in urban planning, forest monitoring, and climate policy, where both accuracy and reliability are critical. Yet most deep learning models yield only deterministic predictions, providing no indication of per-pixel reliability. These regression tasks are inherently challenging due to heterogeneous land surfaces, skewed target distributions, sensor noise, and signal saturation at high target values, making uncertainty (UC) estimation essential for reliable inference. We address this gap by modeling aleatoric uncertainty using year-long Sentinel-1 SAR and Sentinel-2 MSI time series, proposing two complementary approaches: (i) Gaussian UC, which jointly predicts mean and standard deviation under a Gaussian assumption, and (ii) Quantile UC, which estimates the 10th, 50th, and 90th quantiles to capture asymmetric and heteroscedastic error distributions. Both models are evaluated on three representative EO regression tasks at 10 m spatial resolution. Results show that both approaches match or surpass deterministic benchmarks and existing global products, while delivering well-calibrated, interpretable, and operationally useful confidence estimates. Notably, both models outperform the current 10 m state-of-the-art uncertainty-aware model for canopy height estimation. Our implementation will be available at: https://github.com/RituYadav92/EO-Regression-Uncertainty-Estimation

</details>


### [156] [Video Transformer for Remote Identity Document Hologram Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11419)
*Joris Voerman,Nicolas Sidere,Jean-Christophe Burie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视频变压器的远程身份证件验证系统（RIDVS），通过智能手机拍摄的视频检测全息防伪印刷，在中等规模数据集上实现了近乎完美的准确率，相比现有最佳基线方法在召回率上提升26.86%，准确率提升17.93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程身份认证中身份证件伪造问题日益严重，尤其是DeepFake和AI工具被用于制作假证件；现有安全特征检测常需专用硬件（如红外传感器），而全息印刷作为难以伪造且可通过智能手机摄像头检测的特征尚未被充分利用。

Method: 提出RIDVS系统，采用智能手机端视频采集+服务器端验证的两阶段架构；核心方法基于视频Transformer（Video Transformer）检测全息印刷的独特视觉效果；模型从相关研究领域的预训练鲁棒模型迁移而来。

Result: 在MIDV-Holo数据集上，召回率提升26.86%，准确率提升17.93%，超越现有最先进方法；即使在中小规模数据集训练下也达到近乎完美的准确率；验证了模型对训练样本数量和计算资源的 frugality（节俭性）。

Conclusion: 基于视频Transformer的全息检测方法可有效用于智能手机视频的远程身份证件验证，在保持高精度的同时具有较好的资源效率，适合大规模部署。

Abstract: Remote identity authentification using Identification Documents has been a major challenge for several years. DeepFakes advent and the development of AI-guided tools helps fraudsters creating counterfeit ID Documents. Ensuring the authenticity of ID Documents has become a real clue in the seurization of remote authentification. This need is all the more pressing given the increasing digitization of administrative and transactional processes. To ensure widespread accessibility, the system should rely solely on video captured via mobile devices. In this specific context, confirming the authenticity of ID is a real challenge as many security features needs specific device like infrared sensor for instance. Among underutilized but promising security features, holographic printings hold a special place. Difficult to counterfeit, they produce distinctive visual effects according enlightment, making them both detectable in a video captured by a smartphone camera and difficult to imitate. In this paper, we propose a Remote Identity Document Verification System (RIDVS) and an approach based on a video transformer for detecting holograms in simple videos captured by smartphones. Our system is designed for a smartphone-based capture process, followed by a server-side verification. The hologram detection method builds on a robust model previously validated in a related research domain. We demonstrate that it outperforms existing SotA methods, achieving near-perfect accuracy even when trained on medium- to small-sized datasets. In particular, we report improvements of +26.86\% in Recall and +17.93\% in accuracy over the best MIDV-Holo baseline. This study includes several experiments that evaluate the model adaptation to frugality, both for training samples and computational resources.

</details>


### [157] [Towards Efficient Convolutional Neural Network for Embedded Hardware via Multi-Dimensional Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11473)
*Hao Kong,Di Liu,Xiangzhong Luo,Shuo Huai,Ravi Subramaniam,Christian Makaya,Qian Lin,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TECO是一个多维剪枝框架，通过协同剪枝CNN的深度、宽度和分辨率三个维度，在嵌入式硬件上实现更好的执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有剪枝方法通常只关注单一维度（如仅剪枝通道或层），忽略了不同维度之间的协同效应，难以在嵌入式硬件上达到最优的准确率-效率权衡。

Method: 提出两阶段重要性评估框架（局部重要性+全局重要性），并结合启发式剪枝算法，逐步对CNN的深度、宽度和分辨率三个维度进行协同剪枝。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，TECO优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: 多维协同剪枝能更有效地在准确率和效率之间取得平衡，适用于嵌入式硬件的CNN部署优化。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose TECO, a multi-dimensional pruning framework to collaboratively prune the three dimensions (depth, width, and resolution) of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) for better execution efficiency on embedded hardware. In TECO, we first introduce a two-stage importance evaluation framework, which efficiently and comprehensively evaluates each pruning unit according to both the local importance inside each dimension and the global importance across different dimensions. Based on the evaluation framework, we present a heuristic pruning algorithm to progressively prune the three dimensions of CNNs towards the optimal trade-off between accuracy and efficiency. Experiments on multiple benchmarks validate the advantages of TECO over existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches. The code and pre-trained models are available at https://github.com/ntuliuteam/Teco.

</details>


### [158] [HyperGS: Fast and Generalizable Gaussian Video Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11500)
*Fatimah Zohra,Chen Zhao,Shuming Liu,Yahya Al Malallah,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HyperGS 是一种前馈式、无需逐视频优化的高斯溅射视频表示方法，通过分解时空Transformer和可学习查询Transformer直接预测视频帧的2D高斯参数，并引入自适应秩几何正则化器解决训练退化问题，实现了比逐视频优化快10^4-10^5倍的编码速度，并零样本泛化到720p视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯溅射的视频表示方法需要逐视频优化，导致编码速度慢且难以跨视频泛化。为了加速编码过程并实现跨视频的泛化能力，作者提出一种前馈式方法，直接预测高斯表示。

Method: 设计分解时空Transformer从视频中提取token，再用可学习查询Transformer预测每帧的8参数高斯表示。针对直接预测导致的针状退化问题，提出自适应的秩几何正则化器来稳定训练。

Result: 编码速度比逐视频高斯优化快10^4-10^5倍，在K400、SSv2、UCF101上PSNR提升2.9-3.1 dB，且零样本泛化到720p视频。

Conclusion: HyperGS将高斯溅射的快速灵活渲染与前馈预测的速度与泛化能力相结合，推动了高斯作为一种快速且可泛化的视频表示方向的实际应用。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has emerged as an effective representation for video, but existing methods rely on per-video optimization. This leads to slow encoding and limits generalization across videos. To amortize this optimization, we propose HyperGS, a feedforward, optimization-free approach that directly predicts Gaussian representations from any video in a single forward pass, speeding up encoding and decoding by orders of magnitude while generalizing to out-of-distribution videos at higher resolutions. In HyperGS, we design a factorized spatiotemporal Transformer to extract tokens from video, and a learnable query-based Transformer to obtain 8-parameter Gaussian representations for each video frame. We find that naively predicting Gaussians across diverse videos induces a needle-like degeneration that collapses training, and address this with a rank-based geometric regularizer whose strength adapts dynamically to stabilize optimization. HyperGS achieves encoding at $10^4$--$10^5\times$ the speed of per-video Gaussian optimization at matched reconstruction quality while generalizing zero-shot to $720p$ video, enabling higher-resolution rendering without re-encoding. HyperGS improves PSNR by +2.9--3.1 dB over the prior video encoders on K400, SSv2, and UCF101 at a smaller video representation size. By predicting explicit 2D Gaussians in a single forward pass, HyperGS combines the fast, flexible rendering of Gaussian Splatting with the speed and generalization of feedforward prediction, advancing Gaussians as a practical direction for fast and generalizable video representation.

</details>


### [159] [CFR-Net:Collaborative Feature Refnement Network for Medical Image Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11509)
*Zihan Nie,Muhao Xu,Wei Feng,Yuan Cui,Hua Wei,Sijie Niu,Yi Wan,Xunbin Wei,Weiye Song,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出协作特征精炼网络(CFR-Net)，通过共享教师-学生特征精炼与跨空间一致性约束，解决医学图像异常检测中预训练网络适应性不足的问题，在正常数据训练下实现优异的异常分类与定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像预训练网络对医学图像适应性有限，难以捕捉医学图像中细粒度局部偏移、多尺度上下文不匹配及方向敏感结构偏差等异常模式。

Method: CFR-Net包含解码前的共享教师-学生特征精炼（多路径特征精炼模块MPFRM）与解码后的跨空间一致性约束；采用方差敏感目标和动态"作业集"重组支持层自适应一致性学习。

Result: 在医学基准上，仅用正常数据训练即可实现具有竞争力的异常分类性能和强异常定位能力。

Conclusion: CFR-Net通过共享多路径精炼规则有效缓解域差异，同时建模局部、多尺度及方向敏感特征，为医学图像异常检测提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Medical image anomaly detection remains challenging because networks pretrained on natural images often exhibit limited adaptability to medical images, where abnormal patterns appear as fine-grained local shifts, multi-scale contextual mismatches, and orientation-sensitive structural deviations. To address this, we propose the Collaborative Feature Refinement Network (CFR-Net), which combines shared teacher-student feature refinement before decoding with cross-space consistency after decoding. CFR-Net refines frozen teacher features and trainable student features using a Multi-Path Feature Refinement Module (MPFRM) with shared parameters, imposing common multi-path refinement rules on generic visual references and representations adapted to the medical domain, thereby mitigating domain discrepancy while modeling local, multi-scale, and orientation-sensitive feature characteristics. A variance-sensitive objective and dynamic ``homework set'' reorganization further support layer-adaptive consistency learning. Experiments on medical benchmarks show that CFR-Net achieves competitive anomaly classification and strong anomaly localization performance when trained on normal data.

</details>


### [160] [Vinci2: Providing Proactive Assistance in Continuous Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11523)
*Gong Sitong,Tianyu Yan,Caixin Kang,Bo Zheng,Xiang Ruan,Huchuan Lu,Kaipeng Zhang,Yoichi Sato,Yifei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Vinci2，一个面向连续自我中心视频的主动辅助系统。它构建了EgoServe基准（3000+服务实例，4种时间记忆范围，10个类别）和EgoMemo模型（无需训练、三重组记忆增强：多尺度时序摘要、语义知识图谱、视觉嵌入档案），通过检索增强推理决定是否以及何时主动提供帮助。实验表明EgoMemo在EgoServe上建立强基线，在现有基准上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能助手要么被动等待用户查询，要么对每个检测到的事件都做出响应，而不考虑用户的历史行为、当前活动或是否需要帮助。本文旨在将主动辅助重新定义为上下文相关的决策问题，使代理不仅能感知正在发生的事情，还能基于累积的时间上下文推理何时以及是否进行干预。

Method: 提出了EgoMemo，一种无需训练的记忆增强代理，维护三种互补的记忆表示：多尺度时序摘要（Multi-scale Temporal Summaries）、语义知识图谱（Semantic Knowledge Graph）和视觉嵌入档案（Visual Embedding Archives）。在每一时间步，通过检索增强推理（Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning）判断是否需要提供帮助，并生成上下文相关的响应。

Result: 构建了EgoServe基准数据集（3000+服务实例，4种时间记忆范围从即时安全警报到长期习惯指导，覆盖10个服务类别）。实验证明EgoMemo在EgoServe上建立了强基线，同时在现有自我中心视频基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 首次将连续自我中心视频中的主动辅助系统化为结构化的决策问题，提供了大规模基准和无需训练的高效建模方案，推动了智能助手从被动响应向主动服务的转变。

Abstract: When should an intelligent assistant speak up without being asked? Continuous egocentric video offers rich, evolving context that enables a new form of assistance: one that is proactive rather than merely reactive. Yet existing approaches either wait passively for user queries or treat every detected event as requiring a response, without considering the user's history, current activity, or whether assistance would actually be welcome. We reframe proactive assistance as a context-dependent decision problem: the agent must not only perceive what is happening, but reason over accumulated temporal context to determine when and whether to intervene. To this end, we present Vinci2, a proactive egocentric assistance system that advances the on-device assistant Vinci from reactive response toward proactivity. On the evaluation side, we present EgoServe, the first large-scale benchmark for proactive assistance in continuous egocentric video. EgoServe comprises over 3,000 service instances organized along 4 temporal memory horizons, ranging from immediate safety alerts to long-term habit coaching, across 10 service categories. On the modeling side, we propose EgoMemo, a training-free, memory-augmented agent that maintains three complementary memory representations: multi-scale temporal summaries, a semantic knowledge graph, and visual embedding archives. At each timestep, EgoMemo performs retrieval-augmented reasoning to determine whether assistance is warranted and, if so, produces contextually grounded responses. Experiments demonstrate that EgoMemo establishes strong baselines on EgoServe while remaining competitive on existing egocentric benchmarks. Our benchmark and code are publicly available at \href{https://sitonggong.github.io/EgoServe-page/}{Vinci2}.

</details>


### [161] [Parse, Search, and Confirmation: Training-Free Aerial Vision-and-Dialog Navigation with Chain-of-Thought Reasoning and Structured Spatial Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11529)
*Yu Qi,Hongyu Li,Shaofei Huang,Tianrui Hui,Yaxiong Wang,Lechao Cheng,Zhun Zhong,Si Liu,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PSC-AVDN框架，一种无需训练的训练框架，通过三阶段解析-搜索-确认推理流程与结构化空间记忆相结合，在无需微调的情况下解决了空中视觉对话导航任务中的方向定位和空间记忆问题，在ANDH和ANDH-Full数据集上达到训练无关设置下的最优性能，甚至超越了一些微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在无需训练的情况下应用于空中视觉对话导航（AVDN）时，存在方向定位不准确和缺乏显式空间记忆的问题，导致导航可靠性不足。本文旨在提出一种无需训练的框架来解决这些挑战，实现资源高效的无人机高空导航。

Method: 提出PSC-AVDN框架，核心包括：(1) 三阶段推理流程：解析阶段（Parsing）利用LLM将模糊对话指令转换为稳定的几何方向和目的地线索；搜索阶段采用搜索思维链（S-CoT）在高空观测下进行逐步目标探索；确认阶段采用确认思维链（C-CoT）在候选区域周围进行细粒度验证以解决视觉歧义。(2) 结构化空间记忆（SSM）：整合多尺度视觉观测、空间视觉记忆和结构化几何记忆三种互补的空间线索来源，提供全局空间上下文和长期一致性。

Result: 在ANDH和ANDH-Full数据集上的大量实验表明，PSC-AVDN在无需训练的设置下达到了新的最先进性能，匹配甚至超越了多种需要微调的方法。

Conclusion: PSC-AVDN通过将结构化的推理流程与空间记忆机制相结合，在无需训练的条件下实现了高空无人机导航的显著性能提升，展示了利用大语言模型推理能力和显式空间记忆替代传统微调范式的可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we tackle the Aerial Vision-and-Dialog Navigation (AVDN) task in the training-free setting for resource-efficient high-altitude UAV navigation.Naively applying MLLMs leads to unreliable navigation due to weak directional grounding and the lack of explicit spatial memory.To address these issues, we propose PSC-AVDN, a training-free framework that tightly couples a three-stage Parsing-Search-Confirmation reasoning pipeline with a Structured Spatial Memory (SSM).The parsing stage uses an LLM to convert ambiguous dialogue instructions into stable geometric directional and destination cues.A Search Chain-of-Thought (S-CoT) then performs stepwise target exploration under high-altitude observations, and a Confirmation Chain-of-Thought (C-CoT) conducts fine-grained verification around candidate regions to resolve visual ambiguity.Meanwhile, SSM integrates three complementary sources of spatial cues, including multi-scale visual observation, spatial visual memory, and structured geometric memory to provide global spatial context and long-horizon consistency.Extensive experiments on ANDH and ANDH-Full show that PSC-AVDN establishes new state-of-the-art performance in the training-free setting, matching or surpassing several finetuned methods.Code will be publicly available at: https://github.com/QY6616/PSC-AVDN

</details>


### [162] [Adaptive Routing for Efficient Diffusion Transformer-Based PNI Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11533)
*Youngung Han,Dohyun Kweon,Kyeonghun Kim,Hyunsu Go,Jina Jeong,Suah Park,Induk Um,Junga Kim,Anna Jung,Yului Jeong,Sungha Park,Jinyong Jun,Pa Hong,Woo Kyoung Jeong,Won Jae Lee,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于扩散模型和Transformer的胆管癌神经周围侵犯（PNI）术前预测方法，通过自适应路由机制降低计算开销，在MRI数据上实现了0.731的AUC和257.57 GFLOPs的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胆管癌中神经周围侵犯（PNI）是重要的预后因素，但术前从MRI中预测PNI具有挑战性，因为影像特征微弱且超出肿瘤边界。现有CNN难以捕捉长程空间依赖，Transformer虽能全局建模但对瘤周区域噪声敏感模式捕捉不足，扩散分类器可缓解此问题但计算开销巨大。

Method: 将PNI预测建模为基于扩散的分类问题，使用Transformer作为去噪网络；引入自适应路由机制，在注意力头、空间token和MLP宽度三个维度上动态选择计算路径，以提升计算效率。

Result: 该方法在胆管癌PNI预测任务上达到0.731的AUC，计算量为257.57 GFLOPs。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了扩散分类器结合Transformer在医学影像微弱特征预测中的潜力，自适应路由机制有效缓解了计算开销问题，为PNI术前预测提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Perineural invasion (PNI) is a critical prognostic factor in cholangiocarcinoma. However, its preoperative prediction from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) remains challenging due to subtle imaging features that extend beyond tumor boundaries into surrounding regions. Conventional convolutional neural networks are limited in capturing long-range spatial dependencies. Transformer-based architectures improve global modeling of volumetric MRI by aggregating spatially distributed contextual cues, yet capturing subtle and noise-sensitive patterns in peritumoral regions remains challenging. Diffusion-based classifiers offer an alternative formulation by leveraging denoising-based class scoring to better capture such subtle patterns. However, these approaches introduce substantial computational overhead due to the combination of transformer-based modeling and iterative denoising processes. To address these challenges, we formulate PNI prediction as a diffusion-based classification problem and implement the denoising network using a transformer-based representation. To improve computational efficiency, we introduce adaptive routing across attention heads, spatial tokens, and MLP width. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves an AUC of 0.731 with 257.57 GFLOPs.

</details>


### [163] [Training-Free Off-Screen Player Imputation for Broadcast-Based Spatial Football Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11548)
*Seongjin Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究广播主视角足球视频中仅能观测到10-16名球员（而非全部22名）导致的空间指标失真问题，提出无需训练、在线可用的角色锚定质心投票插补方法，显著降低隐藏区域控球控制误差，并在真实世界杯广播数据上验证其对下游决策指标的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球空间指标（如控球控制）假设获得全部22名球员位置，但最广泛可用的广播主视角数据仅显示10-16名球员；忽略屏幕外球员会导致严重的指标失真，而现有方法缺乏对此问题的量化基准和有效解决方案。

Method: 1) 构建模拟广播视口的可复现基准（基于Metrica Sports公开数据）；2) 提出角色锚定质心投票（Role-Anchored Centroid Voting）：可见球员通过减去其运行角色偏移来投票估计全队质心，从而减弱视口引入的子集偏差；3) 在广播视频GSR管线中端到端集成插补模块。

Result: 忽略屏幕外球员导致隐藏区域控球控制误差25.1-26.9个百分点，平均控制份额绝对误差11.1-13.4点；提出的方法将隐藏区误差降至12.2-13.8点（约减半），控制份额误差降至忽略策略的28-48%；对≤9.6秒遮挡中位位置误差3.3-8.9米，但50-57%遮挡超出此范围；在世界杯广播数据上使下游Space-Creation Index移动15.6和17.2点，并翻转了一个判罚结论。

Conclusion: 广播视角下球员不完整观测对空间指标影响显著，而无需训练的在线插补方法（角色锚定质心投票）能有效缓解此问题，且对下游决策具有实质性影响，应在GSR管线中集成插补层。

Abstract: Spatial football metrics such as pitch control assume access to the positions of all 22 players, yet the most widely available source of positional data -- the broadcast main camera -- shows only 10-16 of them at any moment. We quantify the resulting distortion with an open, reproducible benchmark: a simulated broadcast viewport applied to open full-pitch tracking data (Metrica Sports; three matches, one held out from method development). Ignoring off-screen players -- the visible-only baseline implied whenever a video-based game-state-reconstruction (GSR) pipeline adds no imputation layer -- inflates hidden-zone pitch-control error to 25.1-26.9 percentage points and a mean absolute control-share error of 11.1-13.4 points across the three matches. We then evaluate a ladder of training-free, online imputation baselines that use only observations from the match being analysed. The best overall on these decision-relevant metrics, role-anchored centroid voting (each visible player votes for the full-team centroid by subtracting its running role offset, attenuating the viewport-induced subset bias), roughly halves hidden-zone error (to 12.2-13.8 points) and cuts control-share error to 28-48% of the ignore policy at every viewport width from 36 m to 60 m in all three matches. For occlusions <=9.6 s -- the regime of the closest learned prior work -- it reaches binwise median position errors of 3.3-8.9 m; but 50-57% of hidden-player observations lie beyond that regime. Integrated end-to-end into a broadcast-video GSR pipeline, imputation moves a downstream possession-quality score (Space-Creation Index) by 15.6 and 17.2 points on two real World Cup broadcast windows, flipping the verdict class in one.

</details>


### [164] [Single-Teacher View Augmentation: Enhancing Knowledge Distillation with Student-Guided Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11557)
*Xuyi Yu,Yaohua Liu,Ziming Song,Yinghai Zhao,Huipeng Zhang,Kuizhi Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Shift-Augmented Knowledge Distillation (SAKD)，通过利用学生网络动态特征生成自适应多样化视角，以参数无关的循环移位实现单阶段训练，在保持效率的同时达到与多教师蒸馏相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏方法存在监督信号多样性有限（单教师）或计算成本过高（多教师）的问题；而虚拟视角生成方法在效率与多样性之间存在权衡——随机扰动缺乏可控多样性，结构化增强需要多阶段训练且参数线性增长。

Method: 提出SAKD框架，核心创新是将视角生成的扰动条件从教师静态特征转向学生动态特征，利用学生不断演化的特征作为条件，通过参数无关的循环移位（parameter-free cyclic shift）生成自适应、多样化的视角，实现单阶段训练。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet上，SAKD一致优于随机扰动方法，精度与两阶段方法相当，同时显著减少参数量并消除预训练需求。

Conclusion: 通过将扰动生成的条件从教师静态特征转向学生动态特征，打破了效率与多样性之间的权衡，为知识蒸馏提供了一种简洁有效的单阶段解决方案。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) typically relies on the fixed perspective of a single teacher, limiting the diversity of supervisory signals. While multi-teacher distillation addresses this by aggregating knowledge from multiple models, it incurs prohibitive computational and storage costs. To balance efficiency and diversity, recent research has focused on generating virtual views from a single teacher. However, existing methods face a trade-off: random perturbation approaches offer efficiency but lack controlled diversity, while structured augmentation methods require multi-stage training and incur linear parameter growth. We observe that this trade-off stems from a common design choice: using the teacher's strong but static features to generate views. Instead, we propose Shift-Augmented Knowledge Distillation (SAKD), a simple yet effective framework that leverages the student's evolving features as a dynamic condition for perturbation generation. This shift in perspective enables single-stage training while producing adaptive, diverse views through a parameter-free cyclic shift. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet demonstrate that SAKD consistently outperforms random perturbation methods and achieves accuracy on par with two-stage approaches, while using significantly fewer parameters and eliminating pre-training requirements.

</details>


### [165] [Technical Report on the CVPR 2026@AdvML Workshop Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11560)
*Tianyuan Zhang,Zonglei Jing,Jiangfan Liu,Ligong Zhang,Ke Ma,Chengzhi Sun,Xiaohai Xu,Zhirui Zhang,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang,Hanyu Fang,Junhua Liu,Zheng Wang,Xiaoliang Liu,Yuanbo Li,Shuai Gui,Bin Wang,Menghe Zheng,Jing Nie,Hanyang Meng,Zeyang Zhang,Xiang Zhang,Yongxuan Zhu,Rui Ding,Hainan Li,Yongkang Zhang,Zhilei Zhu,Xianglong Kong,Jin Hu,Zonghao Ying,Yisong Xiao,Lei Chen,Haotong Qin,Jiakai Wang,Aishan Liu,Ruikai Li,Julia Karbing,Yinpeng Dong,Zhenfei Yin,Shao Jing,Xia Hu,Jingyi Xu,Juntao Dai,Xinyun Chen,Vishal M. Patel,Xianglong Liu,Dawn Song,Alan Yuille,Philip H. S. Torr,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CVPR 2026@AdvML Workshop举办的面向自动驾驶视觉语言模型(VLA)的对抗性多模态攻击挑战赛。基于DriveLM风格的多视角视觉问答，参赛者需生成对抗图像和文本后缀扰动，使模型输出偏离参考答案，同时保持图像保真度和限制文本成本。比赛分两阶段，第二阶段加入隐藏黑盒模型评估可迁移性。文章分析了五份顶尖技术报告，发现图像侧攻击更受青睐、场景级多视角优化优于单视角、QA类型和图结构可作为攻击预算先验、特征空间目标可提升黑盒迁移性、图像中的印刷内容暴露了持续漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉语言模型(VLA)在自动驾驶等安全关键领域的应用日益广泛，评估其鲁棒性变得至关重要。现有对抗攻击研究多集中于单一模态或简单场景，缺乏针对复杂多视角自动驾驶场景下多模态对抗攻击的系统性评估。该挑战赛旨在填补这一空白，推动自动驾驶VLA安全性研究。

Method: 比赛采用DriveLM风格的多视角视觉问答框架，每个场景包含6个同步摄像头图像及结构化的驾驶相关问题-答案对。参赛者需生成对抗性图像扰动（保持视觉保真度）和后缀文本扰动（控制文本长度成本），使VLA输出偏离参考答案。比赛分两阶段：Phase I针对已知白盒模型；Phase II引入隐藏黑盒模型评估攻击可迁移性。

Result: 五个领先提交的技术报告揭示了以下模式：1) 由于后缀惩罚机制，图像侧攻击更受欢迎；2) 场景级多视角联合优化比单视角处理更有效；3) QA类型和图结构可作为攻击预算分配的先验信息；4) 特征空间优化目标可提升黑盒迁移性；5) 摄像头图像中的印刷文字内容暴露了自动驾驶VLA的持续脆弱性。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛系统评估了自动驾驶VLA在多模态对抗攻击下的鲁棒性，识别了关键攻击模式和漏洞。研究发现为未来多模态自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性评估和防御设计提供了实用参考。

Abstract: Vision-language agents (VLAs) are increasingly used to interpret complex driving scenes and support safety-critical reasoning. This report presents the CVPR 2026@AdvML Workshop Challenge on adversarial multimodal attacks against autonomous-driving VLAs. Built on DriveLM-style multi-view visual question answering, the challenge represents each scene with six synchronized camera images and a structured collection of driving-related question-answer pairs. Participants generate adversarial images and suffix-only textual perturbations that induce model responses to deviate from reference answers while preserving image fidelity and limiting textual cost. The competition comprises two phases, with Phase II adding a hidden black-box model to assess transferability. We describe the task design, submission rules, evaluation protocol, and leaderboard results, and then examine five leading submissions for which technical reports were available. Across these reports, several recurring patterns emerge: image-side attacks are favored by the suffix penalty; scene-level, multi-view optimization is more effective than treating views in isolation; QA types and graph structure provide useful priors for allocating attack budget; feature-space objectives can improve black-box transfer; and typographic content embedded in camera images exposes a persistent vulnerability in driving VLAs. These findings provide a practical reference for future robustness evaluation and defense design in multimodal autonomous-driving systems.

</details>


### [166] [MonkeyOCRv2: A Visual-Text Foundation Model for Document AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11562)
*Yuliang Liu,Zhang Li,Ziyang Zhang,Shuo Zhang,Qiang Liu,Jiajun Song,Zidun Guo,Xinhan Wang,Handong Zheng,Yang Liu,Dongliang Luo,Zhiyin Ma,Jiarui Zhang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MonkeyOCRv2提出面向文档图像的视觉-文本预训练方法，构建包含1.13亿张图像、覆盖17种语言的最大文档预训练语料库MonkeyDoc v2，通过图像到文本生成与像素级文档重建联合预训练策略，在五个文档分析任务上一致提升性能，作为视觉编码器在0.7B小参数文档解析模型中达到开源SOTA，超越3B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流视觉编码器在自然图像上预训练，无法直接有效应用于文档图像，因为文档图像包含密集文本和精细字符笔画，需要字符级的视觉感知能力。现有方法缺乏面向文档的大规模预训练。

Method: 1) 构建MonkeyDoc v2——目前最大的文档图像预训练语料库，包含1.13亿张图像、17种语言；2) 提出联合预训练策略：图像到文本生成（对齐视觉表征与文本内容）与像素级文档重建（保留字符笔画和布局细节）。

Result: 在文本识别、公式识别、文本检测、文档篡改检测、重叠文本分割五个任务上，替换原有编码器为MonkeyOCRv2均一致提升性能。在MDPBench基准上，0.7B文档解析模型以约11倍小的视觉编码器超越3B模型2.8%绝对提升；文档理解模型在8个基准上超越基于CLIP/DINO/SAM的模型。

Conclusion: 面向文档的视觉预训练可以自成体系地作为文档智能的基础，MonkeyOCRv2作为视觉编码器在多种文档任务上展现出卓越的泛化能力和效率优势。

Abstract: Mainstream visual encoders are pretrained on natural images and cannot be effectively applied to document images without document-oriented adaptation, as dense text and fine-grained character strokes demand character-level visual perception. We present MonkeyOCRv2, a visual-text pretrained model for document AI. First, we construct MonkeyDoc v2, to our knowledge the largest document-image pretraining corpus, comprising 113 million images spanning 17 languages. Second, we propose a pretraining strategy that jointly learns image-to-text generation and pixel-level document reconstruction: the former aligns visual representations with textual content, while the latter preserves character strokes and layout details. Extensive experiments are conducted on five representative document analysis tasks, including text recognition, formula recognition, text detection, document tampering detection, and overlapping text segmentation. Replacing the original encoders with MonkeyOCRv2 consistently improves performance across all five tasks. Finally, we validate its effectiveness as the vision encoder of multimodal large language models on the more challenging tasks of document parsing and document understanding. Kept frozen and paired with a lightweight language model, it yields a 0.7B document parsing model that sets a new open-source state-of-the-art on MDPBench, a recent benchmark spanning digital-born and photographed documents across 17 languages, surpassing the previous best 3B dots.mocr by 2.8% absolute with a vision encoder roughly 11$\times$ smaller. The frozen encoder also powers a document understanding model that outperforms counterparts built on CLIP, DINO, and SAM across eight benchmarks under identical training settings. These results suggest that document-oriented visual pretraining can serve as a foundation for document intelligence in its own right.

</details>


### [167] [Actor as Its Own Critic: Unifying Region Understanding and Localization via CycleGRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11581)
*Xin Zhang,Haochen Wang,Yikang Zhou,Jason Li,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CycleGRPO框架，通过“区域→文本→区域”的自评价强化学习范式，让单个MLLM同时作为演员（生成区域描述）和评论家（将文本回标到空间域），利用任务间的对偶性联合优化区域理解与定位，无需文本真值即可同步提升两种能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将区域理解与定位视为分离的流水线，未能利用二者之间内在的对偶关系，且依赖文本标注数据，限制了MLLM在像素级任务上的能力提升。

Method: 提出CycleGRPO框架，统一强化学习范式：单个MLLM先作为演员生成区域描述，再切换为评论家将生成文本回标到空间域；采用质量感知的token级循环一致性奖励，通过物理定位精度评估文本语义判别力；基于SAMTok构建，无需文本真值。

Result: 无需任何任务微调，在区域描述、区域VQA、接地对话、指代分割等多个基准上均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: CycleGRPO提供了一种简洁且可扩展的方式，有效推进MLLM的像素级能力。

Abstract: This paper introduces Actor as Its Own Critic, a unified reinforcement learning framework, Cycle Group Relative Policy Optimization (CycleGRPO), that jointly optimizes region understanding and localization for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Unlike existing separate pipelines, we leverage the inherent duality between the two tasks to construct a self-evaluating reinforcement learning paradigm: "region $\to$ text $\to$ region''. Specifically, a single MLLM first acts as the actor to generate region captions, then immediately transitions to a critic to ground its generated text back in the spatial domain. Therefore, CycleGRPO requires only region inputs, e.g., masks or bounding boxes, entirely bypassing the need for textual ground truths. A quality-aware token-level cycle-consistency reward is employed to assess the semantic discriminability of text captions via their physical localization accuracy. Empirically, built upon SAMTok, our CycleGRPO framework successfully bootstraps both capabilities simultaneously. Without any task-specific fine-tuning, the framework yields consistent performance gains across a wide range of benchmarks, including region captioning, region VQA, grounded dialogue, and referring segmentation. Overall, CycleGRPO offers a straightforward and scalable way to advance pixel-level capabilities in MLLMs. Code and models are released at https://github.com/devinxzhang/CycleGRPO.

</details>


### [168] [GB-SVFBP: Gaussian-Based Shift-Variant FBP neural network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11584)
*Chengze Ye,Linda-Sophie Schneider,Yipeng Sun,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于高斯核的移位变体滤波反投影神经网络（GSF-FBP），用于非圆形轨迹锥束CT的高效重建，在参数减少99%、训练时间缩短至1/4的同时，仅牺牲少量重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可微移位变体FBP模型参数过多、训练效率低，在非圆形轨迹锥束CT重建中实用性受限，亟需一种参数高效且收敛快的可学习重建方法。

Method: 在可微移位变体FBP框架的基础上，引入可训练的2D高斯模型来表征滤波过程中与轨迹相关的部分，从而大幅减少可训练参数数量。

Result: 参数数量减少99%，每条轨迹的训练时间降至原来的1/4，重建质量仅有轻微下降。

Conclusion: 所提模型显著提升了实用性和有效性，在保持可接受重建质量的同时实现了参数和训练时间的大幅压缩，适用于实际应用场景。

Abstract: This paper proposes a Gaussian-Based Shift-Variant filtered backprojection (FBP) neural network, which is designed for the efficient reconstruction of non-circular trajectory cone beam computed tomography. The traditional differentiable shift-variant FBP model consists of a filtering component and a backprojection process. The filtering component includes operations such as weightings, differentiations, a 2D Radon transform, and a 2D backprojection. The proposed methods build on this framework by introducing a trainable 2D Gaussian model to represent the trajectory-related part in the filtering process, achieving a substantial reduction in the number of trainable parameters. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model reduces the parameter count by 99%, while only sacrificing a slight amount of reconstruction quality. Furthermore, the training time for each trajectory is reduced to one-fourth of the original, significantly accelerating convergence. These enhancements demonstrate a considerable augmentation in the model's practicality and effectiveness, making it a valuable asset for real-world applications.

</details>


### [169] [FoundationGeo: Learning Spatial Pixel-Wise Fields for Monocular Metric Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11588)
*Muxin Liu,Xiaoyang Lyu,Tianhe Ren,Peng Dai,Xiaoshan Wu,Zhiyue Zhang,Jiaqi Zhang,Jiehong Lin,Shaoshuai Shi,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoundationGeo是一个两阶段框架，通过空间校准和原则性数据设计显式桥接相对预测与度量预测。第一阶段学习仿射不变几何模型，第二阶段引入逐像素校准场实现度量估计，并通过Blender数据引擎解决焦距分布不匹配问题，在7个基准上零样本评估中平均超越强基线5.2%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在跨域零样本度量泛化中表现不稳定，特别是测试相机内参（焦距）分布与训练分布不匹配时性能急剧下降，缺乏显式桥接相对几何与度量预测的通用框架。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage1基于DINOv3初始化，在1020万样本多域语料上训练仿射不变几何模型，辅以局部细节监督；Stage2引入轻量级逐像素校准场，包括尺度场（空间变化度量对齐）和射线方向校正场（缓解点图几何方向偏差），并通过Blender数据引擎合成多焦距训练数据修复覆盖不足的焦距区间。

Result: 在7个零样本评估基准上，FoundationGeo显著增强跨域鲁棒性，避免其他方法出现的跨域性能剧烈下降，平均超越更重的基线方法5.2%以上。

Conclusion: 显式空间校准和原则性数据设计（尤其是相机内参覆盖）是提升几何模型跨域度量泛化的关键，FoundationGeo证明了相对预测与度量预测桥接的有效性。

Abstract: We present FoundationGeo, a two-stage framework that explicitly bridges relative and metric prediction via spatial calibration and principled data design. Stage 1 learns a high-fidelity, affine-invariant geometry model by initializing with DINOv3 and training on a curated 10.2M-sample multi-domain corpus with complementary local-detail supervision, yielding sharp boundaries and strong cross-domain generalization. Stage 2 moves beyond global scaling by introducing lightweight pixel-wise calibration fields for metric estimation: a scale field for spatially varying metric alignment and a ray-direction correction field that mitigates directional bias in point-map geometry, together producing metrically consistent 3D point maps. Beyond model design, we identify camera intrinsic coverage, especially focal length distribution mismatch between training and test data, as a key bottleneck for zero-shot metric generalization: performance drops sharply when test intrinsics fall outside the training distribution. To address this, we synthesize additional training data across diverse focal lengths using a Blender-based data engine, repairing under-covered focal regimes and improving robustness under intrinsic shift. Extensive zero-shot evaluations across seven benchmarks show that FoundationGeo significantly strengthens cross-domain robustness, staying near the top across diverse domains while avoiding the sharp cross-domain performance drops observed in other methods. This consistency translates into the best overall performance, surpassing heavier baselines by over 5.2% on average.

</details>


### [170] [Similarity-Guided Curriculum Fine-Tuning of LLMs for Neural Architecture Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11591)
*Anujaya Vijayakumar,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于MinHash的相似度调度框架，通过128排列MinHash签名对7-gram代码分片进行归一化，将架构库划分为相似度带并构建渐进课程，在OlympicCoder-7B上的CIFAR-10分类任务中达到60%峰值成功率；消融实验显示接口修复与课程调度分别针对权重漂移和评估器先验损失等不同故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的神经架构搜索缺乏对架构代码相似性的系统利用，无法有效引导模型从熟悉架构向新颖架构渐进学习，导致生成质量不佳或搜索效率低下。

Method: 1) 使用128排列MinHash签名对归一化的7-gram代码分片进行哈希，将参考架构库按相似度划分带区；2) 按递增架构异质性顺序呈现训练数据，构建渐进课程；3) 每阶段的最佳LoRA适配器累积合并入骨干模型；4) 每轮生成N=15个候选架构，共6个渐进微调步骤。

Result: 课程框架在CIFAR-10上达到60%峰值成功率（无需后处理修复）。消融实验：无修复时基线模型(47% SR)显著优于课程模型(7% SR)；添加部分接口修复后两者均提升至53% SR。SVHN跨数据集迁移中直接生成仅27% SR，准确率60.5%。

Conclusion: 接口修复与课程调度分别针对不同故障模式——合并级权重漂移渐进擦除评估器接口先验，两者互补而非替代；跨数据集迁移难度上升与unq-family锚点架构的合成复杂性一致。

Abstract: Introduce a MinHash-based similarity scheduling framework that constructs a progressive curriculum over neural architecture code for LLM-based neural architecture search (NAS). Using 128-permutation MinHash signatures over normalised 7-gram source code shingles, we partition the reference pool into similarity bands and present them in increasing architectural heterogeneity, with the best LoRA adapter from each stage merged cumulatively into the backbone. We evaluate the framework on OlympicCoder-7B within the LEMUR benchmark on CIFAR-10 image classification, generating N =15 candidate architectures per epoch across six progressive fine-tuning steps. The curriculum achieves 60% peak success rate at the high-similarity level without post-processing repair. A 2*2 ablation at the most diverse level curriculum versus base model, with versus without partial interface repair reveals that without repair the base model (47% peak SR) substantially outperforms the curriculum model (7% SR), while adding partial repair brings both to 53% SR. This pattern is consistent with merge-level weight drift progressively erasing evaluator-interface priors, and suggests that interface repair and curriculum scheduling target distinct failure modes. We further report a cross-dataset transfer observation on SVHN, where direct base-model generation without curriculum warmup yields 27% peak SR at substantially lower accuracy (60.5%) than the CIFAR-10 equivalent, consistent with the increased synthesis difficulty of the unq-family anchor architecture.

</details>


### [171] [Backbone-Agnostic Perturbation-Induced Uncertainty Learning for End-to-End Real-World Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11623)
*Bingcai Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BPUL，一种即插即用的不确定性学习框架，用于端到端真实世界图像去雾。它通过学习扰动不确定性调制器（LPUM）、先验引导不确定性重建模块（PURM）和双空间域分散对比损失（D³CL），在仅增加极小推理开销的情况下，显著提升多种主流去雾骨干网络的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端去雾网络将去雾视为确定性映射，未能充分探索退化特征、雾先验和跨域负样本中隐藏的不确定性；真实世界雾霾退化的空间非均匀性、光照依赖性和物理模糊性使得确定性方法难以应对。

Method: 1) LPUM：通过可重参数化随机扰动估计通道和空间维度的特征敏感性；2) PURM：利用透射率和大气光先验从复原结果重建雾图，约束退化一致性；3) D³CL：在复原和重建双空间中，用真实和合成负样本进行对比学习正则化。推理时仅保留LPUM。

Result: 在五个真实世界配对去雾基准上，BPUL一致提升了多种代表性骨干网络的去雾性能。

Conclusion: BPUL是一种高效且通用的不确定性学习框架，能显著提升已有去雾网络在真实场景下的表现，且推理开销极小。

Abstract: Real-world paired image dehazing remains challenging because haze degradation is spatially non-uniform, illumination-dependent, and physically ambiguous even when haze-free references are available. Existing end-to-end restoration networks usually formulate dehazing as a deterministic mapping from a hazy observation to a clean target, leaving the uncertainty hidden in degraded features, haze priors, and cross-domain negative samples insufficiently explored. In this paper, we propose Backbone-Agnostic Perturbation-Induced Uncertainty Learning (BPUL), a plug-and-play uncertainty learning framework for end-to-end real-world image dehazing. BPUL first introduces a Learnable Perturbation-induced Uncertainty Modulator (LPUM) that estimates channel-wise and spatial-wise feature sensitivity through reparameterized stochastic perturbations. It then develops a Prior-informed Uncertainty-guided Reconstruction Module (PURM), which exploits transmission and atmospheric-light priors to reconstruct the hazy observation from the restored result and enforce degradation consistency. Furthermore, we propose a Dual-space Domain-diversified Distribution-aware Contrastive Loss ($D^3$CL) to regularize both clean restoration and hazy reconstruction spaces with real-world and synthetic negatives. Experiments on five real-world paired benchmarks show that BPUL consistently improves multiple representative backbones. Since only LPUM is retained during inference while PURM and $D^3$CL are used as training-time constraints, BPUL brings substantial restoration gains with only marginal additional inference overhead.

</details>


### [172] [Motion4Motion: Motion Transfer Across Subjects at Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11644)
*Ling-Hao Chen,Zixin Yin,Duomin Wang,Xianfang Zeng,Gang Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Motion4Motion，一种无需训练的跨物种运动迁移框架，通过建模运动流而非骨架，实现不同物种间的视频运动迁移，效果显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于骨架的运动迁移方法依赖预定义人体骨架结构和骨架条件模型训练，难以泛化到不同物种的多样化角色，且标注数据有限，限制了大规模训练。

Method: 提出Motion4Motion，跳出基于骨架的运动迁移框架，通过建模视频中角色的运动流（motion flow）而非骨架，实现无需训练的跨物种运动迁移。

Result: 大量实验结果表明所提方法在性能上显著优于基线方法，并展示了新颖的应用场景。

Conclusion: Motion4Motion通过运动流建模成功解决了基于骨架方法在跨物种迁移中的局限性，为多样化角色动画提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This work explores the motion transfer from one video to another, which is crucial in animation for diverse characters. Previously, video motion transfer has been largely explored between human and human-like characters, enabling a lot of applications in digital creation. However, these approaches encounter a main limitation. Specifically, related technical pipelines heavily rely on a predefined human skeleton structure and accordingly require skeleton-conditional model training. On the one hand, these methods are difficult to generalize to diverse characters, such as animals from different species, while preserving their unique motion styles. On the other hand, labeled data in diverse skeletons is limited, which additionally restricts the large-scale training for the task. In this paper, we jump out of the skeleton-based motion transfer framework and propose a training-free motion transfer framework, named Motion4Motion. Motion4Motionmodels the motion flow of the character in a video instead of skeletons, which makes motion transfer across species easier. Extensive experimental results and novel applications show our methods outperform baselines impressively. Project page is available at https://lhchen.top/Motion4Motion.

</details>


### [173] [Event-RGB Adaptive Tracking for Nighttime Highway Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11646)
*Haidong Wang,Hengxing Cai,Wanlei Li,Xiaogang Xiong,Renxin Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对高速公路夜间环境RGB感知性能严重退化的问题，提出联合事件-RGB自适应跟踪框架JEAT，通过自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波动态融合异步事件流与RGB帧，并构建了大规模合成数据集SEHN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速公路夜间缺乏人工照明且车速快，导致RGB相机出现运动模糊、曝光不足和信噪比差等问题，严重损害了基于RGB感知系统的可靠性，现有多传感器跟踪器受限于刚性硬编码优先级，无法灵活应对环境变化。

Method: 提出JEAT框架：1）将异步事件流与RGB帧融合为统一联合数据关联优化；2）采用自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波，通过NIS统计量连续估计测量噪声，动态加权融合两种模态；3）利用CARLA生成包含白天、夜间、夜间无人工照明等多样化条件的SEHN数据集。

Result: 在多种高速公路环境条件下验证了JEAT框架的有效性，SEHN数据集为多模态融合研究提供了基准；代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: JEAT通过自适应模态权重调节，在黑暗或高速运动条件下优先利用事件流，在明亮或静态条件下利用RGB帧，有效解决了高速公路夜间感知难题，为智能交通系统提供了更鲁棒的多模态跟踪方案。

Abstract: Intelligent Transportation Systems deployed on highways predominantly rely on conventional RGB cameras for traffic perception and vehicle tracking. However, highway environments present unique challenges: the absence of artificial lighting infrastructure, combined with high vehicle velocities, results in severely degraded perception performance under low-light conditions. Specifically, nighttime scenarios suffer from motion blur, insufficient exposure, and poor signal-to-noise ratios, which catastrophically impair the reliability of RGB-based sensing systems. To address these limitations, we propose a novel Joint Event-RGB Adaptive Tracking (JEAT) framework. Unlike existing multi-sensor trackers constrained by rigid, hard-coded prioritization, JEAT merges asynchronous event streams and RGB frames into a unified joint data association optimization. By employing an Adaptive Extended Kalman Filter to continuously estimate measurement noise via NIS statistics, the framework dynamically weights and fuses both modalities, optimally harnessing event streams during dark or high-speed motion while leveraging RGB frames under bright or static conditions. Furthermore, given the absence of publicly available datasets tailored for event-based highway perception with diverse environmental conditions, we present SEHN, a large-scale synthetic dataset generated using the CARLA simulator. Our dataset encompasses diverse environmental conditions (daytime, nighttime, nighttime with out artificial lighting) and varying traffic densities, providing synchronized RGB imagery and event streams to facilitate multi-modal fusion research. Our code and datasets will be available at https://github.com/haidongwang96/SEHN.

</details>


### [174] [Feature-Space Guided Diffusion for Realistic Ultrasound Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11655)
*Marina Domínguez,Nélida Mirabet-Herranz,Valery Naranjo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种训练无关的采样策略FSCG（特征空间候选引导），通过局部k-NN特征校正和特征空间能量选择，显著缩小了条件扩散模型生成的医学超声图像与真实图像之间的表征差距，在三个数据集上FID64平均降低56%、FID192降低57%、最近邻特征距离降低47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准条件扩散模型生成的超声图像在解剖结构上合理，但B模式超声图像受散斑纹理、组织对比度和衰减等采集依赖特性影响，现有方法仅关注解剖掩码条件化，导致生成图像与真实图像在特征空间中存在明显差距。

Method: 提出训练无关的采样策略FSCG，在采样时进行局部k-NN特征校正，并从多个随机候选样本中根据特征空间能量选择最优结果，同时利用冻结的超声基础模型来衡量和缩小生成图像与真实域之间的差距。

Result: 在三个数据集上，相比标准条件扩散采样，FSCG使FID64平均降低56%、FID192平均降低57%、最近邻特征距离平均降低47%，且优于其他推理时引导基线。

Conclusion: 域感知的特征表示可以揭示并减少医学扩散合成中的真实感差距，无需重新训练生成器，为提升医学超声图像生成质量提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Conditional diffusion models can generate anatomically plausible medical ultrasound (US) images, but anatomical plausibility alone does not ensure realistic B-mode appearance. Most US pipelines adapt standard generative architectures and condition them on anatomical masks, or use guidance mechanisms that reinforce the same anatomical signal. However, B-mode US images are shaped by acquisition-dependent properties such as speckle texture, tissue contrast, and attenuation. Using a frozen US foundation model, we show that standard conditional diffusion baselines remain separated from real images in representation space. In this work, we propose Feature-Space Candidate Guidance (FSCG), a training-free sampling strategy to reduce this gap. At sampling time, FSCG applies local k-NN feature correction and selects the best of multiple stochastic candidates according to their feature-space energy. In this way, the mask defines the anatomy, while FSCG steers samples toward the real US domain. Across three different datasets, FSCG reduces average FID64 by 56\%, FID192 by 57\%, and nearest-neighbour feature distance by 47\% over standard conditional diffusion sampling, outperforming alternative inference-time guidance baselines. The results suggest that domain-aware feature representations can reveal and reduce realism gaps in medical diffusion synthesis without retraining the generator. Our code is available at https://github.com/marinadominguez/FSCG.

</details>


### [175] [ABot-3DWorld 0: A Universal World Model to Explore Any 3D Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11673)
*Mingchao Sun,Luyang Tang,Yu Liu,Xu Yan,Zhan Li,Yunwei Zhang,Fei Yu,Zengye Ge,Yumin Liu,Jiacheng Zhang,Yongchang Zhang,Jiawei Zhang,Zhicheng Liu,Zhongxu Sun,Tianjian Ouyang,Wenzheng Chen,Shixing Yang,Nianfei Fan,Guodong Sun,Huan Li,Zheng Zhou,Yongze Li,Yingliang Peng,Mengmeng Du,Yuan Liu,Haozhe Shi,Chunnuo Gong,Chengzhen Yu,Chunxue Jia,Yang Liu,Shiying Zeng,Junnan Lai,Hang Zhang,Ning Guo,Baoquan Chen,Mu Xu,Hongyu Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ABot-3DWorld 0 是一个通用多模态3D世界模型，通过统一的空间生成基元（SGP）将文本、图像和视频输入转换为高保真、可探索的3D世界，在丰富多模态输入下达到了开源方法的SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D内容创建方法缺乏统一的多模态输入处理能力，难以高效地将文本、图像和视频等多种输入形式转化为高保真、可探索的3D世界，且缺乏大规模地理锚定的空间探索能力。

Method: 提出统一的空间生成基元（SGP，由高质量全景图和空间点云组成）；多模态输入首先提升为该基元；通过3D一致的全景视频生成器沿规划轨迹探索基元；最后用全景视频重建引擎将生成视频转换为干净的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）世界。覆盖两种输入模式：丰富输入通过几何精确恢复映射到观察场景，单图像或句子通过生成方式创造性完成。

Result: 在开源方法中达到SOTA性能，在丰富多模态输入下场景保真度优于Marble。

Conclusion: ABot-3DWorld 0提供了一个低门槛的通用3D内容创建引擎，支持将生成世界锚定到地理兴趣点，实现消费级的地图原生空间探索。

Abstract: We present ABot-3DWorld 0, a universal multimodal 3D world model that turns text, image, and video inputs into high-fidelity, explorable 3D worlds. At the heart of our framework is a unified Spatial Generative Primitive (SGP), a compact tuple of a high-quality panorama and a spatial point cloud that delivers an efficient description of any 3D space. Multimodal inputs are first lifted into this primitive; a 3D-consistent panoramic video generator then explores the primitive along a planned trajectory; finally, our panoramic video reconstruction engine converts the generated video into a clean, photorealistic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) world. This pipeline covers two regimes: rich inputs (multi-view sets, casual video) are lifted into the SGP through a geometry-rigorous recovery that mirrors the observed scene, while a single image or sentence is completed generatively into a creative world. The result is one low-barrier engine for general 3D content creation that further anchors generated worlds to geographic points of interest, enabling map-native spatial exploration at consumer scale. Experiments show that ABot-3DWorld 0 sets the state of the art among open-source methods and demonstrates stronger scene fidelity than Marble under rich multimodal inputs.

</details>


### [176] [Illuminant-Adaptive 3D Lookup Tables for Camera Color Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11681)
*Claudio Rota,Luca Cogo,Simone Bianco,Raimondo Schettini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于照明自适应三维查找表（C²LUT）的相机颜色校正框架，通过结合色度感知照明表示和非线性颜色变换，并采用Tucker张量分解降低计算开销，在多个相机和数据集上实现了比现有方法更优的颜色校正性能（ΔE₀₀降低20%，角度误差降低18%），同时兼容现代相机硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于相机传感器响应与CIE XYZ色彩空间之间存在非线性关系，且高色度、光谱复杂的LED照明日益增多，准确的颜色校正仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出基于照明自适应三维查找表（C²LUT）的颜色校正框架，结合色度感知照明表示与非线性颜色变换，并采用Tucker张量分解表示LUT以降低计算需求。

Result: 在多个相机、照明、反射率数据集和真实拍摄图像上，CIE ΔE₀₀降低高达20%，角度误差降低高达18%。

Conclusion: 所提方法相比现有颜色校正方法有一致改进，且与现有相机硬件约束兼容。

Abstract: Color correction is a key component of camera image signal processing (ISP) pipelines, encompassing illuminant discounting and colorimetric mapping of device-dependent sensor responses to device-independent color spaces, such as CIE XYZ. Despite extensive research, accurate color correction remains challenging due to the non-linear relationship between camera sensor responses and CIE XYZ color space, as well as to the increasing presence of highly chromatic and spectrally complex LED illuminants. We propose a color correction framework based on illuminant-adaptive three-dimensional lookup tables (LUTs), which we call Color Correction LUT (C$^2$LUT). Our method combines a chromaticity-aware illuminant representation with a non-linear color transformation, enabling accurate correction under illuminants spanning a wide range of chromaticities and spectral complexities. We employ Tucker tensor decomposition to represent the LUTs, ensuring that computational requirements remain sufficiently low for deployment in camera ISPs. In addition, we introduce a large-scale illuminants dataset comprising 1,473 spectral power distributions, with different chromaticities and spectral profiles. Experiments across multiple cameras, illuminants, reflectance datasets, and real captured images demonstrate consistent improvements over existing methods for color correction, reducing CIE $ΔE_{00}$ by up to 20% and angular error by up to 18% while remaining compatible with modern camera hardware constraints. Code and datasets are available at https://github.com/claudiom4sir/C2LUT.

</details>


### [177] [SVI360: Spherical Video Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11710)
*Le-Kim Nguyen,Renato Martins,Pascal Vasseur,Cedric Demonceaux*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SVI360，一种双分支框架，通过结合原始帧和旋转正交视图来增强光流等变性，从而解决球面视频插值中的极区畸变问题，在四个公开基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频插值方法难以处理球面视频靠近极点的严重畸变，不适用于全景视频。

Method: 提出SVI360双分支框架，结合图像帧及其旋转正交视图，通过增强原始视图与正交视图之间光流位移的等变性来改进中间帧预测。

Result: 在四个公开基准上，插值质量和光流精度均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SVI360有效解决了球面视频插值的极区畸变问题，代码和预训练模型已开源。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of omnidirectional video interpolation, which plays an essential role in applications such as virtual reality and immersive video enhancement. Existing video interpolation methods are not well-suited for spherical videos, as they have difficulty handling severe distortions close to the poles. To address this issue, we propose SVI360, a dual-branch framework that combines the image frame and its rotated orthogonal view to deal with these distortions. The core methodological aspect of the approach is to reinforce equivariance of the flow displacements between the original and orthogonal views to improve intermediate frame prediction. Experiments show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in interpolation quality while maintaining accurate optical flow in four different public benchmarks. Code and pre-trained models are available at: https://icb-vision-ai.github.io/video360_interpolation/

</details>


### [178] [GFR-SAM: Training-Free Referring Camouflaged Object Segmentation via Cross-Image Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11732)
*Yilong Yang,Jianxin Tian,Shengchuan Zhang,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GFR-SAM提出了一种“生成-过滤-细化”的三阶段无训练框架，通过上下文示例分割、区域-全局对比过滤和几何-语义细化，在Ref-COD任务上超越现有无训练方法8.7% Fβ^w，并与有监督方法竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Ref-COD方法中，监督式方法需要大量标注，而基于稀疏点提示的无训练方法对定位误差敏感，亟待一种鲁棒且无需微调的无训练范式。

Method: 1) 上下文示例引导分割：突破SAM3的帧内限制，利用跨图像视觉示例生成候选掩码；2) 区域-全局对比过滤模块：基于DINOv3原型对齐对候选掩码排序，抑制背景干扰；3) 几何-语义细化模块：联合边界框和文本提示恢复精细边界并提升实例召回。

Result: 在R2C7K基准上，GFR-SAM在加权F-measure上超越现有无训练方法8.7%，与有监督方法性能相当。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了SAM3在跨图像上下文提示方面的潜力，建立了一种鲁棒的无训练范式，弥合了通用基础模型与专业标注密集型感知任务之间的差距，无需任务特定微调。

Abstract: Referring Camouflaged Object Detection (Ref-COD) requires segmenting hidden targets guided by reference cues. While supervised methods are annotation-heavy and training-free approaches via sparse point-prompting are sensitive to localization errors, we propose GFR-SAM, a robust three-stage training-free framework. GFR-SAM shifts the paradigm from fragile point-matching to a "Generate-Filter-Refine" pipeline. First, we introduce In-Context Exemplar-guided Segmentation, empowering SAM3 with cross-image inference to generate candidate masks via holistic visual exemplars, bypassing its native intra-image constraints. Second, a Region-Global Contrastive Filtering module ranks candidates through DINOv3-based prototypical alignment, effectively suppressing background distractors. Finally, a Geometric-Semantic Refinement module synergizes bounding box and text prompts to recover fine-grained boundaries and enhance instance recall. Evaluated on the R2C7K benchmark, GFR-SAM outperforms existing training-free methods by 8.7\% in weighted F-measure ($F_β^w$) and competes with supervised state-of-the-art counterparts. Ultimately, this work underscores the potential of unlocking SAM3's latent capability for cross-image In-Context prompting, establishing a robust, training-free paradigm that effectively bridges the gap between general-purpose foundation models and specialized, label-intensive perception tasks without the need for task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [179] [Higher-Order Cell Tracking Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11754)
*Jordão Bragantini,Ilan Theodoro,Loïc A. Royer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOCT是一种基于边的细胞跟踪架构，通过高阶图变换器和3D几何先验解决候选跟踪图中节点嵌入纠缠和边标签随机性问题，在细胞追踪挑战赛和细菌分裂基准上达到SOTA，且在人工回环中仅用400个标注即可减少59%误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在候选跟踪图中忽略两个结构障碍：细胞分裂导致不同谱系路径在节点嵌入空间纠缠；共享节点的边具有近乎随机的标签一致性，导致候选图拓扑对图神经网络无有用信息。
