Abstract: This paper presents gradient-enhanced non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm II (G-NSGA-II) to address the challenges of local optima and solution non-uniqueness in the complex permittivity extraction problem for the first time. This adaptive hybrid algorithm integrates the global exploration capability of NSGA-II with gradient-based local refinement, triggered by a population-stagnation detection mechanism. Furthermore, multi-dimensional constraints are incorporated by jointly optimizing transmission and reflection coefficients across multiple sample thicknesses. Experimental validation conducted on six typical polymers in the 20--40 GHz band demonstrates that the retrieved relative permittivity and thicknesses are in high agreement with literature values and physical measurements. Compared to standard heuristic and gradient-based algorithms, the proposed G-NSGA-II reduces the number of generations required for convergence by approximately 50\%. This significant improvement in speed, combined with enhanced robustness, provides a highly reliable and efficient solution for broadband dielectric characterization in architectural and electromagnetic engineering. The simple measurement method and the proposed efficient algorithm allow for a rapid evalutaion of wireless performance within indoor environments. This approach serves as a valuable tool for optimizing existing wireless layouts and improving network performance.

</details>


### [282] [A data-driven solving strategy based on a greedy optimization algorithm for the analysis of nonlinear beam structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10401)
*Thi-Hoa Nguyen,Bruno A. Roccia,Cristian G. Gebhardt*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In the last decade, data-driven computational mechanics (DDCM) has emerged as a novel paradigm in computational mechanics, enabling the direct use of constitutive data - such as stress-strain pairs obtained from experiments, without relying on ad-hoc material models and thereby avoiding information loss. In this work, we extend our data-driven solving strategy GO-ADM, which combines a greedy optimization algorithm with the alternating direction method (ADM), to the structural analysis of geometrically exact beams formulated using director-based kinematics. We discuss a data initialization strategy for nonlinear systems based on a conventional finite element analysis of the same structure using a prescribed constitutive model. The resulting discrete stress and strain fields, possibly obtained under multiple loading scenarios, may also be employed as artificial datasets for the subsequent data-driven computations. Furthermore, we investigate the thermomechanical consistency of both the dataset and the discrete solution, and propose a weak enforcement of this consistency in the latter via a penalty approach. Numerical examples involving single- and multi-member structures demonstrate that the proposed penalty term leads to thermomechanically consistent discrete stress and strain fields. Moreover, for the studied examples, the solving strategy GO-ADM yields a generally improved approximation of the globally optimal solution compared to the standard ADM-based direct solver.

</details>


### [283] [Data-efficient continuous conditional denoising diffusion model for microstructure generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10429)
*Tarakram Ramgopal,Gowtham Nimmal Haribabu,Hussein Farahani,Cornelis Bos,Siddhant Kumar*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种连续条件去噪扩散模型，结合邻域损失训练策略，能够在少量数据条件下高效生成受工艺参数（如锰含量）调控的微观结构图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微观结构模拟方法（如元胞自动机、相场法）计算瓶颈严重，难以用于高通量和按需工艺优化；现有生成式扩散模型虽有效，但处理连续工艺条件时需要大量训练数据，数据效率低下。

Method: 采用连续条件去噪扩散模型，通过前向加噪和反向去噪学习微观结构统计模式；提出邻域损失（vicinal-loss）训练策略，将数据稀疏区域的工艺条件与邻近条件关联，结合无分类器引导和去噪扩散隐式采样，实现数据高效的连续条件生成。

Result: 在低合金钢微观结构生成任务中，模型以锰含量为条件，成功生成了具有代表性的微观结构，在相形态、晶粒尺寸分布、相分数和界面面积分布等关键物理特征上与真实数据匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作为材料微观结构的高效工艺设计与优化开辟了新路径，尤其适用于连续工艺参数场景下的数据效率提升。

Abstract: Traditional computational models, such as cellular automata and phase-field methods, are effective for simulating microstructural evolution but often face computational bottlenecks, limiting their application in high-throughput and on-demand process optimization. Generative machine learning approaches, such as denoising diffusion models, have emerged as powerful tools for surrogate modeling of process-structure maps, specifically producing representative microstructures conditioned on process parameters. However, they often require large amounts of data for training, particularly when process conditions are continuous rather than discrete categorical variables. To address this, we present a continuous conditional denoising diffusion model for generating microstructures conditioned on processing parameters. Trained on a compact dataset of process-microstructure pairs, this framework first adds noise to microstructure images and then trains a neural network to progressively remove the noise, learning the underlying statistical patterns of the microstructure. To address data inefficiencies associated with continuously valued process conditions, we propose a vicinal-loss training strategy that associates process conditions in data-sparse regions with nearby conditions in the dataset. Combined with classifier-free guidance and denoising diffusion implicit sampling, this approach enables data-efficient continuous conditional generation of microstructures compared to classical denoising diffusion models. The model successfully generates representative microstructures for low-carbon steel conditioned on manganese composition, matching key physical features such as phase and grain morphology, grain size distribution, phase fraction, and interfacial area distribution. More generally, this approach opens avenues for efficient process design and optimization of materials and their microstructures.

</details>


### [284] [Integrating Physics-Informed Neural Networks and 3D Vascular Geometry Learning for Cerebral Aneurysm Detection and Multimodal Rupture-Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10530)
*Eshan Vipuil,Xianqi Li*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化框架，结合3D血管几何学习、物理信息驱动的血流动力学描述符和临床变量，用于脑动脉瘤检测和破裂风险预测。动脉瘤检测器达到AUROC 0.959、AUPRC 0.859；破裂风险预测采用固定70/30后期融合策略达到AUROC 0.827、AUPRC 0.732，显著优于对比模型。OSI分布、动脉瘤位置、径向几何和TAWSS描述符是破裂风险判别的重要特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前脑动脉瘤评估依赖人工解读影像和临床风险因素，难以将血管形态、血流信息和患者变量整合为统一的定量模型，亟需一种多模态、可量化的分析方法。

Method: 构建模块化框架：(1) 基于PointNeXt的检测器从血管点云识别动脉瘤；(2) 非稳态物理信息神经网络生成几何约束下的血流动力学描述符（压力、速度、WSS、TAWSS、OSI、相对停留时间）；(3) 多模态模型融合血管形态、物理信息血流描述符和临床变量预测破裂风险。

Result: 动脉瘤检测器AUROC 0.959、AUPRC 0.859；破裂风险预测固定70/30后期融合策略AUROC 0.827、AUPRC 0.732，经Holm校正后显著优于所有对比模型（调整后p<0.05）。

Conclusion: 该多模态定量策略能够有效实现病例特定的动脉瘤评估，OSI分布、动脉瘤位置、径向几何和TAWSS描述符是破裂风险判别的重要特征。

Abstract: Cerebral aneurysms are localized dilations of intracranial arteries that may rupture and cause subarachnoid hemorrhage. Current assessment relies on human interpretation of imaging and clinical risk factors, but integrating vascular shape, flow-related information, and patient-level variables into a unified quantitative model remains challenging. This study develops a modular framework for cerebral aneurysm detection and rupture-risk prediction using 3D vascular geometry learning, physics-informed hemodynamic descriptors, and clinical variables. A PointNeXt-based detector first identified aneurysm presence from vascular point clouds. For aneurysm-positive cases, an unsteady physics-informed neural network then generated geometry-conditioned pressure, velocity, wall shear stress (WSS), time averaged WSS, oscillatory shear index (OSI), and relative residence time descriptors under prescribed Navier-Stokes residual and boundary-condition constraints. Multimodal models then integrated vascular morphology, physics-informed hemodynamic descriptors, and clinical variables to produce rupture-risk scores. The aneurysm detector achieved pooled out-of-fold area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.959 and area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) of 0.859. For rupture-risk prediction, fixed 70/30 late fusion achieved the highest performance among evaluated models, with pooled AUROC of 0.827 and AUPRC of 0.732, exceeding all comparison models after Holm-corrected paired DeLong testing (all adjusted p < 0.05). Feature analysis identified OSI distribution, aneurysm location, radial geometry, and TAWSS descriptors as important contributors to cross-sectional rupture-risk discrimination. Together, these results provide a quantitative, multimodal strategy for case-specific aneurysm assessment.

</details>


### [285] [Affordable low-carbon electricity pathways for India under uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10581)
*Guillermo Terrén-Serrano,Ranjit Deshmukh,Srihari Dukkipati,Measrainsey Meng,Shradhey Prasad,Ana Mileva,Ashwin Gambhir*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 该研究构建了印度电力系统脱碳的成本最优路径模型，发现在所有情景下，2030-2050年实际平均系统成本均低于2020年水平，太阳能光伏占总装机容量50%-75%，短时电池储能是关键平衡手段，需求响应可降低成本达10%，表明可再生能源成本下降与需求响应可使印度兼顾电力可负担性与气候目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度计划到2070年实现碳中和，但其电力系统脱碳面临技术成本不确定、电力需求不确定以及高比例风光发电平衡需求等多重挑战，亟需系统性的优化路径规划。

Method: 构建成本最优模型，在替代性技术成本、电力需求预测、清洁能源及碳排放目标等不同情景下，对发电、储能和输电扩容进行优化分析。

Result: 所有情景下2030-2050年实际平均系统成本均低于2020年；太阳能光伏占总装机50%-75%；短时电池储能在净峰值需求时段提供平衡与可靠性；绿氢、抽水蓄能和核电扩容的成本降幅不足2%，而需求响应可降低成本至多10%。

Conclusion: 可再生能源与电池储能成本持续下降，配合需求响应措施，可使印度同时实现电力可负担性与气候目标。

Abstract: To meet its carbon neutrality goal by 2070, India must accelerate the decarbonization of its electricity system. However, uncertainty in technology costs and electricity demand, together with the need to balance high shares of solar and wind generation, makes planning India's electricity transition challenging. Here, we develop and examine cost-optimal pathways for generation, storage, and transmission expansion in India under alternative technology costs, electricity demand projections, and clean energy and carbon-emission targets. Across all scenarios, real average system costs remain lower in all future years (2030-2050) than in 2020, even when carbon emissions decline linearly to 90% below current levels by 2050. Solar PV comprises over half to three-quarters of total installed capacity in most scenarios. Large-scale deployment of short-duration battery storage provides both energy balancing and reliability during net-peak demand hours. Expanding green hydrogen, pumped hydro storage, and nuclear capacity reduces costs by less than 2%, whereas demand response programs reduce costs by up to 10%. Falling renewable energy and battery storage costs along with demand response programs allow India to meet both electricity affordability and climate goals.

</details>


### [286] [HermesHFL: Incentive-Compatible Hierarchical Federated Unlearning for Dynamic LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11528)
*Chenxi Sun,Minghui Liwang,Wusi He,Zhang Liu,Yuhan Su,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour,Xianbin Wang,Yiguang Hong*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出HermesHFL框架，通过神经引导的双层进化优化（Neogen）解决分层联邦遗忘中的客户端动态参与、参数耦合和选择性遗忘问题，在LLM微调中效果优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层联邦遗忘面临分层聚合、动态客户端参与和LLM参数强耦合的挑战，客户端更新的跨阶段传播和退出/重新加入问题使得选择性移除客户端贡献极为困难。

Method: 提出HermesHFL框架：1）构建统一优化问题联合建模客户端参与、边缘关联、激励分配和遗忘；2）开发Neogen神经引导双层进化优化，结合CMA-ES优化连续激励和CHC进化机制处理离散决策；3）引入神经代理加速优化搜索。

Result: 在LLM微调任务上，HermesHFL在模型效用、遗忘效果、收敛稳定性和资源效率方面持续优于最先进基线。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化客户端参与、边缘关联、激励分配和遗忘，结合神经引导双层进化优化，HermesHFL有效解决了分层联邦遗忘在LLM微调中的核心挑战。

Abstract: Hierarchical federated unlearning (HFUL) for large language model (LLM) fine-tuning faces significant challenges due to hierarchical aggregation, dynamic client participation, and strong parameter coupling in LLM adaptation. Selectively removing client contributions is particularly difficult because model updates propagate across multiple aggregation stages while unlearning requests may coincide with client departures and rejoining. To address these issues, we propose **HermesHFL**, a hierarchical federated learning framework that supports selective unlearning, dynamic client participation, and client reintegration for scalable LLM fine-tuning via parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) with LoRA. We formulate a unified optimization problem that jointly models client participation, edge association, incentive allocation, and unlearning under heterogeneous client behaviors. To solve this problem efficiently, we develop **Neogen**, a neural-guided bilevel evolutionary optimization framework that combines CMA-ES for continuous incentive optimization with a CHC-based evolutionary mechanism for discrete participation and association decisions. A neural surrogate further accelerates optimization and improves search efficiency. Extensive experiments on LLM fine-tuning tasks demonstrate that HermesHFL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in model utility, unlearning effectiveness, convergence stability, and resource efficiency.

</details>


### [287] [Model Order Reduction of a Sliding Beam using a Global Basis: Formulation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11794)
*Sebastian Weyrer,Johannes Gerstmayr,Aki Mikkola,Grzegorz Orzechowski*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文针对伸缩结构中滑动梁的模型降阶问题，提出了一种基于快照（模态矩阵）构建全局降阶基的方法，并利用本征正交分解（POD）进行压缩。在带代数约束的多体动力学框架下应用降阶，实现了连续滑块运动。与绝对节点坐标公式（ANCF）的对比验证显示，该方法在保持均方根位移误差低于2%的同时，将计算时间减少了约90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑动梁（如伸缩结构）在模型降阶中面临根本性挑战：固定模态坐标无法捕捉演变中的系统特性，而更新模态基会导致模态坐标含义改变。现有方法难以兼顾精度与效率，亟需一种能够适应系统时变特性的全局降阶策略。

Method: 首先从模态矩阵形式的快照中构建全局降阶基，再通过本征正交分解（POD）进行压缩；在带代数约束的多体动力学框架中应用降阶，实现连续滑块运动；通过绝对节点坐标公式（ANCF）进行验证，并研究快照数量与每快照特征模态数对误差的影响。

Result: 在高柔性梁的时变载荷与滑块运动测试中，全局降阶基使计算时间减少约90%，同时均方根位移误差保持在2%以下；给出了不同快照数量与模态数组合下的误差图。

Conclusion: 所提全局降阶基方法有效解决了滑动梁时变系统特性的降阶难题，在大幅提升计算效率的同时保持了较高精度，适用于柔性伸缩结构的动力学仿真。

Abstract: Model order reduction decreases the dimension of a mechanical system by introducing modal coordinates that retain important dynamic characteristics. Sliding beams, as found in telescopic structures, pose a fundamental challenge. Fixed modal coordinates fail to capture evolving system properties, and updating the modal basis during simulation causes modal coordinates to change meaning. The present work addresses this challenge by constructing a global reduction basis for a sliding beam. The global basis is constructed from snapshots in the form of modal matrices and compressed using proper orthogonal decomposition. Reduction is applied within a constraint multibody formalism with algebraically enforced constraints that permit continuous slider movement. The method is validated against an absolute nodal coordinate formulation of a sliding beam with a sliding joint. Different combinations of snapshot quantity and eigenmodes per snapshot are investigated and an error map is shown. A challenging test case involving a highly flexible beam subjected to time-dependent loading and slider movement demonstrates that the global reduction basis reduces computation time by approximately 90% while keeping the root-mean-square displacement error, introduced by the global reduction, below 2%.

</details>

### [288] [Fundamental market design as a layer of AI-agent alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09702)
*Omar Inverso,Emilio Tuosto,Dragisa Zunic*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This paper argues that AI-agent alignment in markets should not be understood only as a property of agents, but also as a property of the interaction infrastructure in which agents act. In financial markets, this infrastructure is the market core: the rule system that determines how orders enter, interact, match, persist, and stabilize. If this fundamental interaction layer allows or rewards undesired behaviour, then higher-level alignment of agents may be insufficient.
  We propose to view fundamental market design as a layer of AI-agent alignment. Alongside the important work of computational economics in modelling agents, strategies, and learning, we focus on a complementary but more fundamental layer: the formal modelling of the market core itself. Market design, especially at the level of the core mechanism, can benefit from a rigour characteristic of theoretical computer science. This gives a transparent-box model of the market, whose core properties can be formally specified and reasoned about. It also lets us treat the trading venue not as a static order book, but as a computational process combining resident orders with incoming order flow, and ask which computational model, perhaps yet unknown, naturally lies at its core.
  This perspective is especially relevant for markets populated by adaptive or AI agents. Such agents may learn what the mechanism rewards, including speed, delay, liquidity provision, or manipulation. These behaviours are not only properties of individual agents, but may emerge from the agent-mechanism system. We therefore argue that transparent formal models of market cores can support incentive-oriented analysis and the design of mechanisms in which desirable behaviours are structurally favoured and undesirable behaviours are harder to sustain.

</details>


### [289] [How to program a never-losing chess engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09715)
*Fabio Romano*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文基于图论提出了一种用于表示完全信息双人游戏（如象棋、跳棋）的模型，并提供了回溯极大极小算法来寻找完美游戏策略（游戏分辨率）及其变体算法，用于确定永不输（至少达到平局）的策略存在性。文章从理论上证明了这些算法在形式上的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的完全信息双人游戏（如象棋、跳棋）缺乏一个统一的图论模型来形式化表示，且需要从理论上证明存在算法能够确定完美游戏策略及永不输策略的存在性。

Method: 基于图论构建博弈表示模型，提出回溯极大极小算法寻找完美游戏策略，并给出该算法的变体以确定永不输策略的存在性。

Result: 从理论上形式化证明了所提出的回溯极大极小算法及其变体能够确定完美游戏策略和永不输策略的存在性。

Conclusion: 尽管实际计算中算法可能不能在短时间内找到策略，但至少在理论上证明了这种形式化方法的可行性。

Abstract: This article proposes a model, based on graph theory, to represent a variety of two-player games of perfect information, such as chess and checkers. I then provide a backtracking minimax algorithm to find, if it exists, a perfect game strategy (game resolution), and subsequently a variant of that algorithm which is required to determine a weaker condition: the existence of a strategy to never lose (always reach at least a draw).
  Of course, this does not mean that in practice the algorithm can find such a strategy in a short time, but here we are only concerned with formally proving that this is possible, at least theoretically.

</details>


### [290] [Eigenmanifold in Game: Evidence from human continuous strategy game experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09782)
*Wang Zhijian,Yao Qinmei,Shan Lixia,Wang Yijia,Zhang Chuchen*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文通过人类实验数据验证了演化博弈动力学中的一个假设——即连续策略博弈中稳态系统的动态结构由特征流形的线性叠加表征，该假设此前在离散策略博弈中已得到广泛支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证演化博弈动力学中的“特征流形线性叠加假设”在连续策略博弈中是否成立，填补该假设在连续策略博弈中缺乏实证支持的空白。

Method: 使用人类被试的连续策略博弈实验数据，对假设进行统计验证。

Result: 该假设在连续策略博弈中同样得到显著支持。

Conclusion: 特征流形线性叠加假设在离散和连续策略博弈中均成立，具有跨策略空间的普适性。

Abstract: In evolutionary game dynamics, there exists a hypothesis, which states that, the dynamic structure of the game's steady -- state system is characterized by the linear superposition of eigenmanifolds, which depends specifically on the eigenvector structure at the Nash equilibrium and is ultimately governed by the game dynamics equations. This hypotheses has been supported widely in discrete strategy game. In continuous -- strategy game, using experimental data from human -- subject games, this paper finds that the hypothesis is supported in significant, too.

</details>


### [291] [Threshold Dynamics and Correlated Prophet Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09887)
*José Correa,Maximilian Fichtl,Reda Jlibene,Rida Laraki,Vasilis Livanos,Kevin Schewior,Victor Verdugo*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种潜在状态变量Z引发的相关模型下的先知不等式问题。在公共基模型中，单阈值确定性算法竞争比为0.381，随机化可提升至0.4；下界0.41，上界0.475。核心贡献是引入微分方程刻画单阈值算法期望奖励，并给出闭式解。公共尺度模型中，竞争比不超过1/n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先知不等式大多假设输入随机变量独立，限制了其应用范围。本文旨在突破独立性假设，研究由潜在状态变量Z诱导的相关模型下的先知不等式问题。

Method: 1) 提出公共基模型（Z+X_i）和公共尺度模型（Z·X_i）；2) 分析单阈值算法，引入微分方程刻画最坏实例下阈值期望奖励的动态变化，该方程具有闭式解；3) 通过极小极大论证扩展结果；4) 建立更强的下界0.41和上界0.475。

Result: 公共基模型：确定性单阈值算法竞争比0.381，随机化提升至0.4；下界0.41，上界0.475。公共尺度模型：任何算法竞争比不超过1/n。

Conclusion: 单阈值算法在相关输入下无法达到独立输入情形下的1/2竞争比；公共尺度模型的乘法相关性导致极强的不可能性结果。所提出的微分方程分析框架统一了已知的单阈值先知不等式。

Abstract: Prophet inequalities have become a central tool for analyzing the performance of online algorithms. However, most existing results assume that input random variables are independent, which limits their applicability. Motivated by this gap, we study prophet inequalities under two correlation models induced by a latent state of the world variable $Z$. In the common-base model, the algorithm observes the sequence $Z+X_1,\dots,Z+X_n$. We analyze single-threshold algorithms with the constraint that they always accept the final item, guaranteeing a reward of at least $Z$. When $Z$ is chosen adversarially, we characterize the optimal deterministic algorithm of this form, achieving a competitive ratio of $0.381$. We then show that randomizing improves the guarantee to $0.4$. By a minimax argument, the same ratio is achievable when $Z$ is random.
  We depart from standard techniques by establishing a stronger lower bound of $0.41$ and an upper bound of $0.475$, ruling out the possibility that this class of algorithms attains the $1/2$ ratio known for independent inputs. The core technical contribution is a new analytical framework that captures the reward dynamics of single-threshold algorithms. We introduce a differential equation characterizing the expected reward of a threshold in the worst-case instance, parameterized by the distribution of the maximum. This equation admits a closed-form and unifies known single-threshold prophet inequalities, yielding a simple threshold-optimality condition applicable to the common-base model.
  Finally, we study the common-scale model, where inputs take the form $Z\cdot X_1,\dots,Z\cdot X_n$. We show that this minimal multiplicative correlation yields strong impossibility results: no algorithm can achieve a competitive ratio exceeding $1/n$.

</details>


### [292] [Implicit Midpoint Gradient Descent: Fast and Learning rate free convergence for Zero-Sum Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09950)
*Gaoqi Xue,James P. Bailey*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文针对无约束双线性零和博弈，提出了一种基于辛积分方法从连续时间跟随正则化领导者动力学推导出的隐式中点梯度下降规则，证明其具有有界轨道、快速遍历收敛到纳什均衡以及与学习率无关的稳定性保证，实验表明该方法显著优于标准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无约束双线性零和博弈是在线学习、对抗优化和多智能体决策中的基础模型，现有方法难以同时兼具有界轨道、快速遍历收敛和与学习率无关的稳定性。

Method: 通过辛积分方法从连续时间跟随正则化领导者动力学推导出隐式中点梯度下降规则。

Result: 该方法同时具备有界轨道、快速遍历收敛到纳什均衡以及与学习率无关的稳定性保证，计算实验表明显著优于乐观梯度下降和交替梯度下降等标准方法。

Conclusion: 隐式中点梯度下降是首个在无约束双线性零和博弈中同时实现上述属性的传统在线优化方法。

Abstract: We study unconstrained bilinear zero-sum games, a fundamental model in online learning, adversarial optimization, and multi-agent decision-making. We introduce the implicit midpoint gradient descent rule, which we derive from continuous-time follow-the-regularized leader dynamics via symplectic integration methods. We prove that implicit midpoint gradient descent inherits several powerful properties from the continuous-time dynamics, including bounded orbits, fast ergodic convergence to Nash equilibria, and learning-rate-independent stability guarantees. This is the first traditional online optimization approach to simultaneously achieve these properties in unconstrained bilinear zero-sum games. Finally, computational experiments demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms the standard methods, optimistic and alternating gradient descent.

</details>


### [293] [A Knowledge-Based Multi-Agent Framework for Security Control Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09954)
*Carolina Fernández-Martínez,Shuaib Siddiqui,Vanesa Daza*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体影响图（MAID）和非遗憾在线学习的安全决策支持系统（DSS），通过将安全控制选择建模为非零和同时博弈，在7个安全维度上实现最优推荐。实验表明，使用约65%的可实施安全控制即可达到99%的满意度覆盖率（耗时1.2-35.7秒），使用约29%的控制可达73%-77%覆盖率（耗时0.8-13.8秒）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏足够网络安全专业知识的团队难以对本地IT环境进行安全加固，需要一种嵌入专家知识的决策支持系统来辅助用户做出安全控制推荐。

Method: 构建统一数据集（整合信息安全和学术来源），将安全DSS定义为非零和同时博弈，基于多智能体影响图（MAID）建模7个安全维度智能体，采用非遗憾在线学习（no-regret online learning）探索决策空间，以最小化安全资源的过度配置和欠配置。

Result: 使用约65%的可实施安全控制时达到99%满意度覆盖率（1.2-35.7秒）；使用约29%的控制时达到73%-77%覆盖率（0.8-13.8秒）。

Conclusion: 该安全DSS能够在显著减少安全控制数量的前提下实现高覆盖率推荐，验证了其在性能和准确性上的有效性。

Abstract: Hardening IT on-premises environments can be a daunting task for teams without access to adequate cybersecurity expertise. In this regard, Decision Support Systems (DSS) with embedded expert knowledge can assist users by guiding them with security recommendations to meet their objectives. This work proposes a Security DSS that recommends security control sub-families given minimal user requirements indicating coverage of different security dimensions. It leverages a curated, unified dataset from both well-known Information Security (InfoSec) and academic sources. This DSS is defined as a non-zero-sum, simultaneous game that is grounded in a Multi-Agent Influence Diagram (MAID) model and explores the decision space over 7 security dimensions or agents, using no-regret online learning to ultimately find the security control sub-families that best fit the requirements while incurring minimal under- and over-provisioning of security resources. This work was validated in terms of performance and accuracy, among others, for varying dataset sizes. It shows exceptional satisfaction coverage results of 99% when using as little as ~65% of the SW-implementable security controls, running in 1.2-35.7 seconds; and more moderate coverage results of 73%-77% when using ~29% of the controls, resolving in 0.8-13.8 seconds.

</details>


### [294] [Beyond Bayesian Nash: Learning Minimax-Regret Equilibria for Adversarial Team Games under Asymmetric Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09993)
*Naman Aggarwal,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为概率鲁棒最小化遗憾均衡（PR-MRE）的新型均衡概念，结合了极小化最大遗憾推理的分布无关鲁棒性与名义类型分布的概率信息，并通过鲁棒双线性规划及其半定松弛，以及PRMRE-PSRO元求解器，在图结构对抗性团队游戏中实现了对隐藏类型下战略分布偏移的鲁棒策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险中性均衡（如贝叶斯纳什均衡）对分布偏移敏感，而分布鲁棒方法仅在预设模糊集内提供保证，无法应对对手利用隐藏类型进行战略性的概率质量再分配（即欺骗行为）。

Method: 定义PR-MRE均衡概念，将其转化为鲁棒双线性规划问题，推导可处理的半定松弛；在此基础上设计PRMRE-PSRO元求解器，通过深度强化学习最优响应的种群学习来近似求解PR-MRE策略。

Result: 在图结构对抗性团队游戏实验中，PR-MRE相比风险中性均衡解显著提升了跨隐藏类型的 worst-case 性能，在战略分布偏移下展现出更强的鲁棒行为。

Conclusion: PR-MRE通过在高置信度类型子集上最小化最坏情况遗憾，在完全分布无关的保守性与概率信息的利用之间取得了有效平衡，为不对称信息下的对抗性团队博弈提供了更具鲁棒性的均衡求解框架。

Abstract: Adversarial team games (ATGs) with asymmetric information, such as adversarial path-finding, goal search, and reachability games on graphs, require strategies that are robust to hidden opponent types, such as a hidden goal flag, and to deception. Under asymmetric information, deception is seen as strategic shifts in the type distribution such that the omniscient opponent can collude with Nature and condition its play on the observed type. Existing risk-neutral solution concepts, such as Bayesian Nash equilibrium (BNE), are sensitive to distribution shifts, while distributionally robust approaches provide guarantees only within a prescribed ambiguity set. To address these limitations, we introduce Probabilistically Robust Minimax-Regret Equilibrium (PR-MRE), a novel equilibrium concept that combines the distribution-free robustness of minimax-regret reasoning with probabilistic information from a nominal type distribution. PR-MRE minimizes worst-case regret over a high-confidence subset of the type space, providing protection against strategic redistribution of probability mass while avoiding the conservatism of fully distribution-free approaches. We show that, for normal-form Bayesian games, PR-MRE can be formulated as a robust bilinear program and derive a tractable semidefinite relaxation. We then adapt this relaxation into a novel meta-solver within a robust double-oracle framework, PRMRE-PSRO, enabling population-based learning of approximate PR-MRE strategies via deep reinforcement learning best responses. Experiments on graph-structured adversarial team games demonstrate that PR-MRE discovers strategies with substantially improved worst-case performance across hidden types compared to risk-neutral equilibrium solutions, resulting in more robust behavior under strategic distribution shifts.

</details>


### [295] [Optimal Subsidy Bounds for Goods and Chores: One Dollar Each Suffices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10089)
*Xinhang Lu,Simon Mackenzie,Mashbat Suzuki*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究不可分割物品（兼具 goods 和 chores）在加法效用下的公平分配，证明每人最多补贴1美元（总计最多n-1美元）即可保证存在无嫉妒分配，且该界紧致，并可多项式时间计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无嫉妒分配在不可分割物品场景下通常不存在，引入可分割的货币作为补偿手段时，需要探索保证无嫉妒存在所需的最小补贴额度。

Method: 假设每个物品的效用或负效用的绝对值不超过1，通过构造性算法证明每人最多补贴1美元即可实现无嫉妒，并证明该上界是紧的。

Result: 每人最多需要1美元补贴（总补贴最多n-1美元）即可保证存在无嫉妒分配，且该界不可改进，同时可在多项式时间内计算出这样的分配。

Conclusion: 给出了在混合 goods 和 chores 场景下通过有限货币补贴实现无嫉妒分配的最优上界和高效算法。

Abstract: We study the fair allocation of $m$ indivisible items to $n$ agents with additive utilities. In our setting, each indivisible item may be a good, yielding non-negative utility to some agents, or a chore, yielding negative utility to others. Whilst envy-free allocations may not exist in the indivisible-items setting, envy-freeness can be achieved if some amount of divisible good (i.e., \emph{money}) is introduced. When each item's utility or disutility is bounded by one, we show that a subsidy of at most one dollar per agent suffices to guarantee the existence of an envy-free allocation, and that this bound is tight. Moreover, such an allocation can be computed in polynomial time. Since at least one agent need not receive any subsidy, our results imply that a total subsidy of at most $n-1$ dollars suffices to ensure envy-freeness.

</details>


### [296] [Best-of-Both-Worlds Fairness for Mixed Goods and Chores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10232)
*Haris Aziz,Xiaolin Bu,Xinhang Lu,Simon Mackenzie,Mashbat Suzuki,Biaoshuai Tao,Toby Walsh*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究混合物品（既有商品也有苦差）的公平分配问题，提出了在事前无嫉妒（ex-ante EF）和事后至多一件物品无嫉妒（ex-post EF1）之间实现同时满足的随机分配机制。通过引入概率型的Hall矩阵分解方法，结合连续极小极大对偶（Sion定理）和偏置流网络，解决了商品与苦差混合分配的核心技术难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平分配研究大多假设物品全部为商品（非负效用）或全部为苦差（非正效用），但在实际场景中物品对不同代理人可能同时具有正负效用。本文旨在填补混合物品公平分配的理论空白，并追求"两全其美"（best-of-both-worlds）视角下事前公平与事后公平的兼容。

Method: 提出一种新型的概率型霍尔矩阵分解（probabilistic Hall-type matrix decomposition），将商品和苦差的分数分配进行关联。先通过Sion极小极大定理建立连续对偶结构，再结合精心设计的偏置流网络（biased flow networks）求解该分解问题，从而构造出满足事前EF且随机分配支撑在事后EF1分配上的机制。

Result: 证明了在混合物品（商品与苦差共存）场景下，可以同时实现事前无嫉妒（ex-ante EF）和事后至多一件物品无嫉妒（ex-post EF1）。这一结果为混合物品公平分配提供了理论保证。

Conclusion: 本文首次在混合物品（商品+苦差）的公平分配中实现了事前EF与事后EF1的同时满足，拓展了公平分配理论的应用边界。所提出的概率型霍尔矩阵分解及偏置流网络方法为解决此类问题提供了新的技术工具。

Abstract: We study the fundamental problem of fairly dividing indivisible items among agents with additive utilities. In our model, an item can be a good yielding non-negative utilities to some agents and simultaneously a chore yielding negative utilities to others. We take the best-of-both-worlds perspective and our goal is to construct a randomized allocation that is exactly fair ex ante while also being supported on ex post approximately fair allocations. The fairness notions examined in this paper are envy-freeness (EF) and its well-known relaxation envy-freeness up to one item (EF1). Our main result is that ex-ante EF and ex-post EF1 can be achieved simultaneously. To achieve this, we introduce a novel probabilistic Hall-type matrix decomposition that intricately correlates the fractional assignments of goods and chores. We resolve this decomposition problem by combining continuous minimax duality -- via Sion's minimax theorem -- with carefully designed biased flow networks.

</details>


### [297] [Double elimination formats for a 64-team FIFA World Cup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10422)
*César Rennó-Costa*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了64支球队参加世界杯的赛制设计，发现双败淘汰制相比传统小组赛+淘汰赛制能消除无意义比赛并增加高质量对决，但会带来赛程复杂和比赛场次不均衡等问题，建议大型赛事应转向非传统赛制设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FIFA世界杯扩军至48队后，进一步扩大至64队的可能性引发了讨论。传统赛制在规模扩大后会加剧结构性缺陷，如增加缺乏竞争意义的比赛场次、降低顶级强队之间的对决概率，因此需要探索替代性赛制方案。

Method: 分析双败淘汰制在64队大型赛事中的应用，从竞争公平性、比赛质量和赛程可行性三个维度对提出的赛制方案进行评估。

Result: 双败淘汰制能够消除数学上无意义的比赛，显著增加高水平对决的频率。但该赛制带来更高的赛程复杂度以及各队比赛场次的不对称分布，需要特定的后勤调整来应对。

Conclusion: 大型体育赛事的持续扩军需要从范式层面转向非传统赛制设计，以保持比赛的整体竞争完整性。

Abstract: The recent expansion of the FIFA World Cup to 48 teams has prompted discussions regarding a potential further increase to a 64-team format. Scaling the traditional tournament architecture (a round-robin group stage followed by a knockout phase) to 64 teams exacerbates existing structural flaws, notably increasing the frequency of matches lacking competitive relevance and reducing the probability of fixtures between top-ranked contenders. This paper investigates alternative tournament designs by analyzing double-elimination structures for a 64-team mega-event. We evaluate the proposed formats based on competitive fairness, match quality, and scheduling feasibility. Our analysis demonstrates that a double-elimination format eliminates mathematically irrelevant matches and significantly increases the frequency of high-profile games. However, these benefits introduce complex operational constraints, including heightened scheduling complexity and an asymmetric distribution of matches per team, which require specific logistical adjustments. Ultimately, our findings suggest that the continuous scaling of mega-sporting events necessitates a paradigm shift toward non-traditional tournament designs to preserve competitive integrity.

</details>


### [298] [Which Wallpaper Groups Arise from Tiled Games?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10552)
*Matthew Fried*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文证明所有17种壁纸群均可作为双矩阵博弈的对称群实现，通过将博弈的支撑复形平铺于平面并施加受控边界规则构造，并提供了两种计算验证途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究离散对称群能否从策略互动中自然产生，为博弈论与晶体学（空间对称群）建立形式化联系。

Method: 将双矩阵博弈的支撑复形拷贝平铺于平面，通过受控边界规则连接，构造显式生成元（机器验证的图自同构），利用精确有理算术的晶体学识别器分类，并在GAP中交叉验证。通过三条引理将经典symmorphic/non-symmorphic区分转化为晶格分类。

Result: 所有17种壁纸群均实现为博弈自同构群；13种symmorphic群在平移包含完整平铺晶格时实现，4种non-symmorphic群以平移晶格指数恰好2实现；图轨道上商覆盖精确恢复原博弈；博弈轨道上检测重复策略覆盖为线性时间，平铺关联均衡系统维度精确为r(d-q)+q且不可扩张；多矩阵覆盖将对称性原样继承。

Conclusion: 建立了博弈对称性与二维晶体学对称群之间的完整对应关系，壁纸群（包括滑移反射）均可作为真正的博弈自同构群出现。

Abstract: Which discrete symmetry groups can arise from strategic interaction? We tile the plane with copies of a bimatrix game's support complex, joined by controlled boundary rules, and show that all seventeen wallpaper groups act on the resulting covers: explicit generators, each a machine-verified graph automorphism, every realization certified as the exact toroidal quotient, with types identified by a crystallographic recognizer in exact rational arithmetic and cross-validated in GAP. A three-line lemma turns the classical symmorphic/non-symmorphic distinction into a lattice classification: realizations whose translations contain the full tile lattice exist precisely for the thirteen symmorphic groups, and the four non-symmorphic groups are realized at translation-lattice index exactly two, the minimum possible: the tile is the glide's half-step.
  Two computational tracks accompany the construction. On the graph track, quotienting a straight cover by its translations recovers the tile exactly, $\beq(M/\calT)=\beq(K)$, and swap boundaries add exactly $\binom m2$, independent of payoffs and of cover size. On the game track, detecting a duplicated-strategy cover is a linear-time payoff scan, one tile solution folds to a full translation orbit of cover equilibria, and the tiled correlated-equilibrium system has dimension exactly $r(d-q)+q$, with expansion impossible. The polymatrix cover then carries the symmetry outright: every wallpaper action, glides included, is a group of genuine game automorphisms, equilibria collapse along any symmetry subgroup to a folded fixed-point problem, and a decorated refinement has game automorphism group exactly the toroidal wallpaper group.

</details>


### [299] [Representation theorems for actual and alpha powers over general concurrent game frames without assuming independence of agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10567)
*Zixuan Chen,Fengkui Ju,Thomas Agotnes*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了并发博弈框架中联盟权力的两种概念（α权力和实际权力），并在不假设主体独立性的四类广义并发博弈框架与相应四类邻域框架之间建立了表示等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发博弈框架是策略推理逻辑的标准语义框架，其中存在两种联盟权力概念：α权力和实际权力。近期研究将标准模型中的三个结构性假设（序列性、主体独立性和确定性）分离，产生了八类广义并发博弈框架。本文旨在探究这些广义框架与邻域框架之间的表示关系，特别是在不假设主体独立性的情况下。

Method: 通过严格的数学证明，本文首先针对实际权力，证明了不假设主体独立性的四类广义并发博弈框架可由对应的四类邻域框架表示；进而基于此结果，证明了对于α权力，同样的四类广义并发博弈框架也可由对应的四类邻域框架表示。

Result: 建立了不假设主体独立性的四类广义并发博弈框架与四类对应邻域框架之间的表示等价性，分别针对实际权力和α权力两种联盟权力概念。

Conclusion: 广义并发博弈框架（不假设主体独立性）与邻域框架之间存在系统的表示对应关系，这为理解联盟权力的语义基础提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Concurrent game frames are a standard semantic framework for logics of strategic reasoning. Two notions of coalition power can be derived from such frames: alpha powers and actual powers. An alpha power of a coalition is a set of possible futures such that the coalition has an action that forces the resulting future to lie in that set. An actual power of a coalition is a set of possible futures satisfying the following condition: the coalition has an action such that (1) the action forces the resulting future to lie in the set, and (2) every future in the set is compatible with that action. Recent generalizations of concurrent game frames separate three structural assumptions built into the standard model: seriality, independence of agents, and determinism. This yields eight classes of general concurrent game frames. In this paper, we prove that for actual powers, the four classes of general concurrent game frames, where independence of agents is not assumed, are representable by four corresponding classes of neighborhood frames. Building on this result, we show that for alpha powers, the same four classes of general concurrent game frames are likewise representable by four corresponding classes of neighborhood frames.

</details>


### [300] [The Complexity of Computing Coarse Correlated Equilibria in Markov Games with a Single Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10897)
*Gabriele Farina,Andreas Kontogiannis,Ioannis Panageas,Vasilis Pollatos*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在两人单控制器随机博弈中，即使只考虑平稳马尔可夫粗相关均衡（CCE）的计算，也是PPAD完全的，且不需要依赖于均衡崩溃现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已证明两人一般和随机博弈中计算CCE是PPAD难的，但依赖的是交替控制（每个状态由不同玩家控制）导致CCE坍缩为纳什均衡的结构。核心开放问题是：当单一玩家控制所有状态转移时，这种坍缩不再发生，此时计算CCE是否仍然困难？

Method: 构造单控制器博弈的"gadget"（组件），其局部激励约束即使在强相关的平稳策略下也能强制求解Pure-Circuit实例。不依赖于CCE坍缩为NE的途径，而是直接从CCE的定义出发构造硬度归约。

Result: 证明两人单控制器随机博弈中计算近似平稳马尔可夫CCE是PPAD完全的。(1) 对于完美概念（每个状态的均衡约束），常数精度下无条件成立；(2) 对于非完美概念，在PCP-for-PPAD假设下常数精度困难，无条件逆多项式精度困难。

Conclusion: 首次证明了不需要通过均衡崩溃现象或纳什结构来证明CCE的计算硬度，为随机博弈中CCE的计算复杂性提供了更深入的理解。

Abstract: We study the complexity of computing stationary Markov coarse correlated equilibria (CCE) in discounted single-controller stochastic (Markov) games [PR81, FV97], a fundamental subclass of stochastic games in which all players may affect rewards, but only one player controls the state transitions. Prior work [DGZ23, JMS23, HN25] established PPAD-hardness for computing stationary Markov CCE in two-player general-sum stochastic games via turn-based constructions in which each state is controlled by a single player, with control alternating across states. This structure forces every Markov CCE to collapse to a Nash equilibrium (NE), so hardness for NE transfers immediately to CCE. It remained open whether hardness persists when a single player controls all transitions--a setting where no such collapse occurs.
  We resolve this question: computing an approximate stationary Markov CCE in two-player single-controller stochastic games is PPAD-complete, even with a fixed discount factor and binary actions. For the perfect notion (equilibrium constraints at every state) this holds unconditionally at constant accuracy; for the non-perfect notion, we prove constant-accuracy hardness under the PCP-for-PPAD hypothesis [BPR16, DFHM26] and inverse-polynomial-accuracy hardness unconditionally. To the best of our knowledge, our result is the first to show hardness for computing CCE without relying on equilibrium collapse phenomena or other routes through Nash-like structure [FGK23, AKSZ24, PR24]. Instead, we construct single-controller gadgets whose local incentive constraints force a solution of a Pure-Circuit instance even under strongly correlated stationary policies.

</details>


### [301] [Decoupling Corruption and Horizon in Robust Contextual Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11210)
*Matteo Castiglioni,Francesco Emanuele Stradi*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究鲁棒重复上下文定价问题，提出一种算法实现了 $\mathcal O(Cd + d^2\log T)$ 的遗憾上界，首次将腐败预算 $C$ 与时间域 $T$ 的依赖分离，解决了 Gupta 等人（2025）留下的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重复上下文定价场景中，卖家每轮仅能观察到可能被腐败的二元销售反馈，现有方法未能将腐败预算 $C$ 与时间域 $T$ 的依赖分离开来，Gupta 等人（2025）将该问题列为开放问题。

Method: 设计了一种新算法，利用对腐败预算 $C$ 的上界知识，结合上下文特征维度 $d$，在定价决策中引入鲁棒机制，使得遗憾上界中的 $C$ 项与 $T$ 解耦。

Result: 算法遗憾上界为 $\mathcal O(Cd + d^2\log T)$，其中 $C$ 为腐败预算，$d$ 为上下文维度，$T$ 为时间域。

Conclusion: 该结果首次实现了鲁棒上下文定价中腐败预算与时间域的依赖分离，填补了 Gupta 等人（2025）提出的开放问题，为具有对抗性腐败的在线定价提供了更强的理论保证。

Abstract: We study robust repeated contextual pricing, where valuations depends linearly on the features. At each round $t\in[T]$, a seller observes a context, posts a price, and receives only a possibly corrupted binary sale feedback. The seller knows an upper bound $C$ on the number of corrupted rounds. We design an algorithm with regret $\mathcal O(Cd+d^2\log T)$, where $d$ is the context dimension. This is the first guarantee for robust contextual pricing that separates the dependence on the corruption budget $C$ from the horizon $T$, closing the problem left open by Gupta, Guruganesh, Paes Leme, and Schneider (2025).

</details>


### [302] [From PBS to ePBS: the Microstructure of Block Building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11240)
*Jingyu Liu,Bolin Zhang,Lin William Cong,Siguang Li,Xuechao Wang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究以太坊Glamsterdam升级引入的ePBS机制（内置提议者-构建者分离），通过不完全信息两阶段拍卖模型分析其与PBS的差异，发现ePBS存在棘轮效应导致分配无效率和收入效率低谷，并提出TEE侧链方案可提升提议者收入约25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以太坊Glamsterdam升级将PBS（中继为中心的提议者-构建者分离）替换为ePBS（内置提议者-构建者分离），构建者直接向提议者投标。这一结构性变化如何影响区块构建的微观市场机制尚未被充分研究，本文旨在通过博弈论模型刻画这一变化带来的激励效应与效率损失。

Method: 建立一般化的不完全信息两阶段拍卖模型（含可验证消息），其中早期出价同时作为价格要约和信号。将PBS和ePBS建模为同一区块构建博弈的不同约束版本：PBS外生固定停止和披露规则，ePBS让提议者事后选择停止和披露时机。捕获延迟异质性通过不对称信息更新（快速构建者可观察到披露的早期信息后再出价，慢速构建者不能）。结合可处理情形下的完美贝叶斯均衡精确刻画与有限博弈中的校准无遗憾学习。

Result: 对PBS，分离均衡保留了标准第一价格拍卖的收益基准，并给出了存在性条件。对ePBS，展示了棘轮效应：因提议者可延迟区块提议并在第二阶段利用早期出价信息，构建者预期事后攫取而压低或混同早期出价，导致分配无效率和收入效率低谷。分析了棘轮扭曲源于承诺缺失。完全承诺下最优机制退化为静态Myerson拍卖并消除棘轮通道。提出基于可信执行环境（TEE）的侧链机制，将收入最大化TEE机制建模为双线性优化问题。在保守的有限基准测试中，TEE设计相比第一价格基准提升提议者收入约25%。

Conclusion: ePBS虽然消除了中继依赖，但引入了棘轮效应这一新的效率损失源；通过TEE侧链实现有限承诺可部分恢复完全承诺的优势，显著提升提议者收入。该机制为以太坊区块构建市场的实际设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Ethereum's Glamsterdam upgrade introduces enshrined proposer-builder separation (ePBS), replacing relay-centric PBS with direct builder bids to proposers. We study how this shift changes the block-building microstructure through a general imperfect-information two-stage auction with verifiable messages, where an early bid serves as both a price offer and a signal. PBS and ePBS are modeled as restrictions of the same block-building game: PBS fixes stopping and disclosure exogenously, while ePBS lets the proposer choose stopping and disclosure ex post. Latency heterogeneity is captured by asymmetric information updates: fast builders observe disclosed early information before rebidding, while slow builders do not. We combine exact perfect Bayesian equilibrium characterizations in tractable cases with calibrated no-regret learning in finite games. For PBS, we show that separating equilibria preserve the standard first-price-auction payoff benchmark and provide conditions for their existence. For ePBS, we demonstrate a ratchet effect: because the proposer can defer block proposal and use early bid information in the second stage, builders anticipate ex-post extraction and shade or pool early bids, generating allocation inefficiency and revenue-efficiency valleys.
  We interpret this ratchet distortion as a commitment failure. Under full commitment, the optimal policy collapses to the static Myerson auction and removes the ratchet channel. To realize part of this commitment advantage in a feasible mechanism, we propose a Trusted Execution Environment (TEE) sidecar that enforces limited commitment. We formulate the revenue-maximizing TEE mechanism as a bilinear optimization problem. In conservative finite benchmarks, the TEE design increases the proposer revenue relative to the first-price benchmark by approximately \(25\%\).

</details>


### [303] [Paradoxes of Game Theoretic Equilibria and Price of Anarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11752)
*Georgios Piliouras,Ian Gemp,Siqi Liu,Luke Marris*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文揭示了将多智能体学习简化到静态均衡和黑箱无遗憾分析会掩盖动态非均衡和博弈论界。主要发现包括：（1）内部纳什均衡缺乏C¹向量场信息，导致智能体无法区分对齐与严格对抗的激励；（2）决定鲁棒PoA界的纯纳什均衡是拓扑不稳定的严格鞍点，在拥塞博弈中是全局排斥子且支撑在几乎处处严格占优策略上；（3）允许所有正仿射成本会使PoA无界；（4）投影到相关策略单纯形会容纳非理性行为，Coarse Correlated Equilibria及其近端改进无法排除严格占优策略；（5）最优O(1/T)交换遗憾最小化无法排除宏观湍流和混沌极限集；（6）在离散时间学习中，非原子拥塞博弈的独特均衡失稳为Li-Yorke混沌，其时间平均效率随多项式度p呈2^p指数级退化。这些结果要求重新评估用于动态基础度量的最坏情况均衡框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统算法博弈论依赖静态均衡解概念（纳什、相关、粗相关均衡）和无遗憾学习的黑箱分析，但本文认为这种简化掩盖了多智能体学习中的动态非均衡现象和博弈论界的真实结构，因此需要从动力学视角重新审视这些经典框架。

Method: 通过几何动力学分析、拓扑稳定性理论、混沌理论（Li-Yorke混沌）以及非原子拥塞博弈的极限分析，系统性地检验了静态均衡概念在动态学习过程中的几何性质、拓扑稳定性和效率退化行为。

Result: （1）内部纳什均衡缺乏C¹向量场信息；（2）最坏情况纯纳什均衡是拓扑不稳定的严格鞍点和全局排斥子；（3）允许所有正仿射成本使PoA无界；（4）相关投影和非理性行为容纳；（5）最优遗憾最小化仍产生混沌；（6）非原子拥塞博弈的均衡在离散时间下失稳为混沌，效率呈指数退化。

Conclusion: 当前基于最坏情况均衡的框架（如PoA）在动态学习场景下具有根本性缺陷，需要建立以动态为基础的新度量体系来替代或补充传统的静态平衡分析。

Abstract: For decades, static solution concepts (Nash, Correlated, and Coarse Correlated Equilibria) and the Price of Anarchy (PoA) have formed the bedrock of algorithmic game theory, with no-regret learning proving fast convergence to such game-theoretic equilibria. We show that reducing multi-agent learning to static equilibrium and black-box regret analysis obscures underlying dynamic disequilibrium and game theoretic bounds.
  First, interior Nash equilibria lack $C^1$ vector field information, meaning agents cannot distinguish aligned from strictly opposing incentives. Inheriting this geometry, the worst-case pure Nash equilibria dictating robust PoA bounds manifest as topologically unstable strict saddles, and in canonical congestion games, as global repellers supported on almost everywhere strictly dominated strategies. Anchoring efficiency guarantees to these unstable states causes algebraic sensitivity; we prove that accommodating all strictly positive affine costs renders the PoA unbounded. Furthermore, projecting learning trajectories onto the discrete simplex of correlated play systematically accommodates non-rationalizable behavior. Evaluating dynamics via Coarse Correlated Equilibria or proximal refinements fails to preclude strictly dominated strategies. Moreover, optimal $O(1/T)$ swap-regret minimization does not preclude macroscopic turbulence, manifesting as chaotic limit sets even in minimal games. Finally, we examine the non-atomic limit of congestion games. Though considered highly stable with tight sub-linear $Θ(p/\ln p)$ PoA bounds (where $p$ is the polynomial degree), we prove that under discrete-time learning, the unique equilibrium destabilizes into Li-Yorke chaos and global attractors whose time-averaged inefficiency degrades exponentially as $2^p$. These results necessitate re-evaluating worst-case equilibrium frameworks for dynamically grounded metrics.

</details>

### [304] [Large Multimodal Model-Based Environment-Aware Mobility Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09795)
*Seokhyun Jeong,Sangmok Shin,Seungnyun Kim,Jiao Wu,Byonghyo Shim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Recently, large language models (LLMs) have been successfully adopted in various fields, including wireless communications, robotics, and autonomous vehicles, owing to their outstanding adaptability and reasoning abilities. Despite their huge potential, the application of LLMs for mobility management is relatively scarce since it requires not only analyzing wireless measurements but also predicting dynamic user trajectories and making real-time handover decisions across densely deployed small base stations (SBSs). In this paper, we propose an environment-aware mobility management scheme based on large multimodal models (LMMs), which extend capabilities of LLMs to process multimodal sensing data. By leveraging LMMs, the proposed scheme extracts contextual information on the surrounding environments from RGB-D images to capture user equipment (UE) mobility patterns and identify signal reflections and blockages caused by static reflectors and dynamic obstacles. Using the extracted environmental information, the proposed scheme learns the intrinsic mapping from UE and SBS positions to channel capacity, referred to as channel capacity map (CCM), from which future channel capacities along UE trajectories are predicted. Based on the predicted channel capacities, we determine proactive handover decisions maximizing the cumulative channel capacities. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves substantial channel capacity improvements over conventional deep learning (DL)-based approaches.

</details>


### [305] [Channel Knowledge Empowered Finite-Blocklength Rate-Splitting Transmission for High-Mobility Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10138)
*Yi Wang,Yingyang Chen,Feng Bai,Li Wang,Gang Feng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: To meet the extended ultra-low latency and high reliability (xURLLC) requirements for autonomous driving systems, multiple access schemes must operate reliably in high-mobility and complex propagation environments. Recently, rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) has emerged as a promising multi-user transmission framework, showing robustness in dynamic situations where imperfect and outdated channel state information (CSI) is prevalent.Moreover, the advanced sensing, localization, and on-board computation capabilities of autonomous driving vehicles facilitate the construction of a channel knowledge map (CKM), which is a key enabler for environment-aware communications in future 6G networks.In this work, we propose a CKM empowered finite-blocklength (FBL) RSMA for downlink autonomous driving system. The location-dependent large-scale channel information provided by CKM is exploited in RSMA to develop a refined rate-splitting design. The min-rate performance of FBL rate splitting is analyzed explicitly to ensure user fairness. We derive a new and tight closed-form bound for the private-stream ergodic rate. Combined with the closed-form common-stream expression, an efficient optimization design of rate-splitting ratios has been formulated. Numerical results show that the CKM empowered FBL RSMA outperforms space-division multiple access (SDMA) and non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA), particularly in high-mobility scenarios. Its performance is improved by a data-based CKM, which provides more accurate large-scale channel information than model-based approaches and enables more precise common-stream allocation. The results also reveal that RSMA is sensitive to errors in large-scale channel knowledge, emphasizing the importance of accurate CKM information for optimal rate-splitting.

</details>


### [306] [A Faster Closest-Point Algorithm for the $A_n^*$ Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10479)
*Yuriy A. Reznik*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对对偶根格 $A_n^*$ 的最远点问题提出了一种新的线性时间算法，通过利用前缀聚合（计数和残差和）避免桶内排序和遍历，在 $n=2,\dots,100$ 范围内相比现有最快MCSQ算法实现约 $1.8\times$ 到 $3.0\times$ 的加速，且支持纯整数精确计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶根格 $A_n^*$ 在量化、编码和估计中具有重要应用，特别是对单纯形约束数据（如直方图、概率分布）的量化。解决其最近点问题的现有最快算法（MCSQ）虽为线性时间，但依赖于桶排序中的链表遍历和指针追踪，存在性能瓶颈。

Method: 关键观察：最近点目标函数仅依赖于舍入残差的两个前缀聚合量——计数和残差和。因此桶内元素无需排序、存储或遍历。算法用基于计数排序风格的两平面数组单次桶传递替代MCSQ的链表遍历，并通过缩放目标函数使大部分计算转为精确整数算术；当输入坐标为有理数（如直方图）时，整个算法退化为纯整数运算。

Result: 在Intel Core i9-13900H上对 $n=2,\dots,100$ 进行实验，相比MCSQ实现约 $1.8\times$ 到 $3.0\times$ 加速，高维时增益更大。同时显著快于Conway和Sloan方法对其他根格（$A_n$、$D_n^*$、$E_8$）的处理。开源实现见fanstar项目。

Conclusion: 提出了一种更快、更简洁且支持精确整数计算的 $A_n^*$ 最近点算法，通过避免桶内操作从根本上提升了效率，并适用于更广泛的根格和有理数输入场景。

Abstract: The dual root lattice $A_n^*$ is an important lattice in quantization, coding, and estimation. It can be represented as the projection of the integer lattice $\mathbb{Z}^{n+1}$ onto the $n$-dimensional hyperplane whose coordinates sum to zero. This representation makes $A_n^*$ particularly natural for quantizing simplex-constrained data, such as histograms and probability distributions. This paper studies the closest-point problem for $A_n^*$: given a query vector $y$, find the lattice point $x\in A_n^*$ minimizing $\|y-x\|^2$. The fastest previously known method is the linear-time algorithm of McKilliam, Clarkson, Smith, and Quinn (MCSQ), which employs bucket sort as a core operation. We present a faster linear-time algorithm. The key observation is that the closest-point objective depends on the rounding residuals only through two prefix aggregates: a count and a residual sum. Hence the elements inside each bucket never need to be sorted, stored, or traversed. This replaces the linked-list traversal and pointer chasing of MCSQ with a single bucketing pass over two flat arrays with counting-sort-style accumulates. A scaled objective further makes most of the computation exact integer arithmetic, and when the input coordinates are rationals with a common denominator, for example, histograms or empirical distibutions, the entire algorithm becomes exact and integer-only. Experiments on an Intel Core i9-13900H show speedups of about $1.8\times$ to $3.0\times$ over MCSQ for $n=2,\dots,100$, with larger gains at higher dimensions. The proposed algorithm is also noticeably faster than Conway and Sloan methods for other root lattices, including $A_n$, $D_n^*$, and $E_8$. An open-source implementation is available in the "fanstar" project.

</details>


### [307] [SCMA Inspired Sparse Vector Coding: An Enhanced URLLC Transmission Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10520)
*Zhen-Ming Huang,Zilong Liu,Leila Musavian,Chao-Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SCMA-SVC的新型稀疏矢量编码方案，通过融合SCMA的稀疏模式和星群设计，增大了最小欧氏距离，实现了多用户编码增益和星座整形增益，并在瑞利衰落信道上达到全分集阶数，显著优于现有SVC变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索SCMA与SVC之间的协同交互，利用SCMA的稀疏模式和星群特性来改进SVC的性能，从而增强超可靠低延迟通信。

Method: 提出SCMA-SVC方案，核心思想是利用SCMA的稀疏模式和星群特征扩大SVC码本的最小欧氏距离；同时引入随机相位旋转，在瑞利衰落信道上实现全分集。还设计了利用结构稀疏性的低复杂度解码器。

Result: SCMA-SVC在最大似然解码下，在高斯和瑞利衰落信道上均表现出显著的错误率性能提升；所提低复杂度解码器在保持近ML性能的同时降低了复杂度。仿真结果表明其可靠性显著优于现有SVC变体。

Conclusion: SCMA与SVC的融合可以有效提升SVC的性能，SCMA-SVC在可靠性和分集性能方面均优于传统SVC方案。

Abstract: Sparsity is inherently exploited in sparse code multiple access (SCMA) and sparse vector coding (SVC), yet the interaction between these two has not been explored before. It is intriguing to ask if one can be used to improve the other, and vice versa. In this work, we present a novel SCMA inspired SVC scheme, called SCMA-SVC, for enhanced ultra-reliable low-latency communications. Our key idea is to exploit the sparse pattern and multidimensional constellation nature of SCMA, with which one is able to further enlarge the minimum Euclidean distance (MED) of the corresponding SVC codebooks. Such an innovation allows us to harvest the multiuser coding gain and the constellation shaping gain which are pertinent to SCMA. Moreover, by applying random phase rotations to the sparse vectors, it is shown that the proposed SCMA-SVC achieves full diversity order over Rayleigh fading channels. Under maximum likelihood (ML) decoding, the proposed SCMA-SVC demonstrates remarkable error rate performances over both Gaussian and Rayleigh fading channels. Additionally, we develop a low-complexity decoder that exploits the structural sparsity of SCMA-SVC while maintaining near-ML performance. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed SCMA-SVC achieves significantly improved reliability over the existing SVC variants.

</details>


### [308] [List-Decoding Counterexamples Yield Lower Bounds on Mutual Correlated Agreement Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10572)
*Yiwen Gao,Hong Yang,Yang Xu,Haibin Kan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了列表译码反例可构造性地给出互相关一致性误差的下界，并针对AG码和RS码给出了具体实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互相关一致性（MCA）是批处理邻近测试（Batched Proximity Testing）可靠性中的一个关键性质，需要理解其误差下界与列表译码之间的联系。

Method: 给定线性码的(p,L)-列表可译性反例，构造一个同长度同维度的新码C'，使其MCA误差至少为(1/q)·ceil((L+1)q/(q+L))，且最小距离至多减1。对于以有限集Ω索引的码族，进一步给出保持结构的构造版本，仅改变一个坐标索引。应用于AG求值码和RS码。

Result: 对于AG码，得到的MCA误差下界为(1/q)·ceil((L+1)N/(N+L·deg G))；RS码作为Vandermonde列特例得到相应结论。

Conclusion: 列表译码反例可系统性地转化为互相关一致性的下界，且该构造能保持码的结构性质，为理解批处理邻近测试的可靠性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Mutual correlated agreement captures whether a random linear combination of received words can create a new large agreement with a code, a property relevant to the soundness of batched proximity testing. We show constructively that list-decoding counterexamples yield lower bounds on the mutual correlated agreement error. Given an explicit counterexample to the $(p,L)$-list-decodability of a linear code over $\mathbb{F}_q$, we construct a related code $C'$ of the same length and dimension such that $\operatorname{err}_{\mathrm{MCA}}(C',p)\ge\frac{1}{q}\left\lceil\frac{(L+1)q}{q+L}\right\rceil$, while decreasing its minimum distance by at most one. The construction also produces an explicit pair of words witnessing this error.
  We further give a structure-preserving version for code families whose coordinates are indexed by a finite set $Ω$, with each index determining a generator-matrix column through a map $v:Ω\to\mathbb{F}_q^k$. The construction changes at most one coordinate index and ensures that the output code remains in the same indexed family. As applications, we instantiate this principle for algebraic-geometry (AG) evaluation codes and Reed--Solomon codes. For AG codes, if $G$ is the divisor defining the underlying Riemann--Roch space and $N$ is the number of rational places outside $\operatorname{supp}(G)$ available for evaluation, the resulting code remains over the same function field and Riemann--Roch space, with a modified set of evaluation places. Its mutual correlated agreement error is at least $\frac{1}{q}\left\lceil\frac{(L+1)N}{N+L\mathrm{deg} G}\right\rceil$. The Reed--Solomon conclusion follows as the Vandermonde-column specialization.

</details>


### [309] [Wei-type duality and asymptotics of the footprint bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10680)
*Rodrigo San-José*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文建立了评估码广义汉明权重的足迹界与对偶足迹界之间的Wei型对偶性，该对偶性也适用于Andersen-Geil界和Feng-Rao界。同时证明了足迹界和对偶足迹界无法保证评估码族的渐近优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索评估码广义汉明权重的足迹界与对偶足迹界之间的内在对偶关系，并验证这些界在评估码渐近优性分析中的有效性。

Method: 采用Wei型对偶方法，建立足迹界与对偶足迹界之间的对偶关系，并将该对偶性推广至Andersen-Geil界和Feng-Rao界。

Result: 成功建立了评估码广义汉明权重的足迹界与对偶足迹界之间的Wei型对偶性，该对偶性同样适用于Andersen-Geil界和Feng-Rao界。此外，证明了足迹界和对偶足迹界不能用于保证评估码族的渐近优性。

Conclusion: 足迹界与对偶足迹界之间存在Wei型对偶性，但这些界在评估码渐近优性分析中具有局限性，不能作为保证渐近优性的工具。

Abstract: We obtain a Wei-type duality between the footprint bound and the dual footprint bound for the generalized Hamming weights of an evaluation code. This duality applies between the Andersen-Geil and Feng-Rao bounds as well. We also prove that the footprint and dual footprint bounds cannot be used to guarantee the asymptotic goodness of a family of evaluation codes.

</details>


### [310] [A Unified Dual Framework for Sparse-Array Near-Field Beam Focusing With Spatial Interference Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10764)
*Changhao He,Xiaojuan Zhang,Francois Chin Po Shin*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过拉格朗日对偶分析，建立了稀疏阵列近场波束聚焦中空间干扰抑制的解析框架，证明了最优波束形成器是广义匹配滤波器，给出了对偶测度的有限支撑界，导出了平均SIR的渐近对数标度律，并提出了实用的黎曼共轭梯度算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏阵列近场波束聚焦设计（如SOCP切割平面法）虽能数值求解，但可达信干比（SIR）及其与经典自适应波束形成的关系缺乏解析刻画，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用拉格朗日对偶分析方法，推导最优波束形成器的闭式解；通过对偶测度有限支撑的分析给出收敛性保证；利用渐近分析导出平均SIR的对数标度律；开发基于单位环流形的黎曼共轭梯度算法实现恒模波束形成。

Result: 1) 最优波束形成器为广义匹配滤波器，统一了MVDR、LCMV和SOCP方法；2) 对偶测度支撑集大小不超过M²，均匀线阵可收紧至M；3) 近共线几何下平均SIR具有M的渐近对数标度律，阵列阶数而非优化算法是主导性能因素；4) 所提黎曼算法紧密逼近理论性能极限。

Conclusion: 建立了近场稀疏阵列波束聚焦的解析性能界，揭示了阵列尺寸而非算法选择的主导作用，并为恒模约束下的实际实现提供了有效算法。

Abstract: We study sparse-array near-field beam focusing with spatial interference suppression, a problem arising in coherent satellite formations and other distributed non-terrestrial arrays. State-of-the-art designs solve it numerically through second-order cone programming (SOCP) with cutting-plane refinement, yet the achievable signal-to-interference ratio (SIR) and its link to classical adaptive beamforming have remained without an analytical characterization. We supply this characterization via a Lagrangian-dual analysis, obtaining three results. First, every optimal beamformer is a generalized matched filter against an effective spatial covariance induced by an optimal dual measure; this closed form recovers MVDR, LCMV, and SOCP-based focusing as special cases. Second, the dual measure has finite support of cardinality at most $M^2$ in general, sharpening to $M$ for uniform linear arrays ($M$ the number of array elements), which yields a finite-dimensional convergence certificate for cutting-plane methods. Third, a closed-form upper bound on the mean-SIR admits an asymptotic logarithmic scaling law in $M$ under near-collinear geometry, identifying array order, rather than the optimization algorithm, as the dominant performance factor. A Riemannian conjugate-gradient algorithm on the unit-torus manifold is developed for practical constant-modulus beamforming, and numerical results demonstrate that it closely approaches the derived performance limit.

</details>


### [311] [Minimum distance and decoding of Coxeter codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10774)
*Alexander Barg,Qëndrim R. Gashi,Tianyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了有限Coxeter系统对应的二元Coxeter码的最小距离猜想，并由此将Reed-Muller码的Reed多数逻辑译码算法推广到Coxeter码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Coble和Barg在近期论文中引入Coxeter码作为Reed-Muller码的推广，并提出了Coxeter码最小距离的猜想值。该猜想的证明是理解这类新编码理论性质的关键步骤。

Method: 本文主要通过代数组合与Coxeter群表示理论，对Coxeter码的最小距离猜想给出了严格证明。

Result: 成功证明了Coxeter码最小距离的猜想值，并由此推导出Coxeter理论框架下Reed多数逻辑译码算法的推广形式。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了Coxeter码的理论基础，并展示了其在译码算法方面的应用潜力。

Abstract: A binary Coxeter code associated with a finite Coxeter system $(W,S)$ is an ${\mathbb F}_2$-linear span of indicators of standard cosets of a fixed rank. Coxeter codes, introduced in a recent paper by N. Coble and A. Barg, are a generalization of Reed--Muller codes which arise when $W={\mathbb Z}_2^m$ is the Coxeter group of type $mA_1$. In that paper, the authors proposed a conjectural value for the minimum distance of a general Coxeter code. This conjecture is proved in the present work. As a consequence, we obtain a Coxeter-theoretic generalization of Reed's majority-logic decoding algorithm for Reed--Muller codes.

</details>


### [312] [Construction of self-orthogonal codes over a commutative non-unitary ring of order 25](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10844)
*Jon-Lark Kim,Marvin Olavides,Young Gun Roe*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究素数 p=5 时非幺环 I_5 上的线性码。定义了自正交码、拟自对偶码和自对偶码三类码，通过关联的剩余码和挠码分析其结构，并在给定类型 {k1, k2} 下完成了长度不超过4的monomial等价分类。同时纠正了之前文献中关于 I_5 上拟自对偶码分类的错误，包括某些码的自同构群阶数有误及与质量公式不一致等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非幺环上的编码理论是近年热点，但已有研究中关于 I_5 上拟自对偶码的分类存在错误（如自同构群阶数计算不准确、与质量公式矛盾），需要修正和完善。此外，将 I_p 环上码的研究从 p=2,3 推广到 p=5 也具有重要意义。

Method: 定义了 I_5 上线性码的三类概念，引入关联的剩余码和挠码分析结构；利用质量公式和升阶构造（building-up construction），在给定类型 {k1, k2} 下按monomial等价关系对长度≤4的码进行完全分类；通过重新计算自同构群阶数并验证质量公式一致性来纠正前人错误。

Result: 完成了 I_5 上三类码在长度不超过4时的完全分类；纠正了 Alahmadi 等人关于 I_5 上拟自对偶码分类中的错误，包括对 n=2, type {1,0} 和 n=3, type {1,1} 情况下自同构群阶数及质量公式不一致问题的修正。

Conclusion: 本文将非幺环 I_p 上编码理论从 p=2,3 推广到 p=5，建立了 I_5 上三类线性码的结构理论并完成小长度分类，同时修正了此前文献中的分类错误，为更一般素数 p 的非幺环编码研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Codes over non-unitary rings have been studied recently. In particular, codes over the commutative non-unitary ring $I_p$ (in the classification of Fine) of order $p^2$ where $p$ is a prime are being considered. For $p=2$ (resp. $p=3$), three categories of codes over $I_p$ have been studied: self-orthogonal codes, quasi self-dual codes, and self-dual codes over $I_p$. Using some related mass formulas and building-up constructions, classifications of these codes have been done up to the permutation equivalence (resp. the monomial equivalence) for certain small lengths. In this paper, we take the prime $p=5$ and consider the ring $I_5$. We introduce the notion of linear codes over $I_5$. We also define the same three categories of linear $I_5$-codes, study the structures of these $I_5$-codes and relate them to their associated residue and torsion codes. We classify the three categories of codes completely in lengths at most $4$ up to the monomial equivalence for a given type $\{ k_1 , k_2 \}$. Moreover, in the paper of Alahmadi et al. regarding the mass formula for self-orthogonal codes over $I_p$, mistakes in the classification of quasi self-dual codes over $I_5$ had been made such as incorrect automorphism group order of some codes or inconsistency with the mass formula for self-orthogonal codes over $I_p$ for length $n=2$ and type $\{ 1 , 0 \}$ and for length $n=3$ and type $\{ 1, 1 \}$. We correct and improve such results.

</details>


### [313] [Faster Closest-Point Algorithms for the $E_6^*$ and $E_7^*$ Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10885)
*Yuriy A. Reznik*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对E6*和E7*对偶格子的快速最近点算法，通过将TYK分解重新表述为胶向量形式，利用MCQ的A_n*最近点算法特性，将多个陪集解码合并为单次扫描，实现了约4-6倍（E6*）和3-4倍（E7*）的计算量缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E6*和E7*对偶格子因其最小的归一化二阶矩（即最小平均量化误差）在信源编码和数据压缩中具有重要应用价值，但实际使用需要高效的最近点算法。现有方法需要多次陪集解码和显式距离计算，计算开销较大。

Method: 将TYK分解重新表述为胶向量形式，揭示了E7*是A7*的偶胶类并集，E6*是A1*⊕A5*的奇偶匹配子格。利用MCQ算法对A_n*的最近点搜索会恰好遍历所有胶类且每类内最优的特性，通过单次排序扫描每个坐标块即可同时获得所有陪集的最接近点。

Result: E6*和E7*的解码成本大致相当于单次A5*或A7*量化，粗计算表明相对逐陪集解码，E6*减少约4-6倍，E7*减少约3-4倍。此外还讨论了A_n*算法最新改进带来的常数因子提升，以及无排序线性时间解码的开放问题。

Conclusion: 该方法显著降低了E6*和E7*对偶格子的最近点搜索计算复杂度，使其在实际应用中的可行性大幅提升。

Abstract: The dual lattices $E_6^*$ and $E_7^*$ are of particular interest in source coding and data compression applications. Among all known lattices in dimensions six and seven they attain the smallest normalized second moments, i.e., the smallest average quantization error. Their use in practice requires fast closest-point (nearest-lattice-point) algorithms. The known approach, due to Conway and Sloane and completed for $E_6$ and $E_6^*$ by Takizawa, Yagi, and Kawabata (TYK), decodes these lattices as unions of cosets of root lattices $A_n$: each coset is decoded separately, and the best result is kept. This requires four coset decodings for $E_7^*$ and six for $E_6^*$, together with explicit distance computations.
  This paper shows that all these coset decodings can be collapsed into a single sweep. Reformulated in terms of glue vectors, the TYK decompositions state that $E_7^*$ is the union of the even glue classes of $A_7^*$, and that $E_6^*$ is a parity-matched sublattice of $A_1^*\oplus A_5^*$. The candidate chain constructed by the closest-point algorithm of McKilliam, Clarkson, and Quinn (MCQ) for $A_n^*$ visits every glue class of $A_n$ exactly once and is optimal within each class. Consequently, one sorted sweep per coordinate block yields the closest points of all glue cosets simultaneously, and $E_6^*$ and $E_7^*$ are decoded at roughly the cost of a single $A_5^*$ or $A_7^*$ quantization. Rough operation counts indicate a $4$--$6\times$ reduction for $E_6^*$ and $3$--$4\times$ for $E_7^*$ relative to coset-by-coset decoding. We also discuss further constant-factor improvements available from recent refinements of the $A_n^*$ algorithms, and an open question concerning sort-free linear-time decoding.

</details>


### [314] [Differentially Private Consistent Release of Counting Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10952)
*Borzoo Rassouli,Morteza Varasteh*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在满足一致性（输出在查询可行范围内）和(ε,δ)-差分隐私约束下，释放计数查询结果的随机机制。推导了最小可达错误概率的闭式表达式并得到显式最优机制，随后刻画了全体最优机制类。进一步将框架扩展至隐私机制与固定随机变换（通信媒介）级联的场景，给出了无损条件、基于凸混合与谱扰动的性能界，并在M元PSK+AWGN信道应用中证明高隐私区未编码传输的近似最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在差分隐私计数查询发布中，同时满足一致性（输出在可行域内）与隐私保障是核心需求，但现有工作缺乏对该场景下最优错误概率的精确刻画；此外，实际部署中隐私机制常与通信信道等固定随机变换级联，这种级联对最优性能的影响尚未被系统分析。

Method: 首先通过闭式推导获得最小可达错误概率及显式最优机制；利用该机制满足的主动差分隐私约束，通过传播论证刻画全体最优机制的结构特性。级联场景下，先建立无损条件的充要条件，再基于凸混合和谱扰动推导性能上下界，最后在M元PSK-AWGN信道中验证理论结论。

Result: 得到了最小可达错误概率的闭式表达式和显式最优机制；刻画了全体最优机制的共同结构；给出了级联场景下无损的充要条件以及性能的上下界；在M元PSK+AWGN信道应用中，发现高隐私保护强度下未编码传输接近最优。

Conclusion: 本文为满足一致性与差分隐私的计数查询发布提供了完整的理论基础（最优机制及其结构刻画），并首次系统分析了与固定随机变换级联时的最优性能损失问题，为实际系统中隐私机制与通信信道的联合设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study the problem of releasing counting-query outputs through a stochastic mechanism that is both consistent and \((ε,δ)\)-differentially private. Consistency requires the released value to lie within the feasible range of the query, while utility is measured by the worst-case probability of error. We first derive a closed-form expression for the minimum achievable error probability and obtain an explicit optimal mechanism. By exploiting the active differential privacy constraints satisfied by this mechanism, we then characterize the entire class of optimal mechanisms via a propagation argument, identifying the structural properties shared by all optimizers.
  We next extend the framework to the setting in which the privacy mechanism is cascaded with an arbitrary fixed stochastic transformation representing a predetermined portion of the communication medium between the source and the destination. We first establish necessary and sufficient conditions under which this partial fixation of the medium incurs no loss in utility. We then derive upper and lower bounds on the optimal achievable performance based on convex mixing and spectral perturbation. Finally, we apply the theory to (M)-ary phase-shift keying (PSK) transmission over an additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN) channel and show that uncoded transmission is effectively optimal in the high-privacy regime.

</details>


### [315] [On the Performance of Integrated Satellite-Terrestrial Maritime Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11152)
*Kaiwei Xiong,Xiaoming Chen,Ming Ying*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种岸基地面基站（TBS）与低轨（LEO）卫星协同的海陆集成海事通信系统，分析了近海与远海用户的传输速率和覆盖概率，进行了特殊情况下的渐近分析，并设计了优化算法以最大化近海用户覆盖概率，同时保证所有用户的最低通信速率需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为海事用户提供广域通信覆盖，解决单一地面基站覆盖范围有限、卫星通信容量不足的问题，通过TBS与LEO卫星协同实现近海与远海的全域通信服务。

Method: 1) 基于海事通信环境分别分析近海与远海用户的传输速率和覆盖概率；2) 在特殊情况下进行渐近分析以理解关键参数影响；3) 设计优化算法，通过调整TBS和LEO卫星的发射功率，在满足所有用户最低速率约束下最大化近海用户覆盖概率。

Result: 大量数值分析验证了理论分析的准确性以及所提优化算法在海事通信系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的海陆集成海事通信系统及优化算法能够有效提升近海用户覆盖概率，同时保障近海与远海用户的最低通信速率需求，验证了理论分析的准确性和方法的可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an integrated terrestrial and satellite maritime communication system, where a shorebased terrestrial base station (TBS) and a low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite cooperatively provide wide-area communication services to maritime users. We conduct performance analysis for the integrated satellite-terrestrial maritime communication system. Specifically, we analyze the transmission rate and coverage probability of near-shore and off-shore users respectively according to the maritime communication environment. Besides, in order to better understand the impact of some key parameters, we also make asymptotic analysis in some special cases. Further, we design an optimization algorithm to maximize the coverage probability of near-shore users by adjusting the transmission power of TBS and the LEO satellite, while ensuring the both off-shore and near-shore users can meet the minimum communication rate requirements. Finally, extensive numerical analysis results verify the accuracy of the theoretical results and the effectiveness of the proposed optimization algorithm in the maritime communication system.

</details>


### [316] [Joint Communication and Sensing Design for Integrated Satellite-Terrestrial Maritime Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11164)
*Kaiwei Xiong,Xiaoming Chen,Ming Ying*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向6G的星地一体化海洋通感融合系统（ISTMS），通过差分进化算法和联合波束赋形优化，在满足目标定位精度和功率约束下最大化海洋用户和速率，仿真验证了所提算法在定位精度和传输速率方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将通感一体化技术引入海事通信，使船舶在通信的同时能够感知周围环境，保障航行安全。

Method: 构建了星地一体化海洋通感融合系统（ISTMS），地面基站服务近岸用户、低轨卫星服务远海用户，同时执行目标感知；基于差分进化方法提出感知算法以提升定位精度并降低资源消耗；通过联合波束赋形优化最大化海洋用户和速率，同时满足定位精度和功率约束。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提算法在定位精度和传输速率方面均优于基线算法。

Conclusion: 所提出的ISTMS和联合优化算法能有效实现海洋通信与感知的融合，提升系统性能。

Abstract: Joint communication and sensing has been a key technology in 6G. By integrating sensing into maritime communications, ships can communicate with the base station while sensing the surrounding environment to ensure safe navigation. In this paper, we introduce an integrated satellite-terrestrial maritime system (ISTMS) with joint communication and sensing based on the same radio-frequency signals. Specifically, the terrestrial base station (TBS) and low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite provide communication services for near-shore users (NSUs) and off-shore users (OSUs), respectively, while simultaneously performing target sensing. Based on a differential evolution method (DE), we propose a sensing algorithm, which can enhance the location accuracy and reduce resource consumption. Furthermore, we derive the key performance metrics for both communication and sensing. Through joint beamforming optimization of the TBS and LEO satellite, we maximize the sum rate of maritime users while satisfying target localization accuracy requirements and transmit power constraints. Finally, extensive simulation results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms in terms of location accuracy and transmission rate compared with the baseline algorithms.

</details>


### [317] [Compound Interference Recognition for LR-FHSS Satellite IoT Uplinks via Multi-Domain Instance Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11488)
*H. Xu,B. He,S. Wang,Y. Jiang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对LR-FHSS上行链路复合干扰识别问题，提出了一种多域实例融合的多实例多标签学习方法，在单到复合泛化和少样本复合干扰适应场景下分别将精确准确率提升14.71和14.81个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星物联网链路易受外部干扰，多种干扰分量共存会严重降低接收机可靠性并增加干扰抑制难度，现有方法要么仅针对单一干扰场景，要么将每种复合干扰组合视为独立类别，导致泛化能力有限或可扩展性差。

Method: 将LR-FHSS上行链路复合干扰识别建模为多实例多标签学习问题，提出多域实例融合方法，融合时频域和频域的局部实例并聚合其预测进行包级多标签识别；基于US915 LR-FHSS配置构建数据集，包含阴影莱斯衰落和时变多普勒效应。

Result: 在单到复合泛化场景下，整体精确准确率比最强基线提升14.71个百分点；在少样本复合干扰适应场景（r=1）下提升14.81个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提方法在复合干扰识别任务上显著优于现有基线，具有良好的泛化能力和少样本适应能力。

Abstract: Long range-frequency hopping spread spectrum (LR-FHSS) is a promising uplink physical layer for massive low Earth orbit satellite Internet of Things, where low power terminals report short packets from wide area regions with limited terrestrial infrastructure. However, satellite IoT links are exposed to external interference, and the coexistence of multiple interference components can severely degrade receiver reliability and complicate interference mitigation. Existing recognition methods either focus on single interference scenarios or treat each compound interference combination as an independent class, leading to limited generalization or poor scalability. To address this problem, this paper formulates LR-FHSS uplink compound interference recognition as a multi-instance multi-label learning problem and proposes a multi-domain instance fusion method. The proposed method fuses local instances from the time-frequency and frequency domains and aggregates their predictions for bag-level multi-label recognition. A dataset construction pipeline is developed based on the US915 LR-FHSS configuration and incorporates shadowed-Rician fading and time-varying Doppler to emulate practical satellite communication conditions. Considering the difficulty of obtaining labeled compound interference samples in practice, single-to-compound generalization and few-shot compound interference adaptation are investigated as two practical receiver deployment scenarios. Experimental results show that the proposed method improves the overall exact accuracy over the strongest baseline by 14.71 percentage points in single-to-compound generalization and by 14.81 percentage points in few-shot compound interference adaptation for $r=1$.

</details>


### [318] [Exact Cardinality And Nonredundant Parametrization Of Character-Polynomial Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11595)
*Felice Manganiello,Sitraka Randrianarivo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了特征多项式码（CP码）在扩域上存在的冗余问题，通过迹映射和分圆陪集识别了非单射的根源，确定了码的精确基数，并构建了无冗余的精细化多项式族，从而修正了CP码的参数并阐明了其代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始特征多项式码族在扩域上存在冗余——不同的多项式可能生成相同的码字，这导致码的参数不准确，需要识别并消除这种非单射性。

Method: 通过迹映射（trace map）和分圆陪集（cyclotomic cosets）分析非单射的根源，确定码的精确基数，并构建一个无冗余的精细化多项式族来参数化该码。

Result: 给出了CP码的修正参数，阐明了其代数结构。

Conclusion: 消除了CP码在扩域上的冗余，得到了正确的参数和更清晰的代数结构理解。

Abstract: Character-polynomial codes are constructed by evaluating finite field polynomials and mapping the results to complex roots of unity through additive characters. This paper shows that, over extension fields, the original polynomial family may contain redundancies: distinct polynomials can generate the same codeword. We identify the source of this non-injectivity through the trace map and cyclotomic cosets, determine the exact code cardinality, and construct a refined polynomial family that parametrizes the code without redundancy. These results give corrected parameters for CP codes and clarify their algebraic structure.

</details>


### [319] [Visibility-Region Coupling in XL-MIMO AGV Fleets: Triple-Role Modeling and Masked Beamforming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11781)
*Changhao He,Xiaojuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 针对智能港口中AGV车队的XL-MIMO系统，提出了一种考虑AGV三重角色（通信用户、金属散射体、阻挡物）导致的VR耦合信道模型，并开发了基于掩码加权MMSE的VR感知下行波束成形框架，实现了三倍以上的和速率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能港口中AGV车队的XL-MIMO通信面临金属集装箱环境导致的空间非平稳性问题，现有模型假设用户间VR独立，但AGV同时作为通信用户、金属散射体和阻挡物，导致用户信道和VR相互耦合，需要建立新的模型和方法。

Method: 提出了VR耦合信道模型以刻画AGV的三重角色效应；设计了基于掩码加权最小均方误差（WMMSE）的VR感知下行波束成形框架，通过掩码操作严格施加VR支撑约束并显著降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在真实智能港口场景的仿真中，该方法相比未考虑VR的基线方案实现了三倍以上的和速率提升，且随着AGV车队密度增加，性能增益更加显著。

Conclusion: 所提出的VR耦合信道模型和VR感知波束成形框架有效解决了智能港口XL-MIMO系统中AGV三重角色导致的通信挑战，显著提升了系统性能。

Abstract: Extremely large-scale multiple-input multiple-output (XL-MIMO) is a promising technology for supporting automated guided vehicle (AGV) fleets in smart port terminals. However, the metallic container environment induces spatial non-stationarity, whereby each AGV is visible to only a subset of the array, referred to as its visibility region (VR).
