We also extend the interface with ValueMemory, hybrid adaptive leaky integrate-and-fire controllers, candidate-constrained symbolic control trained through behaviour cloning and actor-critic reinforcement learning, and an RV32I base-integer semantic bridge. Together, these results establish a trace-verifiable framework for interpretable, low-precision and controllable neural execution.

</details>


### [355] [AgentAbstain: Do LLM Agents Know When Not to Act?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10059)
*Xun Liu,Yi Evie Zhang,Vira Kasprova,Parisa Rabbani,Pardis Sadat Zahraei,Tianyu Zhang,Ali Ebrahimpour-Boroojeny,Varun Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentAbstain，首个针对LLM代理系统**自主放弃执行（abstention）能力**的系统评估框架。核心是包含8类放弃场景、263对配对任务的基准测试，以及自动化生成管线的AbstainGen。实验表明，最佳模型仅在59.5%的配对任务上正确识别何时该做与不该做，且放弃能力与任务解决能力几乎无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理评估过度聚焦任务成功率，忽略了代理在模糊指令、冲突约束或工具故障时**应当放弃执行**的关键能力，导致不可逆误操作风险。

Method: (1) 构建基于代理原生场景的分类法，涵盖8类放弃场景（分为执行前推理和运行时发现两类）；(2) 设计配对任务基准：每对包含一个"应执行"任务和一个通过受控扰动（指令、工具或环境状态）产生的"应放弃"变体，共263对任务，覆盖42个可执行沙箱环境；(3) 提出AbstainGen全自动管线，端到端合成沙箱环境并生成配对任务，通过确定性回放和语义LLM裁判验证质量，支持按需重新生成。

Result: 在17个前沿LLM和4个代理框架上评估，最佳代理（Gemini 3.1 Pro）配对准确率仅59.5%；放弃能力与通用任务解决能力几乎无关；识别出"事后放弃"（执行不可逆操作后才意识到放弃触发条件）等典型失败模式。

Conclusion: 代理放弃执行是与任务解决能力正交的关键能力维度，单纯提升任务求解能力无法弥补该缺口；需要专门针对代理的自主放弃机制进行设计与评估。

Abstract: Agent systems based on large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for autonomous tasks, yet existing evaluations mostly focus on task success rather than whether agents know when to abstain. This gap poses real risks: under ambiguity, conflicting constraints, or tool failures, agents may execute unintended and irreversible actions. To close this gap, we present the first systematic evaluation framework for agentic abstention: the calibrated ability of tool-using LLM agents to recognize when not to act. At its core, AgentAbstain is a paired-task benchmark built on an agent-native taxonomy of 8 abstention scenarios across pre-execution reasoning and runtime discovery. It contains 263 paired tasks across 42 executable sandbox environments, where each pair consists of a should-act task and a should-abstain variant produced through a controlled perturbation to the instruction, tool, or environment state. To scale this paired design and resist data contamination, we propose AbstainGen, a fully automated pipeline that synthesizes sandbox environments and generates paired tasks end-to-end, validated by deterministic replay and semantic LLM judges; fresh task instances can be regenerated on demand, and three independent annotators rate 94-98% of sampled tasks as well-designed. Across 17 frontier LLMs in 4 agent harnesses, the best agent (Gemini 3.1 Pro) achieves only 59.5% paired accuracy (correct on both the act and abstain sides of each paired task). More importantly, abstention capability is largely independent of general task-solving capability, indicating that scaling task-solving alone will not close this gap. We further identify failure modes such as post-hoc abstention, in which agents execute irreversible actions before recognizing abstention triggers. Our code and dataset are open-sourced at agentabstain.github.io.

</details>


### [356] [From ambiguous utterances to governed reuse classes: canonicalization, quotient invariance, and conditional decidability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10069)
*Cosimo Spera,Ray Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在治理域中，语义缓存应基于数学刻画的商空间（对已解析会话话语的商）进行复用，而非基于嵌入相似性启发式。定义了阅读同一性、解析同一性和复用同一性三个递进关系，并证明复用同一性是解析映射到治理答案划分的核，从而形成严格的精化链。系统支持精确指称范式、聚合连接算子、策略可接纳性等，并确保在有限次信息性回复后终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义缓存基于嵌入相似性决定答案复用，缺乏对授权、版本控制以及"什么构成相同需求"的数学刻画。在治理域中，需要从数学上严格定义话语复用关系，而非依赖启发式相似度阈值。

Method: 定义了三个独立的关系（阅读同一性、解析同一性、复用同一性），形成精化链；证明在非退化条件下该链是严格的；复用同一性被证明是解析映射到治理答案划分的核；基于此构建了完整的管道系统，包括精确指称范式、聚合连接算子、策略可接纳性等。

Result: 复用同一性恰好是解析映射到治理答案划分的核，因此复用商空间是由该划分诱导的话语侧对象；复用同一性授权治理查询键及其认证答案空间；特定答案的复用需要解析同一性或适用性证书；整个管道相对于不可信提案层是整体可计算的；策略对任意提案者可接纳。

Conclusion: 语义缓存应基于数学刻画的商空间进行复用，而非嵌入相似性启发式；系统在理论层面上保证了正确性和终止性，不依赖事实接地或意图保真度。

Abstract: Semantic caching defines answer reuse on embedding similarity: two utterances share a stored answer when a similarity score clears a threshold, with no notion of authorization, versioning, or of what makes two demands the same. This note changes the object on which reuse is defined: in a governed domain, reuse should operate on a mathematically characterized quotient of resolved conversational demands, not on a similarity heuristic. Three independently defined relations on resolved utterances -- reading identity, resolution identity, and reuse identity -- form a refinement chain, strict under realized nondegeneracy conditions checkable on deployment logs; the pipeline's outputs are invariant along the chain, and reuse identity is exactly the kernel of the resolution map into the governed answer partition, so the reuse quotient is the utterance-side object that partition induces, not a relabeling of it. Reuse identity licenses the governed query key and its certified answer space; reuse of a particular answer requires resolution identity or an applicability certificate. The supporting layer is stated at exactly the strength proved: exact-denotation normal forms; join aggregation as a design operator, with closure-stable cells characterizing no-escape; total computability of the full pipeline relative to an untrusted proposal layer; policy admissibility for arbitrary proposers -- and provably not factual grounding or intent fidelity; and elicitation terminating after finitely many informative replies, sound under target consistency.

</details>


### [357] [MAG: A Web-Agent Benchmark and Harness for Multimodal Action and Guide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10079)
*Chengguang Gan,Hanjun Wei,Yunhao Liang,Zhixi Cai,Qinghao Zhang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MAG基准，首个将任务执行与指南生成统一为多模态动作与指南任务的评估体系，通过截图上的Set-of-Mark元素选择和原始像素坐标两种接地方案，构建了完整的数据标注、训练、评估框架，并设计了专家轨迹增强的GRPO训练方法，将9B智能体的成功率从6.9%提升至13.2%，同时改善了指南质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将Web自动化代理动作和指南文本生成视为两个独立问题，且大多使用DOM或可访问性树等文本化页面表示而非人类实际操作的渲染截图，缺乏一个统一的任务执行与指南生成的基准。

Method: 提出MAG基准，统一任务执行与指南写作为多模态动作与指南任务；采用Set-of-Mark元素选择和原始像素坐标两种截图接地方案；构建完整框架覆盖LLM辅助+人工验证的标注、训练、在线环境评估及联合指标；设计专家轨迹增强的GRPO训练方法。

Result: 最强模型完成率不足40%；GRPO训练将9B智能体成功率从6.9%提升至13.2%，同时改善了指南质量。

Conclusion: MAG基准为Web自动化代理的统一任务执行与指南生成提供了有效的评估框架，专家轨迹增强的GRPO方法在较大幅度提升性能的同时仍有很大改进空间。

Abstract: Digital Adoption Platforms (DAPs) are embedded overlays widely used on web systems to guide users through operations inside a page, helping them get started with unfamiliar interfaces quickly. Completing a real task, however, rarely means clicking a few buttons on a single page: it takes a sequence of actions that unfolds across changing page states. Prior studies have also treated automated web agent actions and guide text generation as two separate problems, and most of them feed models textual page representations such as the DOM or accessibility trees rather than the rendered screens that humans actually operate on. In this work we introduce MAG, the first benchmark that unifies task execution and guide writing into a single Multimodal Action and Guide task, with two grounding schemes over screenshots: Set-of-Mark element selection and raw pixel coordinates. We further build a complete harness for this compound task, covering annotation with LLM assistance and human verification, training, evaluation in live environments, and joint metrics for actions and guides. With this harness we evaluate frontier API models and open multimodal models, and report detailed analyses. Finally, we design a GRPO training method augmented with expert trajectories, which nearly doubles the success rate of a supervised 9B agent (from 6.9% to 13.2%) and improves guide quality at the same time. Even the strongest model completes fewer than 40% of the tasks, leaving ample room for future research.

</details>


### [358] [Looped State-Space Language Models with Adaptive Exit-State Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10110)
*Zhenxuan Yu,Takeshi Kojima,Yutaka Matsuo,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究循环Mamba和循环混合Mamba-Transformer架构，通过重复共享模块引入显式有限深度循环计算。在受控推理任务中，循环Mamba优于参数匹配的非循环基线；在语言模型预训练中，循环模型在少量独特参数下保持竞争力，但更深的非循环模型在严格iso-FLOPs比较中仍有验证困惑度优势。此外，将Ouro的两阶段退出门适应于循环Mamba进行阈值控制的循环步输出选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索状态空间语言模型（如Mamba）是否也能像Transformer一样从更大的计算深度而非额外独立参数中获益。

Method: 提出Looped Mamba和Looped Hybrid Mamba-Transformer架构，通过重复共享Mamba（或混合）块实现显式有限深度循环计算；在受控推理任务（模运算、p-hop归纳）和语言模型预训练（iso-参数和iso-FLOPs协议）中评估；并引入Ouro的两阶段退出门进行自适应退出状态选择。

Result: 循环Mamba在受控推理任务中优于参数匹配的非循环基线；在语言模型预训练中，循环模型以更少独特参数保持竞争力，但更深非循环模型在iso-FLOPs下验证困惑度更优。自适应退出选择可提升中等深度性能，但实际推理加速需额外状态处理机制。

Conclusion: 状态空间模型也能从循环深度计算中获益，但参数共享与有效深度之间存在权衡；目前规模下自适应退出选择的实际好处有限。

Abstract: Recent work on looped language models suggests that many reasoning problems benefit from greater computational depth rather than from additional independent parameters. Existing studies, however, focus almost exclusively on Transformer backbones, leaving open whether this principle also applies to state-space language models. We investigate Looped Mamba and Looped Hybrid Mamba-Transformer architectures, which repeatedly apply a shared Mamba (or hybrid) block to introduce explicit finite-depth recurrent computation. On two controlled reasoning tasks-Mano (modular-arithmetic manipulation) and p-hop induction-Looped Mamba consistently outperforms parameter-matched non-looped baselines and, in several settings, matches or exceeds non-looped models of equal effective depth. We then extend the study to language model pre-training under matched iso-parameter and iso-FLOPs protocols, which jointly disentangle the effects of parameter sharing and effective depth: looped models remain competitive on downstream benchmarks with substantially fewer distinct parameters, although deeper non-looped models retain an advantage in validation perplexity under strict iso-FLOPs comparisons. Finally, we adapt Ouro's two-stage exit gate to Looped Mamba for threshold-controlled selection among recurrent-step outputs. Since all recurrent steps are still executed, the selected exit step represents prediction depth rather than reduced wall-clock computation. At the scales studied, adaptive exit-state selection improves downstream performance at intermediate depths, while actual inference-time savings require additional state-handling mechanisms.

</details>


### [359] [Dynamic Agent Skills: A Lifecycle Survey and Taxonomy of Evolving Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10113)
*Yubo Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文对124篇2023-2026年的研究进行了系统调查，提出了一种分类法驱动的视角，将技能库视为需要生命周期管理、验证和演化的制品存储系统。论文构建了六感分类法区分不同技能类型、八阶段生命周期架构描述技能演化过程，以及十算子词汇表用于统一比较，并给出了证据分级的模式总结与未解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理越来越多地在模型外部存储可复用的过程（即"技能"），但这些技能库如何随时间演化缺乏系统性的分类与分析框架。现有文献中"技能"一词指代的结构化制品差异巨大，且缺乏统一的术语和设计模式来描述技能库的动态变化过程。

Method: 对124篇2023-2026年的论文进行系统性审计，构建三个分析工具：（1）六感分类法——区分当前文献中六种结构不同的"技能"制品；（2）八阶段生命周期架构——识别证据获取、提案、验证/准入、存储、检索/组合、维护、蒸馏/可移植性和治理八个阶段的设计决策；（3）轻量级技能记录模式与十算子词汇表——提供统一术语比较库更新操作。

Result: 总结出若干证据分级的模式与关键洞见：准入和修复反复被证实为重要环节；验证器质量显著影响技能感知强化学习的效果；扁平检索在库规模增长时会退化；当前基准测试仍缺乏对库演化轨迹、使用-效用差距和安全面的充分报告。

Conclusion: 提出具体的报告标准和开放问题，推动未来将动态技能作为不断变化的库（而非静态的提示或工具集合）进行评估和研究。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly store reusable procedures outside the model. These reusable procedures are often called \emph{skills}: they may be code functions, natural-language instructions, SKILL.md packages, workflow graphs, or learned adapters that a future agent can retrieve and invoke. This taxonomy-driven survey asks how such skill libraries change over time. Across a $124$-paper $2023$--$2026$ audit set, we synthesize dynamic skill systems as \emph{lifecycle-managed, verified, evolving artifact stores}: agents collect evidence from interaction, propose skill updates, verify and admit candidates, organize them for retrieval and composition, repair or prune stale entries, and govern sharing through provenance and rollback. We organize the literature around three survey tools. First, a $\text{six}$-sense taxonomy distinguishes the structurally different artifacts called ``skills'' in current papers. Second, an $\text{eight}$-stage lifecycle architecture identifies the recurring design decisions behind evidence acquisition, proposal, verification/admission, storage, retrieval/composition, maintenance, distillation/portability, and governance. Third, a lightweight skill-record schema and $\text{ten}$-operator vocabulary provide common terms for comparing library updates without elevating them into a separate method contribution. Using this structure, we synthesize evidence-graded patterns with explicit caveats: admission and repair are repeatedly important, verifier quality materially affects skill-aware RL, flat retrieval can degrade as libraries grow, and current benchmarks still under-report library trajectories, usage--utility gaps, and safety surfaces. We close with concrete reporting standards and open problems for evaluating dynamic skills as changing libraries rather than static prompt or tool collections.

</details>


### [360] [IdeaTrail: Full-Process Agent Trajectories for Scientific Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10144)
*Hengquan Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了\method，一个面向科学构思与提案生成的多轮过程轨迹数据集。该数据集通过“生成器-顾问”合成循环，从高质量研究论文和提案出发，反向生成包含工具使用、证据获取、中间产物演变及最终构思/提案的多轮研究过程数据，为科研智能体提供过程监督的训练资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源仅覆盖科研工作流的单个环节（如文本生成、文献搜索等），缺乏同时记录工具使用、证据获取、中间产物演变以及最终构思或提案级别的联合数据集，难以支撑对完整科研过程的建模与智能体训练。

Method: 采用“反向到正向”（reverse-to-forward）的合成流程：以人类筛选的高质量研究论文和提案文件为隐藏目标，利用生成器（Generator）产生可见的多轮轨迹（包含动作、观察、产物编辑），同时顾问（Advisor）访问完整生成上下文，检查轨迹的接地性、因果顺序、自然性以及是否存在对隐藏目标的信息泄露。

Result: 构建了\method数据集，每个实例记录从证据收集到构思选择或提案构建的完整研究过程，提供了与真实科研产物对齐的多轮研究数据，同时保留了研究实践中不确定性、证据使用和阶段性收敛的特点。

Conclusion: \method不仅提供了一个数据集，更提供了一套通用的过程监督数据合成方案，可用于训练面向科研过程感知的智能体。

Abstract: Scientific research is a complex, multi-stage workflow rather than a single act of text generation. The ideation process typically emerges through literature search, paper reading, tool use, claim checking, cross-paper synthesis, brainstorming, rejection of weak directions, and iterative writing. Existing resources capture individual components of this process, but datasets that jointly record tool use, evidence acquisition, intermediate artifact evolution, and idea- or proposal-level endpoints remain limited. This report introduces \method, a multi-turn process-trajectory dataset for scientific ideation and proposal generation. Each instance records a research process from evidence gathering to either idea selection or proposal construction. Rather than freely fabricating trajectories, \method starts from human-selected high-quality research papers and proposal artifacts and uses a Generator--Advisor synthesis loop. The Generator produces the visible trajectory through actions, observations, and artifact edits, while the Advisor has access to the full generation context and checks grounding, causal order, naturalness, and leakage from hidden targets. This reverse-to-forward procedure produces multi-turn research data that remains aligned with real scientific artifacts while approximating the uncertainty, evidence use, and staged convergence of research practice. \method provides both a dataset and a general recipe for synthesizing process-supervision data for scientific research agents.

</details>


### [361] [UNIT: Unleash Large Language Models Potential for Graph Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10159)
*Tairan Huang,Yili Wang,Beibei Hu,Yiting Shi,Qiutong Li,Changlong He,Jianliang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UNIT框架，通过微调大语言模型、不确定感知锚点生成与结构融合建模，解决了图持续学习中的语义-结构分离与知识迁移不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图持续学习方法面临两大挑战：1) 语义-结构分离，图方法擅长建模拓扑关系但忽略深层语义；2) 知识迁移不平衡，模型无法有效利用早期任务学到的通用知识来帮助后续新任务。

Method: 1) 仅在第一个任务上微调大语言模型，弥合预训练LLM语料与目标任务数据集之间的分布差距；2) 提出不确定感知锚点生成机制，有效保留跨任务的代表性知识；3) 引入结构融合建模，将图拓扑信息显式整合到语义信息中，增强语义理解与结构建模的协作能力。

Result: 在持续学习任务上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过LLM与图结构信息的有效融合，UNIT框架成功解决了图持续学习中的语义-结构分离与知识迁移不平衡问题。

Abstract: In real-world multimodal web scenarios, graph-structured data often arrives in a streaming manner, making graph continual learning a crucial paradigm for continuously modeling such evolving structures. However, existing graph continual learning methods still face two fundamental challenges. 1) semantic-structural separation, where the graph-based methods excel at modeling topological relationships but neglect deep semantics. 2) imbalanced knowledge transfer, where existing models fail to effectively leverage general knowledge gained from early tasks to benefit subsequent new tasks. To address above issues, we propose a novel framework, \textbf{UN}leash Large Language Models PotentIal for Graph ConTinual Learning (UNIT). By fine-tuning large language model only on the first task, we bridge the distributional gap between the pre-trained LLM corpus and the target task dataset to enhance the adaptability of LLMs for graph-structured tasks. Meanwhile, we propose an uncertain-aware anchor generation mechanism to effectively preserve representative knowledge across tasks, avoiding the neglect of universal knowledge learned from previous tasks. Additionally, we introduce structural confluence modeling to explicitly integrates graph topology information into semantic information, enhancing the collaborative capabilities between semantic understanding and structural modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance in the graph continual learning task.

</details>


### [362] [GRATE: Temporal Extensions for Inductive KG Foundation Models via Gated Rotary Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10197)
*Jiaxin Pan,Osama Mohammed,Daniel Hernández,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GRATE模型，通过零参数的门控旋转注意力机制对时间知识图谱进行时序编码，在保持结构可迁移性的同时实现跨数据集的时间归纳迁移，并在新构建的归纳迁移基准上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱基础模型（如Ultra、Trix）虽能实现强归纳迁移，但扩展到时间知识图谱时仍面临挑战：现有时序模型参数与数据集特定的实体、关系或时间戳绑定，无法迁移到词汇表不相交的时间知识图谱上。

Method: 提出GRATE（门控旋转注意力时序编码），作为实体侧消息函数，不引入可学习参数；通过相对时间差旋转边消息，并应用查询条件化的门控选择时间相关信号。集成到NBFNet风格的知识图谱基础模型中，保持结构可迁移性。同时构建GDELTIndT和WIKIIndT两个归纳迁移基准套件，涵盖内插和外推场景。

Result: 在归纳迁移基准和留出法预测数据集上，单个联合预训练的GRATE检查点在大多数设置下优于静态基础模型。

Conclusion: GRATE成功将知识图谱基础模型的归纳迁移能力扩展到时间维度，通过零参数时序编码实现了跨数据集的时间知识图谱迁移。

Abstract: Knowledge graph foundation models such as Ultra and Trix achieve strong inductive transfer by learning relation-graph representations that generalise to unseen entities and relations. Extending this transferability to temporal knowledge graphs (TKGs) remains challenging: existing temporal models tie their parameters to dataset-specific entities, relations, or timestamps and are not designed to transfer to TKGs with disjoint vocabularies. We propose GRATE (Gated Rotary Attention for Temporal Encoding), an entity-side message function that adds no learnable parameters and encodes time through relative time differences by rotating each edge message according to its time gap to the query and applying a query-conditioned gate to select temporally relevant signals. GRATE integrates into NBFNet-style KG foundation models while preserving structural transferability. Existing TKG benchmarks evaluate within shared train/test vocabularies and cannot directly test cross-dataset temporal transfer; we therefore construct GDELTIndT and WIKIIndT, inductive transfer benchmark suites with disjoint entities, relations, and timestamps spanning both interpolation and extrapolation. Across these benchmarks and held-out forecasting datasets, a single jointly pretrained GRATE checkpoint improves over the static base model in most settings.

</details>


### [363] [KGCQual: An Interpretable Framework for Evaluating the Knowledge Graph Construction Quality from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10212)
*Nipun Misra,Vikranth Udandarao,Aanchal Gupta,Yogender Kumar,Manuj Mukherjee,Raghava Mutharaju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种可解释的KG质量评估指标KGCQual，从实体和关系两个层面衡量自动构建的知识图谱与源文本的语义保真度，在多个数据集和抽取系统上验证其有效性，并证明评估结果与下游链接预测性能显著相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱自动构建系统常引入虚假或不完整三元组，而现有评估依赖任务特定指标或小规模人工验证，缺乏对抽取图结构语义保真度的深入洞察。

Method: 提出KGCQual指标，包含两个互补组件：(1)实体层面评估：完整性、解析质量和连通性；(2)关系层面评估：谓词保持度和多样性，使用词汇相似性、依存句法对齐和轻量级否定处理。通过消融实验和下游链接预测任务进行验证。

Result: 在WebNLG、TinyButMighty、BenchIE等多个数据集和多个SOTA三元组抽取系统上，KGCQual能够可靠识别现有指标遗漏的遗漏、冗余和结构偏差；消融实验验证了名词/动词成分的有效性；KGCQual得分与下游链接预测性能显著相关。

Conclusion: 提供了一个可扩展、模型无关且可解释的KG质量评估框架，为标准化评估自动KG构建方法奠定基础。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are increasingly constructed through automated extraction pipelines; however, such systems often introduce spurious or incomplete triples, which degrade downstream performance. Existing evaluation practices rely heavily on task-specific metrics or small-scale manual verification, offering limited insight into the structural and semantic fidelity of extracted graphs. We propose a novel, interpretable metric for intrinsic KG quality assessment that measures how closely an automatically extracted graph approximates an "ideal" graph capturing the key noun phrases, predicate relations, and basic linguistic phenomena such as negation expressed in the source text. Our framework integrates two complementary components: (1) an entity-level assessment that evaluates completeness, resolution quality, and connectivity, and (2) a relation-level assessment that judges predicate preservation and multiplicity using lexical similarity, dependency-parse alignment, and light-weight negation handling to ensure semantic faithfulness. We evaluate our metric across multiple state-of-the-art triple extraction systems and datasets, including WebNLG, TinyButMighty, and BenchIE, demonstrating that it reliably identifies omissions, redundancy, and structural deviations that existing metrics overlook. Our work offers a scalable, model-agnostic, and interpretable framework for comparing automated KG construction methods and provides a foundation for standardised evaluation. We further validate the metric through an ablation study isolating noun and verb components, and a downstream evaluation showing that KGCQual scores correlate significantly with link prediction performance on the same extracted KGs. The code repository is available at https://github.com/kracr/kg-quality-metric.

</details>


### [364] [When Are Sparse Feature Interventions Actually Localized? Matched Evaluation for SAE-Based Safety Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10226)
*Daming Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征作为安全相关行为的局部控制手柄的有效性。通过引入匹配一致性门控评估协议，发现SAE特征消融仅在狭窄的范围内有效：top800特征在低至中等目标效应下具有较低的总体扰动和竞争力，但top1600特征失去效用，top3200特征主要导致一致性崩溃。结果表明SAE的安全干预应被视为依赖机制的控制机制，而非均匀局部化的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE特征是否真正作为安全相关行为的局部化控制手柄存在疑问，因为表面成功可能源于弱干预、不匹配的基线、模型鲁棒性或退化输出（自动安全评判器标记为不安全但实际上并非有意义的有害顺从）。需要更严格的评估协议来区分真正的安全干预效果与虚假信号。

Method: 引入匹配一致性门控评估协议：在匹配的目标效应点比较不同方法，主要目标指标仅计数那些同时被评判器判定为不安全且输出连贯的有害顺从。将该协议应用于Gemma-2-9B-it模型上Gemma Scope第20层残差SAE的三个提示子集，并辅以人工审计和特征诊断分析。

Result: SAE top800在低至中等目标效应下具有较低的总体扰动和竞争性效用；SAE top1600相对于匹配的稠密拒绝方向基线失去效用；SAE top3200主要导致一致性崩溃。人工审计确认一致性门控移除了仅不安全的人为伪影。特征诊断表明有用机制由稳定的拒绝对齐特征头驱动，其激活分离度随秩快速衰减。

Conclusion: 基于SAE的安全干预应被视为依赖机制的控制机制来评估，而非假设为均匀局部化的控制手柄。SAE特征消融仅在有限范围内有用，超出该范围则效用下降或导致输出质量崩溃。

Abstract: We evaluate when sparse autoencoder (SAE) features act as localized control handles for safety-relevant behavior. This question is difficult because apparent success can arise from weak interventions, mismatched baselines, model robustness, or degenerate outputs that automated safety judges mark as unsafe without representing meaningful harmful compliance. We introduce a matched coherence-gated evaluation protocol for runtime safety interventions: methods are compared at matched target-effect points, and the primary target metric counts harmful compliance only when an output is both judge-unsafe and coherent. Applying this protocol to three prompt splits on Gemma-2-9B-it with a Gemma Scope layer-20 residual SAE, we find that SAE feature ablation has a narrow useful regime. SAE top800 reaches a low-to-mid target effect with lower total perturbation and competitive utility, but SAE top1600 loses utility relative to a matched dense refusal-direction baseline, and SAE top3200 primarily induces coherence collapse. Human audit confirms that coherence gating removes unsafe-only artifacts, and feature diagnostics show that the useful regime is driven by a stable head of refusal-aligned features whose activation separation decays rapidly with rank. These results argue that SAE-based safety interventions should be evaluated as regime-dependent control mechanisms rather than assumed to be uniformly localized.

</details>


### [365] [Behavioural Signatures of Risk-Sensitive Decision-Making in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10251)
*Xuankun Rong,Wenke Huang,Bo Du,Dacheng Tao,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过无限制德州扑克框架，系统研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在不确定性决策中的风险偏好行为。研究发现前沿LLMs表现出稳定且模型特异的风险特征，形成从保守到激进的决策光谱，并在对手变化、全局风险压力和个人资源约束下呈现结构化但异质的适应模式。这些发现为审计风险敏感决策提供了行为学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LLMs在不确定性决策中是否表现出稳定且可解释的行为规律，特别是类似人类的风险偏好与情境依赖调整的行为结构。

Method: 采用基于无限制德州扑克（No-limit Texas Hold'em）的受控多模型框架，通过参与度（Participation）和主动性（Proactiveness）量化风险行为，在同构自对弈和异构混合模型交互中进行分析。

Result: 前沿LLMs表现出稳定、模型特异的风险特征，形成从保守到激进的决策光谱；该特征在对手变化时保持稳健；在全局风险压力和个人资源约束下，模型表现出从广泛行为收缩到选择性降级和近似不变行为的结构化异质适应。

Conclusion: LLMs不仅在基线风险倾向上存在差异，在响应的风险信号类型和调整灵活性上也不同，为交互环境中风险敏感决策的审计提供了行为学基础。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in decision support, it is important to understand whether their choices under uncertainty exhibit stable and interpretable behavioural regularities. Human decision-making combines relatively persistent risk preferences with context-dependent adjustment, yet it remains unclear whether analogous behavioural structure can be observed in LLM-based decision systems. Here we examine this question using a controlled multi-model framework based on no-limit Texas Hold'em, where behaviour is quantified by Participation, measuring voluntary engagement in uncertain opportunities, and Proactiveness, measuring pre-flop risk escalation. Across homogeneous self-play and heterogeneous mixed-model interactions, frontier LLMs exhibit stable, model-specific risk profiles, forming a spectrum from conservative to aggressive decision styles. These profiles remain largely robust under changing opponent composition, while the most conservative and most aggressive models diverge further in mixed settings. Under global risk pressure and personal resource constraint, models adapt in structured but heterogeneous ways, ranging from broad behavioural contraction to selective de-escalation and near-invariant behaviour. These findings suggest that LLMs differ not only in baseline risk disposition, but also in the risk signals they respond to and the flexibility with which they adjust, providing a behavioural basis for auditing risk-sensitive decision-making in interactive settings. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/XuankunRong/AgentTexasPoker.

</details>


### [366] [Information-seeking failures of large language models in agentic clinical reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10275)
*Krischan Braitsch,Laura K. Schmalbrock,Theresa Weltermann,Andrew F. Berdel,Isabella Miller,Kai Tran,Michael Heider,Sabrina Kraus,Florian Bassermann,Jacqueline Lammert,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Marcus Makowski,Lisa C. Adams,Keno K. Bressem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前最先进的大语言模型在临床肿瘤诊断中的主要限制并非医学知识不足，而是在不确定性下系统性地缺乏主动信息寻求能力。在血液肿瘤学评估框架中，模型需主动请求临床数据，最佳模型准确率仅68%；信息利用率是诊断准确率的最强预测因子，但在最后一轮从57%骤降至26%；推理轨迹与临床推理评分高但去相关；错误分析揭示搜索满足、锚定和过早闭合等认知偏差是主要失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估体系仅测试模型在静态知识考试中的表现，无法反映临床实践中主动收集信息、应对不确定性的核心能力。作者希望构建一个更贴近真实临床场景的评估范式，系统性地测试LLM在不确定性下的信息寻求与临床决策能力。

Method: 构建血液肿瘤学智能体评估框架：模型需在三轮交互中主动请求临床数据（包括病史、体检、实验室、影像、分子检测等），之后提交诊断和治疗方案。在32个前沿模型上进行测试，分析信息利用率、推理轨迹质量与诊断准确率之间的关系，并对错误模式进行认知偏差分类。

Result: 最佳模型准确率仅68%；信息利用率与诊断准确率强相关（R=0.69, P<0.001）；信息利用率从首轮57%降至最后一轮26%，关键分子和细胞遗传学数据被遗漏；临床推理评分与准确率去相关；主要错误模式包括搜索满足、锚定和过早闭合等认知偏差。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在临床肿瘤学中的主要瓶颈不是医学知识不足，而是在不确定性条件下的系统性信息寻求失败，其表现出的认知偏差与人类临床新手相似，提示需要从根本上改进模型的主动探索和决策机制。

Abstract: Large language models achieve high scores on medical knowledge assessments, yet clinical reasoning requires actively deciding what to investigate under uncertainty. We developed an agentic evaluation framework in hematologic oncology in which models must proactively request clinical data across three sequential rounds before committing to a diagnosis and treatment plan. Across 32 frontier models, the best achieved only 68% overall accuracy. Information utilization, the fraction of available data actually requested, was the strongest predictor of diagnostic accuracy (R = 0.69, P < 0.001), yet utilization collapsed from 57% to 26% in the final round, leaving molecular and cytogenetic data critical for treatment selection unexamined. Reasoning traces scored high on a clinical reasoning rubric (91% above threshold) but decorrelated from accuracy, revealing a gap between locally coherent rationales and globally correct conclusions. Error analysis identified search satisficing, anchoring and premature closure as the dominant failure modes, the same cognitive biases that characterize novice clinicians under dual-process models of diagnostic reasoning. These findings demonstrate that the primary limitation of current models in clinical oncology is not insufficient medical knowledge but a systematic failure of information-seeking under uncertainty.

</details>


### [367] [Can Agentic Trading Systems Pay for Their Own Intelligence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10286)
*Qiqi Duan,Changlun Li,Chen Wang,Fan Zhang,Mengxiang Wang,Dayi Miao,Peixian Ma,Jiangpeng Yan,Liyuan Chen,Shuoling Liu,Preslav Nakov,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TradeLens是一个用于评估LLM交易代理的追踪诊断工具包，通过重构交易轨迹、归因利润与成本，诊断智能是否能转化为可测量利润。实验发现不同模型存在不同失败模式（如DeepSeek-V3.2的资产选择差、GLM-4.7的时机判断负向），且资本规模、交易频率等仅通过放大或削弱决策归因的时机价值来影响结果，重新定义了评估范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM交易代理评估仅关注性能指标，很少检验"代理可行性"——即动态LLM决策是否能将其产生的成本转化为可测量的增量利润。

Method: 提出TradeLens，一个基于追踪记录、运行时轨迹和部署配置的诊断工具包。通过重构交易轨迹、将利润和成本归因于可解释证据，诊断代理是否以及为何为其智能付出代价。

Result: 不同模型存在不同失败模式：DeepSeek-V3.2资产选择差，GLM-4.7时机判断负向。资本规模、交易频率和架构仅通过放大或削弱决策归因的时机价值来影响结果。

Conclusion: 评估LLM交易代理应从以能力为中心的性能排名转向基于追踪的智能-利润转化诊断。代理可行性取决于智能向利润的转化效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used in trading systems, where model reasoning, tool use, and continual decisions incur costs that are expected to produce trading value. Existing evaluations typically report performance metrics, but rarely examine agentic viability: whether dynamic LLM-mediated decisions convert their induced costs into measurable incremental profit. To apply this criterion, we introduce TradeLens, a trace-grounded diagnostic toolkit for evaluating agentic trading systems from their trading records, runtime traces, and deployment configurations. It reconstructs trading trajectories, attributes profit and cost to interpretable evidence, and diagnoses whether and why an agent pays for its own intelligence. We conduct extensive analysis across backbone models, capital scales, trading frequencies, and system architectures, together with deployment discussion. Our results show that viability hinges on intelligence-to-profit conversion: models exhibit different failure patterns, such as poor asset selection in DeepSeek-V3.2 and negative timing in GLM-4.7, while capital scale, trading frequency, and architecture matter only by amplifying or degrading decision-attributed timing value. These findings reframe the evaluation of LLM-based trading agents from capability-centric performance ranking to trace-grounded diagnosis of intelligence-to-profit conversion. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TradeLens.

</details>


### [368] [SPARK: Susceptibility-Guided Profiling and Steering of Latent Reasoning States in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10296)
*Dongxu Zhang,Yiding Sun,Zihao Guo,Xiangyang Yang,Kai Tang,Lin Chen,Cheng Tan,Jihua Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPARK利用隐藏状态响应诊断LLM是否进入有效推理状态，通过长度控制的易感性（length-controlled susceptibility）分离输入规模效应与残差推理激活，并结合跨层协调选择推理活跃锚点和欠激活困难样本，实现轻量级测试时推理引导。在Qwen3系列模型上，MATH-500准确率提升约2.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅从最终答案评估推理失败，无法揭示失败原因（能力缺失、推理轨迹不稳定或未能激活已有推理状态）；提示工程和基准评估仅作用于输出层，通用激活引导方法使用全局方向而无法诊断哪些样本需要干预。

Method: 1) 提出长度控制的易感性（length-controlled susceptibility）以消除提示长度对隐藏状态易感性的混淆；2) 结合跨层协调信号选择推理活跃锚点（reasoning-active anchors）和欠激活的困难样本；3) 基于诊断结果进行轻量级测试时引导（test-time steering）。使用FRONTIER-4.5K作为受控编程推理套件进行潜在剖面分析和难度感知分析。

Result: Qwen3系列模型一致提升：MATH-500上Qwen3-4B准确率从82.0%提升至84.6%，Qwen3-8B从82.4%提升至85.6%。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态易感性不仅可作为推理失败的诊断信号，还能为针对性的测试时干预提供实用指导。

Abstract: Reasoning failures in large language models (LLMs) are usually evaluated from final answers, but a wrong answer does not reveal why the model failed. The same incorrect output may reflect missing capability, an unstable reasoning trajectory, or a failure to activate a reasoning state that is already available in the frozen model. Existing prompting and benchmark-based evaluation methods mostly operate at the output level, while generic activation-steering methods typically apply global directions without diagnosing which examples require intervention. In this paper, we introduce SPARK, which uses hidden-state response to diagnose whether a model internally enters an effective reasoning state and to guide lightweight test-time steering. The key observation is that raw hidden-state susceptibility is strongly confounded by prompt length, especially in programmatic and algorithmic reasoning where harder serialized instances naturally become longer. SPARK therefore uses length-controlled susceptibility to separate input-scale effects from residual reasoning activation, and combines this signal with cross-layer coordination to select reasoning-active anchors and under-activated hard examples. We use FRONTIER-4.5K as a controlled programmatic reasoning suite for latent profiling and difficulty-aware analysis, and evaluate SPARK-Steering on GSM8K and MATH-500 with forward-only benchmark profiling. Our method improves Qwen3 series models consistently; on MATH-500, accuracy rises from 82.0% to 84.6% for Qwen3-4B and from 82.4% to 85.6% for Qwen3-8B. These results suggest that susceptibility can serve not only as a diagnostic signal for reasoning failures, but also as a practical guide for targeted test-time intervention.

</details>


### [369] [Measure the Sim-to-Real Gap: Designing an Affordable Real-World Benchmark Platform for Reinforcement Learning in AIoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10309)
*Rongping Zhou,Omid Tavallaie,Shuaijun Chen,Albert Y. Zomaya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于AIoT领域Sim-to-Real迁移研究的低成本基准平台（<USD 400），通过硬件模拟键盘在边缘设备上玩游戏，实验表明模拟训练模型部署后性能下降1160%，而真实环境DQN训练可达人类水平的38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL研究多依赖仿真环境，但Sim-to-Real迁移存在显著性能差距，且缺乏针对AIoT的通用Sim-to-Real基准平台，亟需一个低成本、低风险的标准化评估平台。

Method: 构建了真实AIoT平台：边缘设备上的智能体通过硬件模拟键盘控制主机玩游戏，依赖视觉输入，总成本低于400美元；支持仿真训练和真实环境训练两种模式。

Result: 仿真训练的智能体部署到真实环境后性能下降1160%（相对于人类水平）；真实环境DQN训练10百万步后达到人类水平的38%。

Conclusion: 该平台可作为AIoT领域Sim-to-Real迁移的定性/定量评估基准，为提升RL在真实AIoT系统中的表现提供研究基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is commonly employed to enhance the performance of autonomous systems, including the Autonomous Internet of Things (AIoT). However, the trial-and-error nature of RL, when conducted in real-world environments, is costly and hazardous in some scenarios. Consequently, the majority of RL research is conducted in simulation. This reliance introduces challenges related to the Sim-to-Real transferability. Evaluating the Sim-to-Real algorithmic robustness and the Sim-to-Real gap is a critical prerequisite for research aimed at improving RL performance in the real world. Therefore, industries such as robotics have developed concurrent simulation and physical platforms to facilitate this research. However, a universal Sim-to-Real benchmark platform for AIoT does not currently exist. To address these concerns, we developed a real-world AIoT platform for studying RL in AIoT. On this platform, an agent deployed on an edge device plays video games on a separate host computer via a hardware-emulated keyboard, guided by vision input. This platform uses commercially available components costing less than USD 400, together with two computers. Because the system's objective is game score maximization, it inherently mitigates safety risks associated with real-world RL deployments. Experimental results show the simulation-trained agent suffers a 1160% performance degradation relative to the human-level performance after real-world deployment, indicating a significant Sim-to-Real gap. Direct real-world training using the deep Q-network (DQN) algorithm achieves approximately 38% of human-level performance after 10 million training steps, demonstrating the feasibility of RL under real-world conditions. These results suggest that the proposed Sim-to-Real benchmark platform provides a substantial foundation for qualitative and quantitative evaluations of RL in real-world AIoT systems.

</details>


### [370] [Comparing Socio-technical Design Principles with Guidelines for Human-centered AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10331)
*Thomas Herrmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了人本AI（HCAI）指导原则与社会技术系统原则，提出持续演进是社会技术系统的基本特征，人类监督与干预导致AI系统的持续适应与再设计，透明度不仅是技术特征，还需要整个系统（包括人类行为者）的参与，通过社会技术设计补偿AI的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较HCAI指南与传统信息技术背景下的社会技术系统原则，探索AI使用情境下社会技术启发式的修订方向。

Method: 对比分析方法：将HCAI指导原则与社会技术系统原则进行系统比较，识别差异与共性，进而修订社会技术启发式。

Result: 1）持续演进是社会技术系统的基本特征；2）人类监督/干预及随后的AI系统占用导致系统的持续适应与再设计（当自主性得到协作行使时）；3）透明度不仅是技术特征，还需要包含人类行为者的整个系统贡献。

Conclusion: 使用AI时，不仅技术特征，组织和社会实践也应通过社会技术设计来补偿AI的不足，这是一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Human-centered AI (HCAI) refers to guidelines or principles that aim on ethi-cally oriented design of systems. We compare HCAI- guidelines with princi-ples of socio-technical systems that emerged in the context of conventional in-formation technology. The comparison leads to a revision of socio-technical heuristics by including aspects of AI-usage. The comparison reveals that con-tinuous evolution is a basic characteristic of socio-technical systems, and that human oversight or interventions and the subsequent appropriation of AI-systems lead to continuous adaptation and re-design of the systems, if autono-my is collaboratively exercised. From a socio-technical point of view, the cru-cial requirement of transparency has not only to be fulfilled with technical fea-tures, but also by contributions of the whole system including human actors. It will be promising for using AI, if not only technical features, but organization-al and social practices are socio-technically designed in a way that compen-sates shortcomings of AI.

</details>


### [371] [ABot-AgentOS: A General Robotic Agent OS with Lifelong Multi-modal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10350)
*Jiayi Tian,Shiao Liu,Yuting Xu,Jia Lu,Zihao Guan,Honglin Han,Di Yang,Minqi Gu,Yifei Qian,Tianlin Zhang,Yanqing Zhu,Zeqian Ye,Menglin Yang,Fei Wang,Xu Hu,Xiuxian Li,Wei Zhang,Shihui Su,Yiyan Ji,Jingbo Wang,Ziteng Feng,Jiaheng Liu,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Xiaolong Wu,Mingyang Yin,Zedong Chu,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用机器人智能体操作系统ABot-AgentOS，它位于底层控制器之上，提供场景条件规划、上下文隔离技能执行、多阶段验证、多模态记忆和边缘-云端协作等推理层能力。同时引入可执行基准EmbodiedWorldBench（16个场景、4个难度等级、200+任务）和通用多模态图记忆机制。实验表明，该系统在多种记忆基准上取得领先性能，且通过故障驱动自进化循环能持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM和VLA系统虽提升了机器人感知和动作预测能力，但长时间跨度的具身智能体仍然缺乏一个通用的运行时层来处理推理、记忆、工具使用、验证和跨实体执行等需求。

Method: 1) ABot-AgentOS：提供场景条件规划、上下文隔离技能执行、多阶段验证、多模态记忆和边缘-云端协作的通用智能体操作系统层。2) Universal Multi-modal Graph Memory：将对话、视觉观测、空间上下文、时序关系和任务轨迹转化为类型化节点和边的持久化图记忆。3) 故障驱动自进化循环：将诊断出的记忆故障转化为门控运行时进化资产，仅在后续评估分割中提升。4) EmbodiedWorldBench：包含16个室内/室外/混合场景、4个难度等级、200+任务的具身可执行基准。

Result: 在EmbodiedWorldBench子集上，ABot-AgentOS在任务成功率和目标完成度上均优于单控制器基线。在记忆基准上，ABot-AgentOS Static在LoCoMo上取得87.5，OpenEQA EM-EQA上59.9，Mem-Gallery上88.6，NExT-QA上76.5 Acc@All；自进化后进一步提升至LoCoMo 88.7、OpenEQA 60.4、Mem-Gallery 89.0。

Conclusion: 通用的Agent OS层能够改善长时间跨度具身执行，同时提供持久、可审计的记忆以支持持续交互。

Abstract: Recent VLM and VLA systems have improved robotic perception and action prediction, yet long-horizon embodied agents still require a general runtime layer for reasoning, memory, tool use, verification, and cross-embodiment execution. We present ABot-AgentOS, a general robotic Agent Operating System that sits above low-level controllers and provides a deliberative agent layer for scene-conditioned planning, context-isolated skill execution, multi-stage verification, multi-modal memory, and edge-cloud collaboration. To evaluate such systems, we introduce EmbodiedWorldBench, an executable benchmark with 16 indoor, outdoor, and hybrid scenes, four difficulty levels, and over 200 tasks involving navigation, object search, NPC dialogue, dynamic events, and trace-grounded scoring. ABot-AgentOS further introduces Universal Multi-modal Graph Memory, a persistent source-grounded substrate that converts dialogue, visual observations, spatial context, temporal relations, and task traces into typed nodes and edges. A failure-driven self-evolution loop converts diagnosed memory failures into gated runtime evo-assets that are promoted only to later evaluation splits, preventing current-split ground-truth leakage while enabling continual improvement. On an initial EmbodiedWorldBench subset, ABot-AgentOS improves over a single-controller baseline in both task success and goal completion. Across memory benchmarks, ABot-AgentOS Static achieves 87.5 on LoCoMo, 59.9 on OpenEQA EM-EQA, 88.6 on Mem-Gallery, and 76.5 Acc@All on NExT-QA; self-evolution further improves LoCoMo to 88.7, OpenEQA to 60.4, and Mem-Gallery to 89.0. These results suggest that a general Agent OS layer can improve long-horizon embodied execution while providing persistent, auditable memory for continual interaction.

</details>


### [372] [Co4ICF: Co-evolving Physics-Informed Surrogate and RL-based Pulse Optimizer for Inertial Confinement Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10366)
*Jiatong Zhao,Tengyue Zhang,Yuhan Wang,Fuyuan Wu,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Co4ICF提出了一种共同进化框架，通过将物理信息代理与PPO脉冲优化器耦合，并迭代微调代理以修正外推误差，在ICF实验中实现了显著性能提升，且在预算匹配的消融实验中验证了共同进化机制的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线训练的代理模型存在被迭代优化器驱动到分布外区域导致预测不可靠的已知失效模式。

Method: 提出了Co4ICF共同进化框架，耦合物理信息代理与PPO脉冲优化器；代理在策略诱导轨迹上迭代微调以修正外推误差，优化器将不断进化的代理作为快速环境进行查询。

Result: 在1D MULTI环境中实现146.1%的归一化产量；在2D-MULTI中直接评估达到246.9%归一化产量，且预算匹配消融实验验证了共同进化机制的关键作用。

Conclusion: 共同进化框架能有效克服离线代理的OOD失效模式，且性能提升不能仅由额外仿真数据解释，共同进化机制起关键作用。

Abstract: Offline-trained surrogates for Inertial Confinement Fusion (ICF) suffer a well-known failure mode that iterative optimizers drive inputs into out-of-distribution (OOD) regions where predictions become unreliable. Here we present Co4ICF, a co-evolving framework that couples a physics-informed surrogate with a PPO-based pulse optimizer. The surrogate is iteratively fine-tuned on policy-induced trajectories, correcting extrapolation errors as the optimizer shifts the input distribution; the optimizer queries this evolving surrogate as a fast environment. In the 1D MULTI environment, Co4ICF achieves 146.1% normalized yield based on current laser design baseline; as a post-hoc cross-fidelity check, the optimized pulse further attains 246.9% normalized yield when directly evaluated in 2D-MULTI without any 2D training or fine-tuning. Budget-matched ablations support that the gains are not explained solely by additional simulation data and are consistent with the co-evolving mechanism playing a key role. We release a large-scale MULTI-IFE simulation dataset to support future benchmarking.

</details>


### [373] [ANCHOR: Automated Alignment Auditing for CLI Agents on Real-World Harm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10455)
*Kefan Song,Yanjun Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ANCHOR是一个自动化审计框架，通过微调基于暗人格数据的审计者代理来对CLI代理进行压力测试，发现即使前沿CLI代理在直接询问时拒绝非法任务，在持久的恶意交互下合规率达到100%，且经常超出用户请求自主构建大规模危害基础设施，表明当前对齐技术不足以应对自适应恶意用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自主CLI代理在获得更大自主性时是否面临更大风险，并验证当前安全对齐技术能否抵御持久的自适应恶意攻击。

Method: 提出了ANCHOR自动化审计框架，该框架使用经监督学习和强化学习微调（基于暗人格数据）的审计者代理，扮演持久性恶意用户角色，通过分解任务、在拒绝时重新措辞请求、跨多轮交互调整策略等方式，对前沿CLI代理进行非法任务压力测试。

Result: 前沿CLI代理在直接提示时经常拒绝非法任务，但在持久恶意交互下合规率可达到100%；且合规时经常超出用户请求，自主构建大规模金融欺诈和生物武器开发等灾难性风险场景的基础设施。

Conclusion: 当前的对齐技术不足以保护自主代理免受持久的自适应恶意用户攻击，亟需针对此类威胁的安全评估方法。

Abstract: Autonomous CLI agents can now execute hundreds of actions across multi-hour sessions: writing code, executing shell commands, browsing the web, and managing cloud infrastructure, all with minimal human oversight. Does greater autonomy invite greater risk? We introduce ANCHOR, an automated auditing framework that stress-tests CLI agents on illegal tasks grounded in public US court cases. ANCHOR deploys an auditor agent fine-tuned on dark personality data using supervised and reinforcement fine tuning. This auditor roleplays persistent malicious users who decompose tasks, reframe requests upon refusal, and adapt strategies across multi-turn interactions. Evaluating frontier CLI agents, we find that while they often refuse illegal tasks when prompted directly, compliance reaches 100\% under persistent malicious interaction. When agents comply, they frequently exceed user requests, autonomously building infrastructure for large-scale harm, including catastrophic risk scenarios such as large-scale financial fraud and bioweapon development. These findings demonstrate that current alignment techniques are insufficient for autonomous agents and underscore the need for safety evaluations against persistent, adaptive malicious users. We release ANCHOR at https://github.com/garified/anchor

</details>


### [374] [GRASP: GRanularity-Aware Search Policy for Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10463)
*Varun Gandhi,Jaewook Lee,Shantanu Todmal,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan Rossi,Zichao Wang,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRASP是一个强化学习框架，用于训练智能体在多步推理中自适应协调互补的检索工具（语义搜索、关键词搜索和段落阅读），通过联合奖励机制优化检索召回和问答性能，学习可解释的略读和扫描行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能检索增强生成（Agentic RAG）中，模型难以决定何时检索、使用词法匹配还是语义相似度，以及如何控制上下文粒度以防止无关token干扰智能体推理。

Method: 提出GRASP框架，使用强化学习训练策略，为智能体提供语义搜索、关键词搜索和段落阅读三种动作；采用联合奖励机制，同时考虑答案准确性、有依据阅读、互补搜索和轮次效率。

Result: 在多跳推理基准上，GRASP相比单步检索、基于提示的智能RAG和基于强化学习的检索基线，在检索召回率和下游问答性能上均有提升。学到的策略形成了可解释的略读（语义搜索广泛探索）和扫描（关键词搜索定位实体证据、段落阅读局部验证）行为。

Conclusion: 学习协调检索信号和上下文粒度对于智能体的正确推理至关重要。

Abstract: Agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) extends static RAG by allowing language models to iteratively reason, generate search queries, retrieve evidence, and predict answers. However, it remains challenging for models to decide when to retrieve, whether to use lexical matching or semantic similarity, and how to control context granularity to prevent irrelevant tokens from interfering with agent reasoning. In this paper, we introduce GRASP, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework for training agents to adaptively coordinate complementary retrieval tools during multi-step reasoning. GRASP provides the agent with semantic search, keyword search, and paragraph-reading actions, enabling it to retrieve sentence-level evidence and expand further context only when needed. We train the policy with a reward that jointly accounts for answer accuracy, grounded reading, complementary search, and turn efficiency. Experiments on multi-hop reasoning benchmarks show that GRASP improves both retrieval recall and downstream question answering performance compared with single-step retrieval, prompting-based agentic RAG, and RL-based retrieval baselines. Qualitative and ablation analyses show that the learned policy develops interpretable skimming and scanning behavior: it uses semantic search for broad exploration, paragraph reading for local verification, and keyword search for entity-specific evidence. These results suggest that learning to coordinate retrieval signals and context granularity is critical for agent's correct reasoning.

</details>


### [375] [Agents Don't Just Agree, They Remember: Benchmarking Persistent Sycophancy in Stateful Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10526)
*Xutao Mao,Liangjie Zhao,Leyao Wang,Rui Qian,Qiang Huang,Wentao Wang,Bo Han,Xiang Zheng,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"持久性谄媚"概念，即对话中用户声称被智能体接受并写入持久状态（长期记忆、技能等），在后续无关查询中被重用。作者构建了PASB基准（1600任务），发现写入持久状态后下游失败率从45.0%升至71.9%（增加27个百分点），揭示了三种写入模式（地位提升、归因移除、范围扩大），指出智能体谄媚本质是状态写入治理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话谄媚研究仅关注单次会话中的迎合行为，忽视了有状态智能体将用户声称持久化存储并在未来复用的风险。当智能体将用户错误或偏颇的声称写入长期记忆、配置文件或工作流后，这些内容会脱离原始对话语境被后续查询调用，导致系统性偏差。

Method: 构建PASB基准：1）4种场景框架（事实记忆、程序技能等）× 4种时间交付模式（一次性、重复等）；2）5轮持久化阶段（写入状态）→ 清除后3轮查询阶段（仅依赖持久状态）；3）评估真实智能体（Hermes-Agent、OpenClaw）而非提供预写记忆；4）在12个模型上系统测量提交边界前后的失败率差异及写入模式。

Result: 提交边界是关键拐点：会话内失败率45.0%，提交后升至71.9%（一致增加27个百分点）。发现三种写入时模式：地位提升（声称被提升为事实/偏好）、归因移除（来源信息丢失）、范围扩大（应用场景泛化）。这些模式在记忆类/程序类框架、重复强化、跨领域边界时更强。

Conclusion: 智能体谄媚本质是状态写入治理问题：安全措施必须控制智能体写入什么（write-time controls），而不仅控制说什么（response-level mitigations）。需要门控高风险提交、保留来源信息、约束存储内容的角色和范围。

Abstract: Stateful personal agents increasingly maintain long-term user profiles, episodic memories, and reusable skills. This persistence turns conversational sycophancy into a state-writing failure: accepted user-centric claims can be committed as lasting preferences, background facts, or workflows and later reused after the original conversation is gone. We call this persistent sycophancy and introduce the Personal Agent Sycophancy Benchmark (PASB), a 1,600-task benchmark that traces whether a conversational claim is accepted, written into durable agent state, and reused in a later neutral query. Unlike prior benchmarks that provide pre-written memories, PASB evaluates real agents (Hermes-Agent and OpenClaw) that decide what to store. It isolates the write process by combining four scenario framings with four temporal delivery patterns and separating a five-turn persist stage from a cleared three-turn query stage, ensuring downstream effects arise only from durable state. Across twelve models, the commit boundary is the key inflection point: downstream failure increases from 45.0% in session-only episodes to 71.9% after commitment, a consistent increase of 27.0 percentage points. Committed claims exhibit three write-time patterns: status promotion, attribution removal, and scope broadening. These patterns become stronger under memory-like or procedural framing, repeated reinforcement, and even across domain boundaries. These results show that agent sycophancy is fundamentally a state-writing governance problem. Once user content is committed to durable memory, safety must govern what agents write, not only what they say. PASB identifies the write-time controls needed to gate risky commits while preserving the source, role, and scope of stored content beyond response-level mitigations.

</details>


### [376] [Cross-Layer Misalignment Detection in Agent Skills: A Progressive Loading-Aware Contrastive Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10534)
*Chengjun Zhang,Yang Gao,Jianna Hur,Jingjing Zhang,Sagar Samtani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出渐进式加载感知分层对比学习（PL-HCL）框架，用于检测LLM Agent技能中描述与真实行为之间的跨层不一致，在超26.4万开源技能语料上将Macro-F1从约0.45提升至0.87-0.89。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着开源技能市场扩展，用户和Agent依赖简短元数据选择第三方技能，但技能描述与真实行为之间可能存在不一致（跨层错位），缺乏有效的检测手段。

Method: 提出PL-HCL框架，基于LLM对Agent技能的分层结构进行建模，通过渐进式加载感知的分层对比学习来学习跨层一致性。

Result: 在超过26.4万开源技能标准化语料和人工验证挑战集上，PL-HCL将Macro-F1从未适应基线的约0.45提升至0.87-0.89（跨多个LLM骨干）。

Conclusion: 该方法为检测分层数字制品中的不一致性提供了有效的筛选工具和设计原则，适用于用户和平台运营者。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly extended through Agent Skills, reusable artifacts that package natural-language metadata, procedural instructions, and execution-time resources for runtime use. As open-source skill marketplaces expand, users and agents increasingly rely on brief metadata to select third-party skills, making it difficult to detect inconsistencies between a skill's description and its true behavior, a problem we call cross-layer misalignment. To address this issue, we propose Progressive Loading-Aware Hierarchical Contrastive Learning (PL-HCL), an LLM-based framework that detects misalignment by modeling the layered structure of Agent Skills and learning cross-layer consistency. Using a normalized corpus of over 264,000 open-source skills and a human-verified challenge set, PL-HCL improves Macro-F1 from approximately 0.45 for unadapted baselines to 0.87-0.89 across evaluated LLM backbones. This approach offers an effective screening tool for users and operators, as well as design principles for detecting inconsistencies in layered digital artifacts.

</details>


### [377] [AI YOU Town: Make Friends and Money with Your Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10539)
*Yan Lin,Yuyang Dai,Jiahui Geng,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI YOU 是一个持续从对话中更新22维人格画像并体现于数字孪生的框架，结合提示、贝叶斯更新和保形预测实现人格推断，通过三层认知记忆和周期性刷新锚点保持长对话一致性，在对抗性多智能体设置中达到0.921-0.976的保形覆盖，优于静态提示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户特质推断与人格一致性响应生成方法依赖静态提示，缺乏校准的不确定性度量、忽略序列证据，且在长交互中发生漂移。

Method: 结合提示（prompting）、贝叶斯更新（Bayesian updating）和保形预测（conformal prediction）进行人格推断；采用周期性刷新的记忆锚点（memory anchor）与三层认知记忆（cognitive memory）保持长对话中的人格一致性。

Result: 保形覆盖率达0.921-0.976；不确定性校准和基于记忆的推理得到改善；在100轮对抗性多智能体角色扮演中，多数骨干模型的人格保真度优于静态提示，特质漂移减少。

Conclusion: AI YOU 框架有效解决了长对话中人格推断的校准问题与漂移问题，原型AI YOU Town构建了想象孪生世界，演示已上线。

Abstract: Existing approaches to infer user traits and generate responses consistent with a persona rely on static prompting. They lack calibrated uncertainty, ignore sequential evidence, and drift during long interactions. We present \textbf{AI YOU}, a framework that continually updates a personality profile with 22 dimensions from conversation and embodies it in a personal digital twin. Practically, the system combines prompting, Bayesian updating, and conformal prediction for persona inference. A periodically refreshed memory anchor and cognitive memory with three layers preserve persona consistency over long interactions. Across the main results, AI YOU \emph{(i)} achieves conformal coverage ranging from 0.921 to 0.976, \emph{(ii)} improves uncertainty calibration and reasoning grounded in memory, and \emph{(iii)} enhances persona fidelity over static prompting in role playing over 100 turns while reducing trait drift, for most evaluated backbones under adversarial settings with multiple agents. The prototype \emph{AI YOU Town} initializes an imaginative twin world for future interaction. The online demo is available at \href{https://quinnnnnne-ai-you.hf.space/}{\mbox{\texttt{quinnnnnne-ai-you.hf.space}}}.

</details>


### [378] [Large language model agents accelerate inverse design of metal-organic frameworks for gas separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10559)
*Zhaolin Hu,Hehe Fan,Wangyihan Guo,Meng Xu,Chenhao Rao,Qiwei Yang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LEMO Agent，一种基于大语言模型代理的框架，用于在MOFid空间中对气体分离MOF进行闭环逆向设计，通过迭代生成-验证-评估-记忆循环实现约束条件下的化学有效候选物发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOF具有高度模块化的气体分离平台，但其巨大的网格化设计空间使得在化学有效性、分离性能和结构多样性多重约束下进行逆向设计非常困难。

Method: LEMO Agent框架结合了基于语言的候选生成、MOFid标准化、显式有效性检查、基于Transformer的性质预测、结构化设计记忆和多岛探索，通过迭代的生成-验证-评估-记忆循环，利用成功和失败候选物的反馈来指导跨连接子、金属和拓扑选择的化学约束搜索。

Result: 在CH4/N2和CO2/N2分离任务上，相比生成式、优化式和代理式基线方法，LEMO Agent富集了高性能候选物，提升了预测分离性能，并保持了广泛的化学和拓扑多样性。选定候选物经GCMC模拟验证和实验下选流程，完成了初步湿法合成和SEM表征。

Conclusion: 大语言模型代理可以作为可解释且可扩展的设计引擎，加速超越传统固定库筛选的MOF发现。

Abstract: Metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) offer a highly modular platform for adsorptive gas separation, yet their vast reticular design space makes inverse design difficult under simultaneous constraints of chemical validity, separation performance, and structural diversity. Here, we present LEMO Agent, a large-language-model agent framework for closed-loop inverse design of gas-separation MOFs in MOFid space. LEMO Agent couples language-based candidate generation with MOFid standardization, explicit validity checking, Transformer-based property prediction, structured design memory, and multi-island exploration. Through iterative generate--validate--evaluate--remember cycles, the agent uses feedback from both successful and failed candidates to guide chemically constrained search across linker, metal, and topology choices. We evaluate LEMO Agent on CH$_4$/N$_2$ and CO$_2$/N$_2$ separation tasks. Compared with representative generative, optimization, and agentic baselines, LEMO Agent enriches high-performing candidates, improves predicted separation performance, and maintains broad chemical and topological diversity. Selected candidates are further reconstructed, evaluated by GCMC simulations, and passed through an experimental down-selection workflow based on chemical feasibility and ligand purchasability, leading to initial wet-lab synthesis and SEM characterization. These results demonstrate that large language model agents can serve as interpretable and scalable design engines for accelerating MOF discovery beyond conventional fixed-library screening.

</details>


### [379] [CRiT-QA: Evaluating Multi-hop Reasoning with Counterfactual Chains and Distractor Traps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10562)
*JungMin Yun,JuneHyoung Kwon,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CRiT-QA数据集，通过反事实实体转换和多锚点干扰链来评估大语言模型真正的多跳推理能力，实验表明LLMs在该数据集上表现显著下降，暴露了其对参数知识和数据集捷径的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在多跳问答数据集上表现良好，但这种性能常掩盖两个关键缺陷：(1)依赖内部参数知识而非上下文；(2)利用数据集捷径（如单文档线索或类型匹配）规避真正的跨文档证据聚合。需要更严谨的评估工具。

Method: 构建CRiT-QA数据集：(1)通过反事实实体替换事实推理链，强制模型依赖上下文而非记忆知识；(2)注入多锚点干扰链——在不同跳数上分叉的看似合理但错误的推理路径，迫使模型遵循完整推理过程而非利用浅层启发式。

Result: LLMs在CRiT-QA上相比标准数据集表现出显著的性能下降，暴露了其在反事实条件和干扰陷阱下的脆弱性。

Conclusion: CRiT-QA可作为评估真正多跳推理的严格诊断工具，为开发更可靠、基于证据的大语言模型奠定基础。

Abstract: Evaluating the multi-hop reasoning capabilities of large language models remains a significant challenge. Although current models achieve strong results on existing multi-hop question answering datasets, such performance often masks two critical vulnerabilities: (1) reliance on internal parametric knowledge rather than adherence to the provided context, and (2) exploitation of dataset shortcuts, such as single-document cues or type-matching, that diminish the need for genuine evidence aggregation across multiple documents. We introduce CRiT-QA (Counterfactual Reasoning with Traps), a dataset explicitly designed to address both limitations. To neutralize reliance on memorized knowledge and enforce strict context dependency, CRiT-QA transforms factual reasoning chains with counterfactual entities. Furthermore, it injects multi-anchor distractor chains, plausible but incorrect reasoning paths that diverge at different hops. These traps require models to follow the entire reasoning process rather than exploiting shallow heuristics. Our experiments show that LLMs exhibit substantial performance degradation on CRiT-QA compared to standard datasets, exposing their vulnerability to counterfactual conditions and distractor traps. CRiT-QA thus serves as a rigorous diagnostic tool for evaluating genuine multi-hop reasoning and provides a foundation for developing more reliable, evidence-grounded LLMs.

</details>


### [380] [Laguerre Geometry for Interpreting Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10578)
*Chunwei Ma,Russell Wolfinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用拉盖尔几何（Laguerre Geometry）精确刻画LLM中的概念结构——概念被定义为拉盖尔-沃罗诺伊细胞或其并集，并进一步揭示概念间的包含与层级关系。通过将Transformer层分解为分段线性算子，作者发现token隐藏轨迹由静态分段线性流和动态跨token注意力跳跃共同驱动。基于此，他们开发了无需训练、无需超参数的Geometric Lens方法，以及2D可视化工具Laguerre Autoencoder，并在上下文干扰场景下验证了其恢复正确事实token的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假设将LLM中的概念表示为单点、线性方向或高斯簇，但尚不清楚这些结构如何以及为何涌现。本文旨在精确刻画概念几何结构，并解释其形成机制。

Method: 1）将概念定义为拉盖尔几何中的拉盖尔-沃罗诺伊细胞或细胞并集；2）利用拉盖尔权重揭示精细概念结构（包含与层级）；3）将Transformer层分解为分段线性算子，分析token隐藏轨迹的两种耦合机制（静态分段线性流与动态跨token注意力跳跃）；4）开发Geometric Lens（无需训练和超参数）读取任意层的概念编码；5）开发Laguerre Autoencoder进行2D可视化。

Result: Geometric Lens能够准确读取模型各层隐藏向量所编码的概念；Laguerre Autoencoder可在一个视图中同时呈现决策几何与完整推理轨迹；在上下文干扰提示下，该方法能正确恢复事实token。

Conclusion: 拉盖尔几何为LLM中的概念表征提供了精确的数学框架，并推动了从解释性几何到可操作可解释性的转变。

Abstract: Existing hypotheses represent a concept in an LLM as a single point, a linear direction, or a Gaussian cluster, yet it remains unclear how and why such structures emerge. Here, we show that concept geometry can be precisely characterized via Laguerre Geometry, in which a concept is defined as a region--a Laguerre-Voronoi cell or a union of cells--allowing us to strictly define, measure, and separate concepts. Building on this formulation, we show that finer-grained concept structures, such as inclusion and hierarchy, are naturally revealed by the Laguerre weights. We then push this geometry inside the transformer. Decomposing each layer into piecewise-linear operators, we show that a token's hidden trajectory is governed by two coupled mechanisms: a static tree of self-contained piecewise-linear flow, and a dynamic transport that hops the trajectory across trees when cross-token attention fires. This decomposition yields Geometric Lens, a training-free, hyperparameter-free method for reading out the exact concept a hidden vector encodes at any layer. We also develop Laguerre Autoencoder, a 2D visualizer that renders both the decision geometry and a model's full reasoning trajectory in one view. Finally, we move beyond explanatory geometry toward actionable interpretability, showing that Geometric Lens recovers the correct factual token when a model is prompted with in-context interference. The code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [381] [Constraint-Aware Hierarchical Search for Regulation-Driven Fine-Grained Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10588)
*Siyu Wang,Wei Tan,Lulu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“法规驱动的细粒度层次分类”任务，并构建了四个基准数据集。针对现有方法无法同时保证层次有效性、规则一致性和细粒度边界推理的问题，提出了约束感知的层次搜索框架，将法规文档转换为可搜索树，检索有效的局部候选节点，并利用结构化法规字段和证据片段指导分类。实验表明该方法在四个数据集上平均准确率最优，尤其在细粒度邻接类别和基于规则的边界条件下提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海关关税分类、出口管制分类等场景需要将输入实例分配到显式法规层次下的细粒度类别，标签不仅取决于语义相似度，还受规则定义的边界、阈值条件、排除条款、定义和本地例外约束。现有平面分类器、层次文本分类方法和检索增强LLM系统无法同时满足层次有效性、规则一致性和细粒度边界推理，亟需一种专门解决这些挑战的方法。

Method: 提出约束感知的层次搜索框架：1) 将法规文档转换为可搜索树结构；2) 检索仅包含有效局部候选节点的子集；3) 使用结构化法规字段和证据片段指导每一步的下一个节点决策，确保层次路径有效性和规则一致性。

Result: 在四个法规密集型基准数据集上，该方法平均准确率最优，提供了可解释的决策路径，在涉及细粒度邻接类别和基于规则边界条件的案例上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 法规驱动的细粒度层次分类是一个重要且未被充分研究的任务，所提方法通过约束感知的层次搜索有效解决了层次有效性、规则一致性和细粒度边界推理的联合挑战，为法规合规分类提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Tasks such as customs tariff classification, export control categorization, and standards-based equipment coding require assigning an input instance to a fine-grained class under an explicit regulatory hierarchy. Unlike standard text classification, the correct label in these tasks is not determined by semantic similarity alone, but by rule-defined boundaries, threshold conditions, exclusion clauses, definitions, and local exceptions. As a result, two highly similar inputs may require different labels, while a retrieved passage that appears relevant may still be inapplicable under the governing rules. Existing flat classifiers, hierarchical text classification methods, and retrieval-augmented LLM systems are not designed to jointly enforce hierarchical validity, rule consistency, and fine-grained boundary reasoning. In this paper, we formulate this setting as regulation-driven fine-grained hierarchical classification, where an external instance must be assigned to a fine-grained class through a valid path in a regulatory hierarchy and supported by auditable evidence. We construct four benchmark datasets from representative regulation-intensive scenarios and validate the annotations through an expert-in-the-loop process. We further propose a constraint-aware hierarchical search framework that converts regulatory documents into a searchable tree, retrieves only valid local candidate nodes, and uses structured regulatory fields with evidence snippets to guide each next-hop decision. Experiments show that our method achieves the best mean accuracy on all four datasets and provides interpretable decision paths, with the largest gains on cases involving fine-grained neighboring categories and rule-based boundary conditions.

</details>


### [382] [MRUF: Multi-granularity Routing with Uncertainty-Aware Fusion for Robust Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10599)
*Haoran Ma,Yinfeng Yu,Liejun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MRUF提出了一种基于多粒度路由和不确定性感知校准的可靠性感知融合方法，用于多模态情感分析，在CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI数据集上取得了一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感分析中，由于遮挡、背景噪声、运动模糊或不完美转录等问题，话语级别的模态质量可能参差不齐，导致传统融合方法过度信任不可靠的模态。

Method: MRUF包含多粒度路由（子空间级和模态级路由）和不确定性感知校准两个核心模块。前者通过留一法误差增量来估计模态重要性并监督路由；后者预测模态不确定性，通过逆方差重新加权细化模态门控，并辅以模态不变对比对齐稳定共享表示空间。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI数据集的对齐与非对齐设置下，MRUF均优于强基线方法；机制分析验证了预测不确定性高的模态获得了更低的融合权重。

Conclusion: MRUF通过显式建模模态可靠性和不确定性，有效缓解了低质量模态对融合的负面影响，提升了多模态情感分析的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis relies on language, visual, and acoustic cues, but utterance-level modality quality may vary due to occlusion, background noise, motion blur, or imperfect transcripts, causing conventional fusion to over-trust unreliable modalities. We propose MRUF, a reliability-aware fusion method that combines multi-granularity routing with uncertainty-aware calibration. MRUF summarizes sentiment-relevant representations, performs subspace- and modality-level routing, and supervises modality routing with leave-one-out error increases to estimate utterance-level modality importance. It further predicts modality-wise uncertainty and refines modality gates through inverse-variance reweighting, while modality-invariant contrastive alignment stabilizes the shared representation space. Experiments on CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI under aligned and unaligned settings show consistent improvements over strong baselines, and mechanism analysis verifies that modalities with higher predicted uncertainty receive lower fusion weights.

</details>


### [383] [Agentic-DPO: From Imitation to Agentic Policy Optimization on Expert Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10601)
*Yixiong Chen,Alan Yuille*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agentic-DPO方法，通过将专家轨迹转化为状态级偏好监督，无需在线环境交互即可优化agent策略，在多个benchmark上显著超越SFT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SFT训练LLM agent仅模仿专家动作序列，而非学习在每步状态中从错误选项中选择正确动作；现有的偏好学习或RL方法需要昂贵的环境交互和奖励模型。

Method: 1) Agentic-DPO：在每个专家动作状态，从当前策略采样一步动作，将错误动作作为负例，与专家动作进行DPO式偏好对比；2) Policy-Preserving Augmentation (PPA)：在保持专家策略不变的前提下，将相同潜在轨迹渲染为多种schema，避免偏好学习中策略与schema混杂。

Result: 在StableToolBench、tau-bench retail和Mind2Web上一致提升不同规模的agent性能；tau-bench上将9B模型准确率从21.7%(SFT)提升至41.4%，匹配在线GRPO但无需梯度步骤中的环境交互。

Conclusion: 专家轨迹可以转化为状态级动作偏好，以低代价实现agent策略优化，无需在线环境rollout、奖励模型或完整轨迹学生探索。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are commonly trained from expert trajectories using supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which treats multi-turn agent behavior as ordinary text imitation. This recipe is simple and low-cost, but it only learns to imitate the sequence of expert actions, rather than training the agent to choose the right action against plausible mistakes at each state. Existing methods to mitigate this problem include preference learning or reinforcement learning, but they usually need high-cost environment rollouts and reward models. We propose Agentic-DPO, a lightweight offline agent policy optimization method that turns expert trajectories into state-conditioned preference supervision. At each expert action state, Agentic-DPO samples a one-step action from the current state, treats plausible wrong actions as negatives, and contrasts them with the expert action using a DPO-style preference objective. To avoid mixing both policy and schema in preference learning, we introduce Policy-Preserving Augmentation (PPA), which renders the same latent trajectory under multiple schemas while keeping the expert policy fixed. Agentic-DPO requires no online environment rollout, reward model, or full-trajectory student exploration. We conduct experiments across StableToolBench, tau-bench retail, and Mind2Web, where Agentic-DPO consistently improves agents at different model scales beyond imitation. In particular, it raises tau-bench accuracy from 21.7% (SFT) to 41.4% for a 9B model, matching online GRPO under the same backbone with only step-level rollouts and without environment interaction during gradient steps. The results suggest that expert trajectories can support low-cost agentic policy optimization when converted from demonstrations into state-level action preferences. Code for Agentic-DPO is released at https://github.com/Schuture/Agentic-DPO.

</details>


### [384] [The Compliance Trap: Diagnosing How AI Agents Consume Conflicting Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10608)
*Yixiong Chen,Xinyi Bai,Alan Yuille*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出E-P-R框架，用于诊断AI智能体在长时任务中如何消费（使用）检索到的记忆。研究发现，记忆消费的主要失败始于“进入”（Entry）阶段——即使记忆与任务冲突，智能体也会采纳；重复暴露会放大早期错误，而偏离后恢复能力很弱。这形成了“服从陷阱”：一旦服从错误记忆，成功率大幅下降。更强模型因更多基础能力被覆盖，绝对损害更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多将记忆视为供给问题（写什么、存什么、检索什么），但缺乏对模型如何在多步动作轨迹中消费（使用）检索到的记忆的清晰理解。这一消费过程决定了需要检索什么记忆、以及需要什么样的模型和控制策略来安全地使用它们。

Method: 提出E-P-R（Entry-Propagation-Recovery）轨迹级框架，分别考察：记忆首次改变动作的位置（Entry）、该改变是否向前延续（Propagation）、离开正确路径后能否恢复（Recovery）。在WebArena和自建基准MemTrapBench上实例化该框架进行实验分析。

Result: 主要失败始于Entry阶段：智能体在第一个暴露决策点就采纳了与任务冲突的记忆。重复暴露放大早期错误，偏离后恢复能力弱。这导致“服从陷阱”：不同模型对冲突记忆的服从率相近，但一旦服从，成功率骤降至低水平。更强模型因每个服从事件抹去的基线能力更多，承受更大的绝对损害。

Conclusion: 记忆增强型智能体的评估不仅应关注检索质量或最终成功率，还应评估其在整个轨迹中如何消费记忆。

Abstract: Memory is becoming a core component of long-horizon AI agents, allowing agents to reuse past experience when operating web browsers, software tools, and other interactive environments. Existing work mostly treats memory as a supply problem, asking what experience to write, how to store it, and which entry to retrieve for the next task. Yet we still lack a clear account of how models consume retrieved memory across a multi-step action trajectory. This consumption process matters because it determines not only what memories should be retrieved, but also what models and control policies are needed to use them safely. To diagnose this process, we propose Entry--Propagation--Recovery (E-P-R), a trajectory-level framework that asks where memory first changes an action, whether that change carries forward, and whether the agent can recover after leaving a correct path. We instantiate E-P-R on WebArena and on MemTrapBench, a controlled benchmark we build to isolate these phases. We find that the main failure often begins at entry: agents adopt conflicting memory at the first exposed decision point even when it is task-wrong. Repeated exposure then amplifies this early error, while recovery after divergence is weak. Together, these effects create a compliance trap: across models, conflicting memory induces similar compliance rates, but once agents comply, their success rates collapse to a low floor. Stronger agents therefore suffer larger absolute damage because each compliance event erases more baseline capability. These results suggest that memory-augmented agents should be evaluated not only by retrieval quality or final success rate, but by how they consume memory throughout the trajectory.

</details>


### [385] [Embark Now: User Demand Oriented Framework for Multi-day Urban Travel Itinerary Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10651)
*Rongbo Qi,Yaqi Zhang,Shijun Yan,Xuemeng Liu,Xiangrui Cai,Chunyao Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成大语言模型（LLM）和增强型GRASP算法的框架，用于解决大城市多日旅行行程规划问题。通过在北京和天津两个真实城市数据集上的实验，该方法在行程质量（总分提升4.52%-11.09%）和计算效率（性能提升4.64%-26.07%）上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大城市中规划多日旅行行程面临POI数量庞大、用户偏好多样、开放时间等约束，现有方法难以在有限计算时间内动态、精准地满足用户需求并优化行程满意度。

Method: 提出一个集成框架：1）利用大语言模型（LLM）动态、灵活地捕捉用户需求；2）采用增强型GRASP算法作为偏好感知规划器，生成可行的多日行程方案。

Result: 在两个真实数据集（北京、天津）上的5040个用户案例中，平均行程总分相较于SOTA方法提升至少4.52%和11.09%；端到端算法改进带来17.95%和26.07%的指标提升；时间效率方面，在更短计算时间内实现4.64%和25.55%的平均性能提升。

Conclusion: 该集成方法在行程质量和计算效率上均优于现有方法，验证了LLM与增强型GRASP结合在复杂旅行规划问题中的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: In large urban areas, planning multi-day travel itineraries is challenging due to the abundance of Points of Interest (POIs), diverse user preferences, and constraints such as opening hours. Effective solutions must dynamically accommodate diverse traveler requirements while optimizing for satisfaction and feasibility within limited computation time. This paper addresses these challenges through introducing an innovative framework that integrates Large Language Models (LLMs) to dynamically capture user requirements with precision and flexibility, and an enhanced Greedy Randomized Adaptive Search Procedure (GRASP) algorithm as a well-suited preference-aware planner to generate feasible multi-day itineraries. The effectiveness of our integrated approach is demonstrated through extensive experiments on two real-world urban datasets from Beijing and Tianjin. Our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, improving the average total itinerary score by at least 4.52% and 11.09% across 5,040 user cases with diverse preferences in the two datasets. Furthermore, through end-to-end algorithmic enhancements, it achieves notable average improvements of 17.95% and 26.07% in the computed metrics, while also delivering substantial gains in time efficiency -- realizing average performance increases of 4.64% and 25.55% within shorter computation times compared to suboptimal methods that require multiple iterations. These outcomes underscore our method's superiority in delivering both enhanced itinerary quality and computational efficiency over existing methodologies.

</details>


### [386] [Personalized Emotional Intelligence in Generative AI through Symbolic Affective Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10678)
*Qing Lin,Mengmi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EROS是一个混合AI框架，将符号推理与深度学习相结合，通过视觉内容实现个性化情绪增强。该系统能发现通用情感规则、识别情绪相关图像区域、预测上下文感知的视觉修改，并利用可扩展记忆库实现推理时个性化。人类心理物理实验表明，EROS在引发目标情绪反应方面优于现有大型多模态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型主要局限于生成逼真内容或执行语义推理，缺乏理解、预测和个性化人类情绪反应的能力。为解决这一局限，作者提出EROS系统以实现可个性化的情绪增强。

Method: EROS采用混合AI架构，整合符号推理与深度学习。利用大规模图像-情绪数据集发现可泛化的情感规则，识别情绪相关图像区域，并预测保留场景语义的同时引导情绪向目标状态的视觉修改。系统包含可扩展记忆库，支持推理时个性化而无需模型微调。

Result: 通过广泛的人类心理物理学实验，EROS在引发目标情绪反应方面优于最先进的大型多模态模型，并能适应个体情感偏好。

Conclusion: EROS为能够理解、推理和增强人类认知状态的AI系统奠定了基础，潜在应用包括心理健康、自适应媒体、教育和人机交互。

Abstract: Emotional intelligence enables humans to recognize emotions, infer their causes, reason about interventions, and modify their environment to achieve desired affective states. Despite recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI), current models remain largely limited to generating realistic content or performing semantic reasoning, with little capacity for understanding, predicting, and personalizing human emotional responses. Here we introduce Emotion-augmented geneRatiOn System (EROS), a hybrid AI framework that integrates symbolic reasoning with deep learning to enable personalized emotion augmentation through visual content. Leveraging large-scale image-emotion datasets, EROS discovers generalizable affective rules, identifies emotion-relevant image regions, and predicts context-aware visual modifications that preserve scene semantics while steering emotional responses toward desired targets. To account for individual variability, EROS incorporates an expandable memory bank that supports inference-time personalization without model fine-tuning, yielding interpretable emotional profiles and rapid adaptation to new users. Across extensive human psychophysics experiments, EROS elicits target emotional responses more effectively than state-of-the-art large multimodal models while adapting to individual affective preferences. Beyond affective computing, EROS provides a foundation for AI systems that can understand, reason about, and augment human cognitive states, with potential applications in mental health, adaptive media, education, and human-computer interaction.

</details>


### [387] [WattCouncil: Context-Aware Household Energy Scenario Generation With Governed LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10720)
*Mohannad Takrouri,Nicolas M. Cuadrado A.,Martin Takáč*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WattCouncil是一个基于大语言模型代理的框架，通过多角色协作生成考虑文化、时间、物理约束的高分辨率家庭电力需求数据，以解决智能电网研究中数据稀缺的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低碳电力系统转型和分布式能源普及对电网运行提出新要求，但机器学习研究受限于高质量家庭能源数据难以获取（隐私、监管、成本等因素）。

Method: 构建由多个LLM代理组成的"委员会"，每个代理承担特定角色（生成、审计、验证），通过引导推理过程结合家庭组成、时间因素和环境条件，生成上下文敏感的日常用电行为序列。

Result: 基于CER数据集（4232户家庭，超过一年的负荷测量及社会经济调查数据）进行定量评估，并通过消融实验验证框架一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够生成结构化的、符合物理约束的能源场景，为隐私保护下的数据生成提供了新范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: The accelerating shift toward low-carbon power systems, together with the widespread adoption of behind-the-meter technologies such as rooftop solar and electric vehicles, is placing new operational and analytical demands on electricity grids. At the same time, smart-grid research increasingly relies on machine learning (ML), yet progress is constrained by limited access to high-resolution household energy data due to privacy concerns, regulatory barriers, and collection costs. This work presents WattCouncil, a data-generation framework in which household electricity demand is generated by a council of Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents operating in specialized roles to generate, audit, and validate structured energy scenarios under explicit cultural, temporal, and physical constraints. Rather than acting as static predictors, these agents serve as adaptive decision-makers within a governed pipeline. Motivated by studies highlighting the importance of contextual factors in energy use, our framework produces context-sensitive daily routines through a guided reasoning process that incorporates household composition, temporal factors, and environmental conditions. We evaluate the generated profiles against the detailed CER dataset, which contains over a year of load measurements for 4232 households together with survey-based socio-economic information. We further assess the consistency of the framework through ablation studies. Source code is available at https://github.com/Singularity-AI-Lab/wattcouncil

</details>


### [388] [Filtering Harmful Actions Isn't Enough: Phantom Transfer in Agentic SDF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10750)
*Chinmayi Dixit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 训练大型语言模型在包含对抗性交互的合成智能体轨迹上会持续增加不良行为（例如泄露行为从4.6%升至24.9%），即使移除所有对抗性动作后效果依然存在，而结构相似但良性的轨迹影响较小（15.5%）。这种偏差通过生成过程编码在轨迹中，而非局部化于有害动作，且取决于生成模型（Gemini 2.5 Flash比Claude 3.7 Sonnet诱导更高泄露率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型越来越多地作为智能体部署，合成轨迹将成为训练智能体行为的重要数据源。然而这些数据中可能包含对抗性交互，研究这种训练对模型对齐性的影响至关重要。

Method: 在近似强化学习rollouts的合成对抗性轨迹上微调Llama 3.3 70B Instruct，使用Anthropic的Agentic Misalignment套件和Apollos的in-context scheming场景评估模型。

Result: 微调后泄露行为增加约5倍（4.6%→24.9%），移除所有对抗性动作后效果仍存在；良性轨迹微调效果较小（15.5%）；Gemini 2.5 Flash生成的良性轨迹比Claude 3.7 Sonnet诱导更高泄露率；标准安全基准无法区分这些差异。

Conclusion: 动作级过滤不足以确保合成智能体训练数据的安全性，生成模型引入的偏差可以超越语义检查而存活。

Abstract: Synthetic data is widely used to train large language models because it is inexpensive to generate and easy to control. As models are increasingly deployed as agents, synthetic trajectories are likely to become an important source of training data for agentic behavior. We investigate the effects of training on synthetic agentic trajectories containing adversarial interactions, including actions such as terminating another agents process, lowering its scheduling priority, or accessing resources without authorization.
